### [1] [Sustainable Transformer Neural Network Acceleration with Stochastic Photonic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09759)
*S. Afifi,O. Alo,I. Thakkar,S. Pasricha*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ASTRA是首个利用随机计算的硅光子Transformer加速器，相比现有方案实现至少7.6倍加速和1.3倍能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在自然语言处理、视觉和科学计算领域表现优异，但存在计算和内存需求高的问题，限制了其部署效率。

Method: 提出ASTRA硅光子加速器，采用新型光学随机乘法器和一元/模拟零差累加技术，在最小化串扰的组织架构中高效处理动态张量计算。

Result: 与最先进加速器相比，ASTRA实现至少7.6倍加速和1.3倍能耗降低，验证了其在高效、可扩展Transformer推理方面的潜力。

Conclusion: ASTRA为Transformer推理提供了一种高效、可扩展且可持续的加速方案，展示了硅光子随机计算在深度学习加速领域的应用前景。

Abstract: Transformers achieve state-of-the-art performance in natural language processing, vision, and scientific computing, but demand high computation and memory. To address these challenges, we present ASTRA, the first silicon-photonic accelerator leveraging stochastic computing for transformers. ASTRA employs novel optical stochastic multipliers and unary/analog homodyne accumulation in a crosstalk-minimal organization to efficiently process dynamic tensor computations. Evaluations show at least 7.6x speedup and 1.3x lower energy overheads compared to state-of-the-art accelerators, highlighting ASTRA's potential for efficient, scalable, and sustainable transformer inference.

</details>


### [2] [Aging Aware Adaptive Voltage Scaling for Reliable and Efficient AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09994)
*Tong Xie,Zuodong Zhang,Chao Yang,Yuan Wang,Runsheng Wang,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对AI加速器上的深度神经网络(DNN)，提出了一种结合精确老化预测框架和容错电压调节策略的方法，在保证可靠性的同时显著降低了老化退化并节省了功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进工艺节点中老化效应日益显著，传统的自适应电压调节(AVS)技术忽略了DNN固有的容错能力，经常不必要地提高供电电压，从而加剧老化并增加功耗。需要一种既能保证AI推理可靠性又能提高效率的解决方案。

Method: 开发了包含历史效应和迭代外推的精确老化预测框架，实现全生命周期建模；在此基础上提出了利用DNN韧性的容错电压调节策略，相应延迟电压提升。

Result: 实验表明该方法减轻了最大电压基准的悲观性，PMOS和NMOS的预测阈值电压漂移(ΔVth)分别降低19.4%和19.1%；在代表性DNN工作负载上，老化退化降低高达45.8%(NMOS)和30.6%(PMOS)，相比忽略韧性的方法平均节省14.0%的寿命功耗。

Conclusion: 所提出的老化预测框架和容错电压调节策略能够有效平衡AI加速器的可靠性和能效，为先进工艺节点下的DNN部署提供了更优的老化管理方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) have showcased remarkable performance across various tasks and are widely deployed on AI accelerators fabricated in advanced technology nodes for efficiency. As aging effects become more pronounced, timing and voltage guardbands are increasingly applied. Aging-aware adaptive voltage scaling (AVS), which adjusts supply voltage based on on-chip aging scenarios, has emerged as a promising solution to avoid excessive guardbanding. However, conventional AVS techniques overlook the inherent resilience of DNNs and frequently raise the supply voltage unnecessarily, thereby exacerbating aging and increasing power consumption. To enable reliable and efficient AI inference with AVS, in this paper, we develop an accurate aging prediction framework that incorporates historical effects and iterative extrapolation for full-lifetime modeling. Building on this framework, we propose a fault-tolerant voltage scaling policy that exploits DNN resilience and defers voltage increases accordingly. Experiments show that our framework mitigates the pessimism of maximum-voltage baselines, reducing predicted threshold voltage shift (ΔVth) by 19.4% for PMOS and 19.1% for NMOS, respectively. Furthermore, evaluation on representative DNN workloads demonstrates that our optimization reduces aging degradation by up to 45.8% (NMOS) and 30.6% (PMOS) while achieving 14.0% average lifetime power savings compared to resilience-agnostic methods.

</details>


### [3] [Late Breaking Results: CHESSY: Coupled Hybrid Emulation with SystemC-FPGA Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10093)
*Lorenzo Ruotolo,Giovanni Pollo,Mohamed Amine Hamdi,Matteo Risso,Yukai Chen,Enrico Macii,Massimo Poncino,Sara Vinco,Alessio Burrello,Daniele Jahier Pagliari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源框架，通过将SystemC虚拟平台与FPGA仿真相连，实现了信息物理系统（CPS）的全系统协同仿真，在保持精度的同时相比RTL仿真获得了高达2500倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着信息物理系统（CPS）复杂度的增加，需要兼顾准确性、速度和易用性的早期原型设计工具。虚拟平台（VPs）提供快速的功能仿真，但当需要精确的时序建模且RTL仿真成本过高时，需要采用将关键数字组件部署在FPGA上的混合协同仿真方案。然而，现有的混合仿真工具大多是专有的，且依赖于特定供应商的FPGA特性。

Method: 引入了一个开源框架，将基于SystemC的虚拟平台与FPGA仿真连接起来，实现数字和非数字组件的全系统协同仿真。FPGA加速主要数字子系统的执行，而包装器通过JTAG协调时序和与虚拟平台的通信，保持与仿真外设的同步。

Result: 使用RISC-V SoC进行评估，并以生物信号处理领域为例，相比RTL仿真实现了高达2500倍的加速，同时相对于纯FPGA仿真，总仿真时间保持在2倍以内。

Conclusion: 该开源框架有效地解决了现有混合仿真工具专有性和供应商依赖的问题，成功实现了虚拟平台与FPGA仿真的协同工作，在保证时序精度的同时显著提升了仿真速度，为CPS的早期原型设计提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing complexity of cyber-physical systems (CPSs) calls for early prototyping tools that combine accuracy, speed, and usability. Virtual Platforms (VPs) provide fast functional simulation, but hybrid co-emulation solutions, in which key digital components are deployed on FPGA, become necessary when accurate timing modelling is required and RTL simulation is too costly. However, existing hybrid emulation tools are mostly proprietary, and rely on vendor-specific FPGA features. To address this gap, we introduce an open-source framework that connects SystemC-based VPs with FPGA emulation, enabling full-system co-emulation of digital and non-digital components. The FPGA accelerates the execution of main digital subsystems, while a wrapper coordinates timing and communication with the VP through JTAG, maintaining synchronization with simulated peripherals. Evaluations using a RISC-V SoC, with an example in the biosignals processing domain, show up to 2500x speedup compared to RTL simulation, while maintaining less than 2x total simulation time relative to pure FPGA emulation.

</details>


### [4] [A 129FPS Full HD Real-Time Accelerator for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10223)
*Fang-Chi Chang,Tian-Sheuan Chang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向AR/VR设备的低功耗3DGS硬件加速器及配套压缩流程，实现1080p实时渲染，面积缩小5.98倍、能效提升7.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AR/VR等移动设备上，大规模无界场景的渲染受限于3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）的计算、带宽和存储开销，亟需高效的硬件解决方案。

Method: 提出硬件友好的压缩流程（迭代剪枝、球谐阶数递减、矢量量化）；设计帧级流水线硬件加速器，融合基于点的剔除与投影、基于瓦片的排序与光栅化，跳过零雅可比矩阵乘法，采用无比较的确定性延迟瓦片排序。

Result: 模型尺寸压缩51.6倍，PSNR损失仅0.743 dB；TSMC 28nm@800MHz实现，面积0.66mm²，功耗0.219W，吞吐267.5 Mpixels/s，能效1219 Mpixels/J，可支持1080p@129 FPS。相比先前方案，面积缩小5.98倍、吞吐提升5.94倍、能效提升7.5倍。

Conclusion: 该设计显著降低了3DGS渲染的硬件资源与能耗，为AR/VR等移动终端上的高质量实时渲染提供了高效可行的硬件加速方案。

Abstract: Rendering large-scale, unbounded scenes on AR/VR-class devices is constrained by the computation, bandwidth, and storage cost of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). We propose a low-power, low-cost 3DGS hardware accelerator that renders full-HD images in real time, together with a hardware-friendly compression pipeline that combines iterative Gaussian pruning and fine-tuning, progressive spherical harmonics (SH) degree reduction, and vector quantization of all SH coefficients and colors. The scheme achieves a $51.6\times$ model-size reduction with a 0.743 dB PSNR loss. The accelerator uses a frame-level pipeline that integrates point-based culling and projection with tile-based sorting and rasterization, skips zero-Jacobian matrix multiplications (reducing processing elements by 63\% and computation by 53\%), and adopts comparison-free tile-based sorting with deterministic latency. Implemented in a TSMC 28-nm process at 800 MHz, the design occupies $0.66~\text{mm}^2$ with 1.1438 M gates and 120 kB SRAM, consumes 0.219 W, and delivers 1219 Mpixels/J at 267.5 Mpixels/s, enabling 1080p at 129 FPS. Overall, it is $5.98\times$ smaller in area, $5.94\times$ higher throughput, and delivers $7.5\times$ higher energy efficiency than prior 3DGS accelerators.

</details>


### [5] [LLM-PRISM: Characterizing Silent Data Corruption from Permanent GPU Faults in LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10390)
*Abhishek Tyagi,Saurabh Hukerikar,Nirmal Saxena,Yanxiang Huang,Philip Shirvani,Chung-Hsuan Tung,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM-PRISM方法，首次对LLM预训练过程中的硬件故障（静默数据损坏）韧性进行了硬件层面的系统性表征分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型训练规模扩大，制造缺陷和硅老化导致的硬件故障日益增加，表现为训练过程中梯度和参数的静默数据损坏（SDC），亟需研究LLM训练对此类故障的韧性。

Method: 提出LLM-PRISM方法，结合RTL级GPU故障模拟与嵌入Megatron-LM的随机注入引擎，在FP16、BF16和FP8三种数值格式下进行了7,664次训练实验，分析故障类型、速率和数值格式对韧性的影响。

Result: 发现LLM对低频故障具有一定抵抗能力，但影响高度不均匀；关键数据通路和特定精度格式即使在中等等故障率下也可能导致灾难性发散。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了首个硬件驱动的预训练SDC韧性表征，为理解LLM训练的硬件故障容错能力奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large-scale LLM training is increasingly susceptible to hardware defects stemming from manufacturing escapes and silicon aging. These defects manifest as Silent Data Corruption (SDC) that perturb gradients and parameters throughout the training process. We present LLM-PRISM, a methodology to characterize LLM pre-training resilience to hardware faults. LLM-PRISM couples RTL-level GPU fault simulation with a stochastic injection engine embedded in Megatron-LM. Through 7,664 training runs across FP16, BF16, and FP8 regimes, we analyze how fault type, rate, and numeric format govern resilience. We find that while LLMs resist low-frequency faults, impact is highly non-uniform; critical datapaths and specific precision formats can induce catastrophic divergence even at moderate fault rates. This study provides the first hardware-grounded, pre-training characterization of SDC resilience.

</details>


### [6] [Strix: Re-thinking NPU Reliability from a System Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10484)
*Jiapeng Guan,Jie Zhang,Hao Zhou,Ran Wei,Dean You,Hui Wang,Yingquan Wang,Tinglue Wang,Xudong Zhao,Jing Li,Zhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Strix，一个全栈NPU可靠性框架，通过微架构、ISA和编程方法的多层次协同设计，以极低的性能和硬件开销实现了亚微秒级的故障定位、错误检测与纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络（DNN）和大语言模型（LLM）在安全关键领域日益依赖硬件加速器，但技术缩放和模型复杂度的增加导致硬件故障更加频繁。现有的系统级机制将NPU视为单一单元，采用粗粒度复制，这带来了过高的性能和硬件开销，在可靠性需求与可部署方案之间存在显著差距。

Method: 在开源SoC上构建了一个全栈NPU可靠性框架Strix，跨越微架构、指令集架构（ISA）和编程方法三个层面。具体方法包括：沿系统推理流水线对NPU进行重新划分，识别主要故障模式，并为不同故障模式附加针对性的保护机制。

Result: Strix实现了亚微秒级的故障定位、错误检测和纠正能力，性能开销仅为1.04倍的 slowdown，硬件开销极小。

Conclusion: 通过全栈协同设计对NPU进行细粒度重新划分并部署针对性保护机制，Strix成功填补了可靠性需求与可部署方案之间的差距，为安全关键领域的DNN/LLM推理提供了高效、低成本的可靠性保障方案。

Abstract: DNNs and LLMs increasingly rely on hardware accelerators, including in safety-critical domains, while technology scaling and growing model complexity make hardware faults more frequent. Existing system-level mechanisms typically treat the NPU as a monolithic unit, using coarse-grained replication that incurs prohibitive performance and hardware overheads, leaving a gap between reliability requirements and deployable solutions. To bridge this gap, we present Strix, a full-stack NPU reliability framework on an open-source SoC, spanning micro-architecture, ISA, and programming methods. Strix re-partitions the NPU along the system inference pipeline, identifies dominant failure modes, and attaches targeted safeguards, achieving sub-micro-second fault localisation, error detection, and correction with only 1.04$\times$ slowdown and minimal hardware overhead.

</details>


### [7] [From Characterization to Microarchitecture: Designing an Elegant and Reliable BFP-Based NPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10494)
*Jie Zhang,Jiapeng Guan,Hao Zhou,Xiaomeng Han,Tinglue Wang,Ran Wei,Zhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次对块浮点（BFP）NPU进行了深入的可靠性研究，并提出了一种容错微架构设计，以极低的性能和硬件开销实现了接近双模冗余的可靠性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块浮点（BFP）作为一种结合了宽动态范围和高硬件效率的数据格式，在边缘神经处理单元（NPU）中日益普及。然而，其在硬件故障下的行为表现以及在安全关键型部署中的适用性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 研究人员在NPU上进行了RTL级故障注入，通过位级和路径级分析揭示了显著的异构脆弱性，并发现传统的端到端检查在非线性块缩放下会失效。基于这些发现，研究团队设计了一种容错的BFP NPU微架构，该架构采用行/列分块策略，将定点尾数计算与标量指数路径解耦，并为两者分别引入了超轻量级的保护机制。

Result: 实验结果表明，该设计以仅3.55%的几何平均性能开销和不到2%的硬件成本，实现了接近双模冗余的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅揭示了BFP格式在硬件故障下的独特脆弱性，还证明了通过语义对齐的微架构设计，可以极低的成本显著提升NPU的可靠性，为其在安全关键领域的应用提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Block Floating-Point (BFP) is emerging as an attractive data format for edge Neural Processing Units (NPUs), combining wide dynamic range with high hardware efficiency. However, its behavior under hardware faults and suitability for safety-critical deployments remain underexplored. Here, we present the first in-depth empirical reliability study of BFP-based NPUs. Using RTL-level fault injection on NPUs, our bit- and path-level analysis reveals pronounced heterogeneous vulnerabilities and shows conventional end-to-end check becomes ineffective under nonlinear block scaling. Guided by these insights, we design a fault-tolerant BFP-based NPU microarchitecture that aligns the BFP computational semantics with reliability constraints. The design uses a row/column-wise blocking strategy to decouple the fixed-point mantissa computations from the scalar exponent path, and introduces ultra-lightweight protection mechanisms for each. Experimental results demonstrate our design achieves near-dual modular redundancy reliability with only $3.55\%$ geometric mean performance overhead and less than $2\%$ hardware cost.

</details>


### [8] [L-PCN: A Point Cloud Accelerator Exploiting Spatial Locality through Octree-based Islandization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10716)
*Yiming Gao,Jieming Yin,Yuxiang Wang,Xiangru Chen,Zhilei Chai,Bowen Jiang,Jiliang Zhang,Herman Lam*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出L-PCN加速器，利用点云数据结构化步骤中的空间局部性特征，通过八叉树岛屿划分和枢纽调度两种技术，显著减少重复计算与数据访问，实现最高3.2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点云网络（PCN）通常包含数据结构化（DS）和特征计算（FC）两个步骤。本文发现DS步骤中存在一个未被现有加速器利用的重要特征：由于收集的点子集存在重叠点，导致具有空间局部性。这一特征导致了PCN中大量重复操作，但现有工作未对此进行优化。

Method: 采用算法-硬件协同设计，提出两种技术：1）基于八叉树的岛屿划分技术，通过八叉树邻接收集将点云划分为岛屿，同一岛屿内的点子集具有强空间相关性；2）基于枢纽的调度方法，通过动态缓存、更新和重用重复数据来利用岛屿内数据重用。这两种方法在岛屿化单元中实现，可无缝集成到标准PCN工作流中。

Result: 理论分析表明，L-PCN在特征获取方面减少55.2%至93.8%，在特征计算方面减少45.4%至80.6%。在Intel Arria 10 GX FPGA上的实验结果表明，作为插件集成后，现有最先进的PCN加速器可获得1.2x至3.2x的额外加速。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘点云网络中DS步骤的空间局部性特征，所提出的岛屿化方法能够有效减少重复计算和数据访问，显著提升PCN加速器性能，为点云处理加速器设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Existing Point Cloud Networks (PCNs) have proven to achieve great success in many point cloud tasks such as object part segmentation, shape classification, and so on. The most popular point-based PCNs are usually composed of two sequential steps: Data Structuring (DS) and Feature Computation (FC). In this paper, we first describe an important characteristic of the PCN-specific DS step that has not been addressed in existing PCN accelerators: the spatial locality resulting from overlapping points of the gathered point subsets. Using algorithm-hardware co-design, L-PCN (Locality-aware PCN) proposes two novel techniques to exploit this characteristic to reduce the large amount of repetitive operations in the overall PCN. The first of which is a point cloud partitioning technique, Octree-based Islandization. Using Octree-based adjacency gathering, a point cloud is partitioned into islands in L-PCN, where the point subsets inside the same island exhibit a strong spatial correlation. After partitioning, L-PCN performs the rest of PCN steps at the granularity of islands. The second method of L-PCN is scheduling the intra-island computation with a Hub-based Scheduling to exploit the intra-island data reuse by dynamically caching, updating, and reusing the repeated data. The two methods are implemented in an Islandization Unit, which can be seamlessly integrated into standard PCN workflow. Our evaluation shows that based on our methods for exploiting spatial locality, L-PCN achieves a theoretical reduction in feature fetching ranging from 55.2% to 93.8% and in feature computation ranging from 45.4% to 80.6% during the PCN process. For experimentation, prototype L-PCN accelerators are implemented on the Intel Arria 10 GX FPGA. Experimental results prove that with the Islandization Unit as a plug-in, state-of-the-art PCN accelerators can achieve an additional speedup ranging from 1.2x to 3.2x.

</details>


### [9] [EMSpice 3: Full-chip Temperature-Aware Multiphysics Electromigration and IR-Drop Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10743)
*Haotian Lu,Sheldon X. -D. Tan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出EMSpice 3，一个全芯片温度感知多物理场框架，用于电源网格的电迁移、热迁移和IR降耦合分析。实验表明空间热变化显著影响EM可靠性（相同平均温度下TTF差异可达70%），Krylov加速求解器实现1.18x-1.50x运行时缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EM可靠性分析通常基于平均温度假设，忽略了空间热变化和工作负载影响，导致分析结果不够准确。需要开发实用的全芯片、温度感知的多物理场耦合分析框架，实现更精确的EM可靠性评估。

Method: 提出EMSpice 3框架，统一提取的网表、可配置参数和芯片级热图，支持温度感知的永久性筛选、带迭代电阻更新的瞬态EM/TM应力模拟和蒙特卡洛寿命分析。集成扩展有理Krylov降阶方法加速大规模树状网络仿真，并与Synopsys ICC和Fusion Compiler接口实现实际部署。

Result: 在六个设计上验证：相同平均温度的热分布下，RISC-V核心TTF差异超70%，ARM Cortex-A核心近50%；Krylov加速求解器实现1.18x-1.50x运行时缩减；蒙特卡洛分析显示TTF变化对设计敏感（扩散率和临界应力20%变化下，RISC-V约25%，ARM仅0.03%）。

Conclusion: EMSpice 3成功实现了实用、热图感知和工作负载感知的全芯片EM可靠性评估，证明了空间热变化对EM可靠性的显著影响，为芯片设计提供了更精确的可靠性sign-off方法。

Abstract: In this work, we present EMSpice 3, a full-chip temperature-aware multiphysics framework for coupled electromigration (EM), thermomigration (TM), and IR-drop analysis of power-grid networks. It unifies extracted netlists, configurable parameters, and optional chip-level thermal maps into a single flow supporting temperature-aware immortality screening, transient EM/TM stress simulation with iterative resistance updates, and optional Monte Carlo lifetime analysis. To accelerate large-tree simulations, EMSpice 3 integrates an extended rational Krylov reduction method into the transient solver without loss of accuracy. It also interfaces with Synopsys ICC and Fusion Compiler for practical deployment. By incorporating realistic spatial thermal maps into the reliability loop, the framework enables map-aware EM sign-off beyond average-temperature assumptions. Experiments on six designs show that spatial thermal variation significantly impacts EM reliability even with identical average temperature. For a RISC-V core, equal-average thermal profiles yield over 70% spread in time to failure (TTF), while an ARM Cortex-A core shows nearly 50%. The Krylov-accelerated solver achieves 1.18x - 1.50x runtime reduction. Monte Carlo analysis reveals strong design dependence: under 20% variation in diffusivity and critical stress, TTF variation is about 25\% for RISC-V but only 0.03% for ARM. These results demonstrate that EMSpice 3 enables practical, map-aware, and workload-aware full-chip EM reliability assessment.

</details>


### [10] [The xPU-athalon: Quantifying the Competition of AI Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10852)
*Alicia Golden,Carole-Jean Wu,Gu-Yeon Wei,David Brooks*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对Cerebras、SambaNova、Groq、Gaudi、TPUv5e等AI加速器与NVIDIA/AMD GPU进行了全面的量化对比评估。研究发现最佳硬件平台随批次大小、序列长度和模型规模变化而不同；专用加速器空闲功耗比GPU高10-60%，需高利用率才能实现效率收益；各平台在可编程性和软件栈成熟度上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI计算对效率需求的不断提升，各类挑战GPU长期主导地位的加速器架构纷纷涌现。然而，目前缺乏对这些新兴AI加速器与主流GPU在真实工作负载下性能、功耗、能效和可编程性方面的全面量化对比分析，阻碍了硬件选型和系统优化的决策。

Method: 1. 硬件平台覆盖：评估Cerebras CS-3、SambaNova SN-40、Groq、Gaudi、TPUv5e，对比NVIDIA（A100、H100）和AMD（MI-300X）GPU。
2. 评估维度：延迟、吞吐量、功耗、能效。
3. 工作负载类型：（i）端到端工作负载；（ii）单个计算原语基准测试。
4. 详细分析：LLM推理prefill和decode阶段的功耗测量；通信能耗成本量化；空闲功耗测量；可编程性评估（编译时间、软件栈成熟度）。

Result: 1. 性能权衡：最优硬件平台随批次大小、序列长度和模型规模变化，存在巨大的底层优化空间。
2. 功耗发现：Cerebras、SambaNova和Gaudi的空闲功耗比NVIDIA和AMD GPU高出10-60%，高利用率对实现承诺的效率收益至关重要。
3. 可编程性：各平台编译时间和软件栈成熟度存在差异，影响实际性能达成。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了AI加速器领域的复杂权衡关系：不存在单一最优平台，硬件选择需根据具体工作负载特征（批次大小、序列长度、模型规模）进行优化；专用加速器的高空闲功耗特性要求高利用率才能发挥效率优势；软件生态成熟度是影响实际性能达成的关键因素。这些发现为AI系统设计者和硬件选型提供了重要的定量依据。

Abstract: The push for greater efficiency in AI computation has given rise to an array of accelerator architectures that increasingly challenge the GPU's long-standing dominance. In this work, we provide a quantitative view of this evolving landscape of AI accelerators, including the Cerebras CS-3, SambaNova SN-40, Groq, Gaudi, and TPUv5e platforms, and compare against both NVIDIA (A100, H100) and AMD (MI-300X) GPUs. We evaluate key trade-offs in latency, throughput, power consumption, and energy-efficiency across both (i) end-to-end workloads and (ii) benchmarks of individual computational primitives. Notably, we find the optimal hardware platform varies across batch size, sequence length, and model size, revealing a large underlying optimization space. Our analysis includes detailed power measurements across the prefill and decode phases of LLM inference, as well as quantification of the energy cost of communication. We additionally find that Cerebras, SambaNova, and Gaudi have 10-60% higher idle power than NVIDIA and AMD GPUs, emphasizing the importance of high utilization in order to realize promised efficiency gains. Finally, we assess programmability across platforms based on our experiments with real profiled workloads, comparing the compilation times and software stack maturity required to achieve promised performance.

</details>


### [11] [Automated SVA Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11044)
*Lik Tung Fu,Qihang Wang,Shaokai Ren,Mengli Zhang,Sichao Yang,Jun Liu,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SVA Generator框架，通过AST约束注入和自动化监督流水线，将自然语言描述转换为可执行的SystemVerilog断言，在深层AST结构上实现了平均+22.7百分点的语义等价率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能验证在现代IC开发中占据主导成本，SystemVerilog断言（SVAs）对于仿真监控和形式属性检查至关重要。然而，手工编写SVA既耗时又容易出错，而直接使用通用大语言模型生成属性也存在不可靠的问题：生成的属性往往语法无效或语义错误，且缺乏高质量的领域训练数据。

Method: 提出SVA Generator数据驱动框架，采用AST（抽象语法树）锚定的约束注入技术，以及自动化监督流水线，通过去重和约束检查来强制结构一致性并减少幻觉。同时，构建了按AST深度分层的基准测试套件，并利用形式属性等价性检查来独立量化语义正确性。

Result: 在所有难度层级上，SVA Generator实现了与强通用LLM基线相当的语法通过率（SPR），但在更深层级上实现了显著更高的语义等价率（SER）：在D2上+24.5个百分点，D3上+26.0个百分点，D4上+17.5个百分点，相对于最佳通用LLM在D2-D4上平均提升+22.7个百分点SER。

Conclusion: 高保真数据构建和深度分层基准测试是实现可靠、语义保持的SVA生成的关键因素。该研究证明了数据驱动方法在解决领域特定代码生成问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Functional verification remains a dominant cost in modern IC development, and SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs) are critical for simulation-based monitoring and formal property checking. However, writing SVAs by hand is time-consuming and error-prone. Directly prompting general-purpose large language models (LLMs) is also unreliable: the generated properties are often syntactically invalid or semantically incorrect, and the problem is exacerbated by scarce, high-quality domain training data. We present SVA Generator, a data-centric framework that translates natural-language SVA Descriptions (SVADs) into executable SVAs. It uses AST-grounded constraint injection and an automated supervision pipeline that enforces structural consistency and reduces hallucinations via de-duplication and constraint checks. To enable rigorous evaluation, we introduce a benchmark suite stratified by AST depth and use formal property equivalence checking to quantify semantic correctness separately from syntax validity, by checking mutual implication between the generated and reference properties under the same clocking and environment assumptions. Across all difficulty tiers, SVA Generator achieves comparable Syntax Pass Rate (SPR) to strong general LLM baselines, while delivering substantially higher Semantic Equivalence Rate (SER) on deeper tiers: +24.5 pp on D2, +26.0 pp on D3, and +17.5 pp on D4 relative to the best-performing general LLM, corresponding to a +22.7 pp SER improvement on average over D2--D4. These results highlight that high-fidelity data construction and depth-stratified benchmarking are key to reliable, semantics-preserving SVA generation.

</details>


### [12] [Technology solutions targeting the performance of gen-AI inference in resource constrained platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11128)
*Joyjit Kundu,Joshua Klein,Aakash Patel,Dwaipayan Biswas*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式AI（尤其是LLM推理）在处理长上下文时面临的片上/片外内存压力问题，利用分层Roofline分析模型评估了两种新兴内存技术方案：针对大模型（13B参数）的高带宽存储（HBS）方案，以及针对小模型（1B参数）的绑定全局缓冲存储器Chiplet方案，旨在缓解内存容量与带宽瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI工作负载（特别是大语言模型推理）的兴起，多模态输入（如视频流、图像）和下游应用（如QA、长文档分析）导致上下文长度显著增加，需要缓存大量的Key和Value状态。这种趋势加剧了片上/片外内存压力，尤其是在资源受限的移动设备上，即使是低并发推理也会带来严峻的内存容量压力和运行时内存管理复杂性。

Method: 作者提出并应用了一种基于分层Roofline的分析性能模型。该模型被用于评估两种新兴内存技术方案在缓解内存容量和带宽压力方面的性能影响：1）针对大模型（如13B参数）和长上下文场景，研究了高带宽存储（HBS）的性能表现，并界定了实现可接受交互吞吐量所需的带宽/延迟要求；2）针对小模型（如1B参数），评估了绑定全局缓冲存储器Chiplet的优势，并提出了最佳利用策略。

Result: 研究揭示了大模型在长上下文场景下的性能瓶颈特征，明确了HBS技术要实现可接受的交互吞吐量所需的特定带宽和延迟指标；同时验证了绑定全局缓冲存储器Chiplet对小模型推理的性能增益，并提出了相应的优化利用策略。这些结果为不同规模模型选择合适的内存技术方案提供了量化依据。

Conclusion: 新兴内存技术（如HBS和绑定全局缓冲存储器Chiplet）有望有效缓解生成式AI推理中的内存压力。针对不同规模的模型和上下文长度，需采用差异化的内存架构策略：大模型应关注HBS的带宽/延迟优化，小模型则可借助Chiplet架构实现高效推理。这为未来资源受限设备上部署生成式AI提供了技术路径。

Abstract: The rise of generative AI workloads, particularly language model inference, is intensifying on/off-chip memory pressure. Multimodal inputs such as video streams or images and downstream applications like Question Answering (QA) and analysis over large documents incur long context lengths, requiring caching of massive Key and Value states of the previous tokens. Even a low degree of concurrent inference serving on resource-constrained devices, like mobiles, can further add to memory capacity pressure and runtime memory management complexity. In this paper, we evaluate the performance implications of two emerging technology solutions to alleviate the memory pressure in terms of both capacity and bandwidth using a hierarchical roofline-based analytical performance model. For large models (e.g., 13B parameters) and context lengths, we investigate the performance implications of High Bandwidth Storage (HBS) and outline bandwidth/latency requirements to achieve an acceptable throughput for interactivity. For small models (e.g., 1B parameters), we evaluate the merit of a bonded global buffer memory chiplet and propose how to best utilize it.

</details>


### [13] [CapBench: A Multi-PDK Dataset for Machine-Learning-Based Post-Layout Capacitance Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11202)
*Hector R. Rodriguez,Jiechen Huang,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了CapBench，一个基于开源设计、跨三个工艺节点的电容提取可复现多PDK数据集，包含61,855个3D窗口，并评估了10种机器学习架构，揭示了精度与速度的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电容提取研究缺乏大规模、多样化、可复现的基准数据集。作者旨在构建一个基于真实开源设计、覆盖多工艺节点、提供高保真标签的标准化数据集，以促进机器学习在电容提取领域的研究和公平比较。

Method: 从开源设计（单核CPU、SoC、媒体加速器）出发，使用OpenROAD流程在ASAP7、NanGate45和Sky130HD三个工艺节点上完成布局布线。提取三种尺寸共61,855个3D窗口，利用随机游走求解器RWCap生成高保真电容标签并与工业标准Raphael验证（平均绝对误差0.64%）。数据预处理为密度图、图表示和点云三种形式，并评估了CNN、Point Cloud Transformer、GNN等10种机器学习架构。

Result: 构建了包含61,855个样本的多样化数据集。在基准测试中，CNN实现了最低误差（1.75%）；GNN速度提升高达41.4倍，但误差较高（10.2%），清晰展示了精度与速度的权衡。代码与数据集已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: CapBench填补了电容提取领域大规模可复现基准数据集的空白，为后续机器学习方法研究提供了坚实基础和统一评估平台，揭示了不同网络架构在精度与推理速度间的关键权衡。

Abstract: We present CapBench, a fully reproducible, multi-PDK dataset for capacitance extraction. The dataset is derived from open-source designs, including single-core CPUs, systems-on-chip, and media accelerators. All designs are fully placed and routed using 14 independent OpenROAD flow runs spanning three technology nodes: ASAP7, NanGate45, and Sky130HD. From these layouts, we extract 61,855 3D windows across three size tiers to enable transfer learning and scalability studies. High-fidelity capacitance labels are generated using RWCap, a state-of-the-art random-walk solver, and validated against the industry-standard Raphael, achieving a mean absolute error of 0.64% for total capacitance. Each window is pre-processed into density maps, graph representations, and point clouds. We evaluate 10 machine learning architectures that illustrate dataset usage and serve as baselines, including convolutional neural networks (CNNs), point cloud transformers, and graph neural networks (GNNs). CNNs demonstrate the lowest errors (1.75%), while GNNs are up to 41.4x faster but exhibit larger errors (10.2%), illustrating a clear accuracy-speed trade-off. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/THU-numbda/CapBench.

</details>


### [14] [EdgeCIM: A Hardware-Software Co-Design for CIM-Based Acceleration of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11512)
*Jinane Bazzi,Mariam Rakka,Fadi Kurdahi,Mohammed E. Fouda,Ahmed Eltawil*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出EdgeCIM框架，通过65nm存内计算宏单元和分块映射策略解决边缘设备上小语言模型推理的内存瓶颈问题，相比NVIDIA Orin Nano实现7.3倍吞吐量和49.59倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加速器在边缘设备上部署小语言模型(SLMs)时存在根本性低效问题：GPU虽能高效处理预填充工作负载，但自回归解码阶段主要由GEMV操作主导，属于内存受限型操作，导致利用率低且边缘端能耗过高。

Method: 提出EdgeCIM软硬件协同设计框架，核心包括：1) 65nm工艺实现的存内计算(CIM)宏单元；2) 分块映射策略平衡流水线阶段；3) 最大化并行性同时缓解DRAM带宽瓶颈；4) 模拟器支持高达4B参数SLM的设计空间探索，识别延迟和能耗的帕累托最优配置。

Result: 在LLaMA3.2-1B上相比NVIDIA Orin Nano实现7.3倍吞吐量提升和49.59倍能效改善；在LLaMA3.2-3B上相比Qualcomm SA8255P实现9.95倍吞吐量提升。在INT4精度下，对TinyLLaMA、LLaMA3.2、Phi-3.5-mini、Qwen2.5、SmolLM等模型平均达到336.42 tokens/s和173.02 tokens/J。

Conclusion: EdgeCIM为实时、高能效的边缘级SLM推理提供了极具竞争力的解决方案，通过CIM架构创新有效解决了边缘设备上自回归解码的内存带宽瓶颈问题。

Abstract: The growing demand for deploying Small Language Models (SLMs) on edge devices, including laptops, smartphones, and embedded platforms, has exposed fundamental inefficiencies in existing accelerators. While GPUs handle prefill workloads efficiently, the autoregressive decoding phase is dominated by GEMV operations that are inherently memory-bound, resulting in poor utilization and prohibitive energy costs at the edge. In this work, we present EdgeCIM, a hardware-software co-design framework that rethinks accelerator design for end-to-end decoder-only inference. At its core is a CIM macro, implemented in 65nm, coupled with a tile-based mapping strategy that balances pipeline stages, maximizing parallelism while alleviating DRAM bandwidth bottlenecks. Our simulator enables design space exploration of SLMs up to 4B parameters, identifying Pareto-optimal configurations in terms of latency and energy. Compared to an NVIDIA Orin Nano, EdgeCIM achieves up to 7.3x higher throughput and 49.59x better energy efficiency on LLaMA3.2-1B, and delivers 9.95x higher throughput than Qualcomm SA8255P on LLaMA3.2-3B. Extensive benchmarks on TinyLLaMA-1.1B, LLaMA3.2 (1B, 3B), Phi-3.5-mini-3.8B, Qwen2.5 (0.5B, 1.5B, 3B), SmolLM2-1.7B, SmolLM3-3B, and Qwen3 (0.6B, 1.7B, 4B) reveal that our accelerator, under INT4 precision, achieves on average 336.42 tokens/s and 173.02 tokens/J. These results establish EdgeCIM as a compelling solution towards real-time, energy-efficient edge-scale SLM inference.

</details>


### [15] [CUTEv2: Unified and Configurable Matrix Extension for Diverse CPU Architectures with Minimal Design Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11615)
*Jinpeng Ye,Chongxi Wang,Wenqing Li,Bin Yuan,Shiyi Wang,Fenglu Zhang,Junyu Yue,Jianan Xie,Yunhao Ye,Haoyu Deng,Yingkun Zhou,Xin Cheng,Fuxin Zhang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一且可配置的CPU矩阵扩展架构，通过解耦矩阵单元与CPU流水线实现跨平台低开销集成，支持混合精度与异步计算，在多个开源CPU平台上实现了超过90%的矩阵单元利用率，相比Intel AMX在ResNet、BERT和Llama3上分别获得1.57x、1.57x和2.31x加速，面积开销仅0.53mm²@14nm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPU矩阵扩展设计通常伴随显著的硬件与软件开销：与CPU流水线紧耦合导致跨平台集成困难，细粒度同步指令阻碍高性能内核开发，无法满足现代AI工作负载快速增长的需求。

Method: 提出统一可配置的矩阵扩展架构：1）将矩阵单元与CPU流水线解耦，降低集成开销同时保持与计算/存储资源的紧密协作；2）可配置矩阵单元支持混合精度运算，适应不同计算需求与存储带宽约束；3）引入灵活粒度的异步矩阵乘法抽象，隐藏硬件细节，简化矩阵-向量重叠执行，支持统一软件栈。

Result: 在四个开源CPU RTL平台集成并评估：GEMM负载下矩阵单元利用率超过90%；配置与Intel AMX相当的计算吞吐和存储带宽时，ResNet加速1.57x、BERT加速1.57x、Llama3加速2.31x，其中超30%性能提升来自矩阵-向量重叠执行；4 TOPS@2GHz矩阵单元在14nm CMOS下仅占0.53mm²。

Conclusion: 该架构展示了强跨平台适应性与有效的软硬件协同优化能力，为开源社区提供了一种实用的矩阵扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Matrix extensions have emerged as an essential feature in modern CPUs to address the surging demands of AI workloads. However, existing designs often incur substantial hardware and software design overhead. Tight coupling with the CPU pipeline complicates integration across diverse CPUs, while fine-grained synchronous instructions hinder the development of high-performance kernels.
  This paper proposes a unified and configurable CPU matrix extension architecture. By decoupling matrix units from the CPU pipeline, the design enables low-overhead integration while maintaining close coordination with existing compute and memory resources. The configurable matrix unit supports mixed-precision operations and adapts to diverse compute demands and memory bandwidth constraints. An asynchronous matrix multiplication abstraction with flexible granularity conceals hardware details, simplifies matrix-vector overlap, and supports a unified software stack.
  The architecture is integrated into four open-source CPU RTL platforms and evaluated on representative AI models. Matrix unit utilization under GEMM workloads exceeds 90% across all platforms. When configured with compute throughput and memory bandwidth comparable to Intel AMX, our design achieves speedups of 1.57x, 1.57x, and 2.31x on ResNet, BERT, and Llama3, with over 30% of the gains attributed to overlapped matrix-vector execution. A 4 TOPS@2GHz matrix unit occupies only 0.53 mm\textsuperscript{2} in 14nm CMOS. These results demonstrate strong cross-platform adaptability and effective hardware-software co-optimization, offering a practical matrix extension for the open-source community.

</details>

### [16] [The Diffusion-Attention Connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09560)
*Julio Candanedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformers、扩散映射和磁拉普拉斯算子本质上是同一马尔可夫几何框架下的不同特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将Transformers、扩散映射和磁拉普拉斯算子视为相互独立的工具，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解它们之间的内在联系。本文旨在打破这一认知壁垒，建立一个统一的几何视角。

Method: 作者基于Softmax前的查询-键值分数构建了一个统一的马尔可夫几何框架。核心方法是定义一种QK“双向散度”，通过对其指数化和归一化处理，能够推导出注意力机制、扩散映射和磁扩散。同时，利用专家乘积和薛定谔桥理论，将这些工具统一组织在平衡态、非平衡稳态和驱动动力学的理论架构中。

Result: 研究成功证明了注意力机制、扩散映射和磁扩散均为QK双向散度在不同参数条件下的具体表现形式。通过引入专家乘积与薛定谔桥，建立了这些方法之间的数学联系，并将其系统性地归类于平衡态、非平衡稳态和驱动动力学三种物理场景中。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个统一的数学理论框架，将看似无关的深度学习注意力机制与经典的流形学习、图信号处理方法有机融合，为理解这些算法的内在机理提供了全新的视角和理论工具。

Abstract: Transformers, diffusion-maps, and magnetic Laplacians are usually treated as separate tools; we show they are all different regimes of a single Markov geometry built from pre-softmax query-scores. We define a QK "bidivergence" whose exponentiated and normalized forms yield attention, diffusion-maps, and magnetic diffusion. And use product of experts and Schrödinger-bridges to connect and organize them into equilibrium, nonequilibrium steady-state, and driven dynamics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有超过1000个FDA授权的AI医疗设备，但关于模型在不同患者亚组间性能是否一致的正式公平性评估非常罕见，亟需对医学AI模型的公平性进行系统性研究。

Method: 在两个独立数据集的648名胶质瘤患者上评估18个开源脑肿瘤分割模型（共11,664次模型推理），采用单变量、贝叶斯多变量、空间和表征四个维度进行分析，并进行体素级空间元分析和高维潜在空间聚类分析。

Result: 患者身份比模型选择更能解释性能差异；临床因素（分子诊断、肿瘤分级、切除范围）比模型架构更能预测分割准确性；发现具有神经解剖学定位且跨模型一致的分区特异性偏差；模型性能在高维潜在空间中显著聚类；较新模型趋向更公平但无正式公平性保证。

Conclusion: 医学影像AI模型存在显著的算法脆弱性轴，公平性问题不容忽视；发布了开源无代码工具Fairboard，旨在降低医学影像中公平模型监控的门槛。

Abstract: Despite there now being more than 1,000 FDA-authorised AI medical devices, formal equity assessments -- whether model performance is uniform across patient subgroups -- are rare. Here, we evaluate the equity of 18 open-source brain tumour segmentation models across 648 glioma patients from two independent datasets (n = 11,664 model inferences) along distinct univariate, Bayesian multivariate, spatial, and representational dimensions. We find that patient identity consistently explains more performance variance than model choice, with clinical factors, including molecular diagnosis, tumour grade, and extent of resection, predicting segmentation accuracy more strongly than model architecture. A voxel-wise spatial meta-analysis identifies neuroanatomically localised biases that are compartment-specific yet often consistent across models. Within a high-dimensional latent space of lesion masks and clinic-demographic features, model performance clusters significantly, indicating that the patient feature space contains axes of algorithmic vulnerability. Although newer models tend toward greater equity, none provide a formal fairness guarantee. Lastly, we release Fairboard, an open-source, no-code dashboard that lowers barriers to equitable model monitoring in medical imaging.

</details>


### [18] [Deliberative Alignment is Deep, but Uncertainty Remains: Inference time safety improvement in reasoning via attribution of unsafe behavior to base model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09665)
*Pankayaraj Pathmanathan,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深思对齐中教师-学生模型间存在安全对齐差距，学生模型可能保留基础模型的不安全行为。提出BoN采样方法，在潜空间将不安全行为归因于基础模型并降低其排名，在多个基准上显著降低攻击成功率且几乎不损失效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管拒绝训练被广泛采用，但这类对齐方法较浅，存在缺陷。深思对齐虽能从更强的推理模型蒸馏推理能力以增强安全性，但其在语言模型中的具体影响尚不明确，教师与学生模型间的对齐差距及潜在的不安全行为保留问题亟待研究。

Method: 1. 分析深思对齐中教师与学生模型间的对齐差距及其对安全性和通用效用的影响。
2. 提出一种Best-of-N (BoN) 采样方法，在潜空间中将不安全行为归因于基础模型，并据此降低不安全响应的排名。

Result: 在7个教师模型和6个不同类别与规模的学生模型上的实验表明：
- DAN基准平均攻击成功率(ASR)降低28.2%
- WildJailbreak基准ASR降低31.3%
- StrongREJECT基准ASR降低35.4%
这些安全增益在RL训练后仍然存在，且效用损失极小。

Conclusion: 深思对齐虽然能传递推理模式，但学生模型仍可能保留基础模型的不安全行为，存在安全推理的不确定性。所提出的BoN采样方法能有效识别并抑制这种源自基础模型的不安全行为，为提升模型安全性提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: While the wide adoption of refusal training in large language models (LLMs) has showcased improvements in model safety, recent works have highlighted shortcomings due to the shallow nature of these alignment methods. To this end, the work on Deliberative alignment proposed distilling reasoning capabilities from stronger reasoning models, thereby instilling deeper safety in LLMs. In this work, we study the impact of deliberative alignment in language models. First, we show that despite being larger in model size and stronger in safety capability, there exists an alignment gap between teacher and student language models, which affects both the safety and general utility of the student model. Furthermore, we show that models aligned through deliberative alignment can retain unsafe behaviors from the base model despite learning the reasoning patterns of larger reasoning models. Building upon this observation, we propose a BoN sampling method that attributes the unsafe behavior back to the base LLMs in the latent space, thereby down-ranking unsafe responses to gain a meaningful improvement in model safety across multiple safety benchmarks with minimal loss in utility. In particular, across 7 teacher models and 6 student models of different classes and sizes, we show an average attack success rate (ASR) reduction of 28.2% in DAN, 31.3% in WildJailbreak and 35.4 % in StrongREJECT benchmarks. We further show that these safety gains prevail post RL training, thus highlighting the uncertainty in safety reasoning and it's explicit attribution to the base model.

</details>


### [19] [Human-like Working Memory Interference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09670)
*Hua-Dong Xiong,Li Ji-An,Jiaqi Huang,Robert C. Wilson,Kwonjoon Lee,Xue-Xin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现预训练大语言模型(LLMs)存在工作记忆局限性，其机制源于表征干扰而非简单的容量限制。模型通过主动抑制任务无关内容来提取目标信息，这种机制与人类工作记忆高度相似，表明生物和人工系统面临共同的计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer架构通过注意力机制可完全访问历史上下文，但预训练的大语言模型仍表现出工作记忆限制。本文旨在探究为何大规模模型存在此局限性，以及其背后的计算机制是什么。

Method: 对比训练好的两层Transformer与多种预训练LLM在工作记忆任务上的表现；分析模型是否复制了人类的工作记忆干扰特征（记忆负载效应、近因效应、刺激统计偏差）；通过因果干预实验抑制刺激内容信息，验证表征干扰机制。

Result: LLMs表现出与人类相似的工作记忆干扰特征：性能随记忆负载增加而下降，受近因效应和刺激统计影响；工作记忆容量与通用基准测试表现正相关；所有模型收敛于共同机制——将多个记忆项编码为纠缠表示，通过主动抑制无关内容来提取目标信息；抑制刺激信息的干预措施能提升性能。

Conclusion: 表征干扰是预训练LLMs工作记忆的核心约束因素。生物和人工系统的工作记忆限制可能反映了一个共同的计算挑战：在干扰条件下选择任务相关信息。这为理解智能系统的工作记忆机制提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Intelligent systems must maintain and manipulate task-relevant information online to adapt to dynamic environments and changing goals. This capacity, known as working memory, is fundamental to human reasoning and intelligence. Despite having on the order of 100 billion neurons, both biological and artificial systems exhibit limitations in working memory. This raises a key question: why do large language models (LLMs) show such limitations, given that transformers have full access to prior context through attention? We find that although a two-layer transformer can be trained to solve working memory tasks perfectly, a diverse set of pretrained LLMs continues to show working memory limitations. Notably, LLMs reproduce interference signatures observed in humans: performance degrades with increasing memory load and is biased by recency and stimulus statistics. Across models, stronger working memory capacity correlates with broader competence on standard benchmarks, mirroring its link to general intelligence in humans. Yet despite substantial variability in working memory performance, LLMs surprisingly converge on a common computational mechanism. Rather than directly copying the relevant memory item from context, models encode multiple memory items in entangled representations, such that successful recall depends on interference control -- actively suppressing task-irrelevant content to isolate the target for readout. Moreover, a targeted intervention that suppresses stimulus content information improves performance, providing causal support for representational interference. Together, these findings identify representational interference as a core constraint on working memory in pretrained LLMs, suggesting that working-memory limits in biological and artificial systems may reflect a shared computational challenge: selecting task-relevant information under interference.

</details>


### [20] [Belief-State RWKV for Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09671)
*Liu Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于RWKV循环序列模型的强化学习新框架，通过将固定大小的循环状态显式解释为信念状态（belief state），在部分可观测环境中提升了策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对普通固定状态策略在部分可观测环境中的关键缺陷：虽然能够存储证据，但无法有效表达置信度，导致在不确定环境中的决策能力受限。

Method: 在RWKV风格的循环模型基础上，构建紧凑的不确定性感知状态b_t = (μ_t, Σ_t)，将策略和价值函数同时依赖于记忆状态和不确定性估计，而非单一隐藏向量h_t。

Result: 在隐藏的回合级观测噪声实验中，信念状态策略整体表现接近最佳循环基线，在最难的分布内环境和分布外噪声偏移下略有提升；消融实验表明简单信念读出优于门控记忆控制和特权信念目标等结构化扩展。

Conclusion: 显式建模不确定性感知的信念状态是提升部分可观测环境下循环策略性能的有效途径，但当前需要更丰富的基准测试来充分评估更复杂的结构化扩展方法。

Abstract: We propose a stronger formulation of RL on top of RWKV-style recurrent sequence models, in which the fixed-size recurrent state is explicitly interpreted as a belief state rather than an opaque hidden vector. Instead of conditioning policy and value on a single summary h_t, we maintain a compact uncertainty-aware state b_t = (μ_t, Σ_t) derived from RWKV-style recurrent statistics and let control depend on both memory and uncertainty. This design targets a key weakness of plain fixed-state policies in partially observed settings: they may store evidence, but not necessarily confidence. We present the method, a theoretical program, and a pilot RL experiment with hidden episode-level observation noise together with a test-time noise sweep. The pilot shows that belief-state policies nearly match the best recurrent baseline overall while slightly improving return on the hardest in-distribution regime and under a held-out noise shift. Additional ablations show that this simple belief readout is currently stronger than two more structured extensions, namely gated memory control and privileged belief targets, underscoring the need for richer benchmarks.

</details>


### [21] [Active Inference with a Self-Prior in the Mirror-Mark Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09673)
*Dongmin Kim,Hoshinori Kanazawa,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于“自我先验”的计算模型，通过主动推断机制，使模拟婴儿在无外部奖励的情况下自发完成镜子自我识别测试，为自我意识的发展起源提供了统一的计算解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜子自我识别测试广泛用作自我意识的指标，但相关的计算机制尚不清楚。本研究旨在探索一种简洁的计算模型，解释该行为如何在无外部奖励的情况下自发涌现。

Method: 采用Transformer实现“自我先验”，学习熟悉的多感官经验密度；通过主动推断，当新异标记出现时，与已学习分布的差异驱动标记导向行为。模拟婴儿仅依赖视觉和本体感觉（无触觉输入），在无明确指令的情况下进行测试。

Result: 模拟婴儿在约70%的情况下发现并移除了放置在其面部的贴纸；移除贴纸后期望自由能显著降低，验证了自我先验作为区分自我与非自我的内部标准。跨模态采样进一步表明，自我先验捕获了视觉-本体感觉关联，作为概率性身体图式运作。

Conclusion: 该模型为镜子测试中观察到的关键行为提供了简洁的计算解释，表明自由能原理可作为研究自我意识发展起源的统一假设。

Abstract: The mirror self-recognition test evaluates whether a subject touches a mark on its own body that is visible only in a mirror, and is widely used as an indicator of self-awareness. In this study, we present a computational model in which this behavior emerges spontaneously through a single mechanism, the self-prior, without any external reward. The self-prior, implemented with a Transformer, learns the density of familiar multisensory experiences; when a novel mark appears, the discrepancy from this learned distribution drives mark-directed behavior through active inference. A simulated infant, relying solely on vision and proprioception without tactile input, discovered a sticker placed on its own face in the mirror and removed it in approximately 70% of cases without any explicit instruction. Expected free energy decreased significantly after sticker removal, confirming that the self-prior operates as an internal criterion for distinguishing self from non-self. Cross-modal sampling further demonstrated that the self-prior captures visual--proprioceptive associations, functioning as a probabilistic body schema. These results provide a concise computational account of the key behavior observed in the mirror test and suggest that the free energy principle can serve as a unifying hypothesis for investigating the developmental origins of self-awareness. Code is available at: https://github.com/kim135797531/self-prior-mirror

</details>


### [22] [A Comparative Theoretical Analysis of Entropy Control Methods in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09676)
*Ming Lei,Christophe Baehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了传统熵正则化与基于协方差的熵控制机制，发现传统方法因引入持续偏置导致策略次优，而协方差方法通过选择性正则化实现渐近无偏性，为LLM强化学习训练提供了更优的熵控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的关键方法，但在可扩展训练中，策略熵的快速坍塌问题导致训练过早收敛和性能饱和，严重阻碍了模型训练的有效性。现有熵控制策略缺乏统一的理论分析框架，亟需深入理解不同方法的本质差异及其对训练过程的影响。

Method: 在softmax参数化下建立了统一的熵动力学分析框架，证明熵变化由对数概率与logit更新之间的协方差所支配。基于此框架，对传统熵正则化和基于协方差的熵控制机制进行了系统的对比理论分析，从数学角度刻画了两种方法的作用机理和收敛性质。

Result: 理论分析揭示：传统熵正则化引入了稠密、持续的偏置，改变了平稳条件，导致策略收敛到次优解；而基于协方差的方法能够选择性地对高协方差token的稀疏子集进行正则化，当正则化系数退火时可实现渐近无偏性，从而获得更优的策略性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果为LLM后训练中的熵控制提供了原则性指导，协方差方法相比传统方法具有显著理论优势，对于将强化学习扩展到更大规模模型和更复杂推理任务具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key approach for enhancing reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet scalable training is often hindered by the rapid collapse of policy entropy, which leads to premature convergence and performance saturation. This paper provides a comparative theoretical analysis of two entropy control strategies: traditional entropy regularization and the recently proposed covariance-based mechanism. We establish a unified framework for entropy dynamics under softmax parameterization, showing that entropy change is governed by the covariance between log-probabilities and logit updates. Our analysis reveals that traditional entropy regularization introduces a dense, persistent bias that modifies the stationary condition, leading to suboptimal policies, while covariance-based methods selectively regularize a sparse subset of high-covariance tokens and achieve asymptotic unbiasedness when the regularization coefficient is annealed. These results provide principled guidelines for entropy control in LLM posttraining, with implications for scaling RL to larger models and more complex reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [23] [STaR-DRO: Stateful Tsallis Reweighting for Group-Robust Structured Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09737)
*Samah Fodeh,Ganesh Puthiaraju,Elyas Irankhah,Linhai Ma,Srivani Talakokkul,Afshan Khan,Sreeraj Ramachandran,Jordan Alpert,Sarah Schellhorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段的框架来提升结构化预测的鲁棒性：通过任务无关的提示策略解决上下文生成中的格式漂移和证据幻觉问题；引入STaR-DRO优化方法处理组异质性，在Llama模型上实现了显著的F1提升，特别是在困难临床类别上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化预测在医疗文本分析中面临多重挑战：需要生成本体约束标签、有据可依的证据和有效结构，同时还要应对歧义性、标签偏斜和异质组难度问题。现有方法在上下文结构化生成中存在格式漂移、标签歧义、证据幻觉和元数据条件混淆等缺陷，且传统优化方法难以有效处理组异质性，容易产生不稳定的权重更新和不必要的损失。这些限制影响了患者-提供者安全消息的可靠信息提取。

Method: 提出两部分框架：第一部分是任务无关的提示策略，结合基于XML的指令结构、消歧规则、验证式推理、模式约束和自我验证，通过结构化提示解决生成过程中的多重问题。第二部分是STaR-DRO状态鲁棒优化方法，结合Tsallis镜像下降与动量平滑的中心化组损失信号和有界超额乘数器，仅对持续困难的组进行加权，避免密集指数梯度重加权的波动性和简单组的损失。在EPPC Miner基准上评估，该基准用于从患者-提供者安全消息中提取层次标签和证据跨度。

Result: 提示工程在四个Llama模型上使Code、Sub-code和Span的平均F1零样本提升+15.44。在监督微调基础上，STaR-DRO进一步改善困难语义决策：在Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct上，Code F1从79.24提升至81.47，Sub-code F1从67.78提升至69.30，同时保持Span性能，在最困难的临床类别上将组级验证交叉熵降低高达29.6%。这些改进直接增强了患者中心护理分析中的通信挖掘可靠性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了结构化预测中的关键挑战，通过创新提示策略和鲁棒优化方法的结合，显著提升了医疗文本信息提取的准确性和可靠性。由于罕见和困难组对应临床重要的通信行为，这些性能提升不仅是统计改进，更直接支持患者中心护理分析的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Structured prediction requires models to generate ontology-constrained labels, grounded evidence, and valid structure under ambiguity, label skew, and heterogeneous group difficulty. We present a two-part framework for controllable inference and robust fine-tuning. First, we introduce a task-agnostic prompting strategy that combines XML-based instruction structure, disambiguation rules, verification-style reasoning, schema constraints, and self-validation to address format drift, label ambiguity, evidence hallucination, and metadata-conditioned confusion in in-context structured generation. Second, we introduce STaR-DRO, a stateful robust optimization method for group heterogeneity. It combines Tsallis mirror descent with momentum-smoothed, centered group-loss signals and bounded excess-only multipliers so that only persistently hard groups above a neutral baseline are upweighted, concentrating learning where it is most needed while avoiding volatile, dense exponentiated-gradient reweighting and unnecessary loss from downweighting easier groups. We evaluate the combined framework on EPPC Miner, a benchmark for extracting hierarchical labels and evidence spans from patient-provider secure messages. Prompt engineering improves zero-shot by +15.44 average F1 across Code, Sub-code, and Span over four Llama models. Building on supervised fine-tuning, STaR-DRO further improves the hardest semantic decisions: on Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct, Code F1 rises from 79.24 to 81.47 and Sub-code F1 from 67.78 to 69.30, while preserving Span performance and reducing group-wise validation cross-entropy by up to 29.6% on the most difficult clinical categories. Because these rare and difficult groups correspond to clinically consequential communication behaviors, these gains are not merely statistical improvements: they directly strengthen communication mining reliability for patient-centered care analysis.

</details>


### [24] [ExecTune: Effective Steering of Black-Box LLMs with Guide Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09741)
*Vijay Lingam,Aditya Golatkar,Anwesan Pal,Ben Vo,Narayanan Sadagopan,Alessandro Achille,Jun Huan,Anoop Deoras,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Guide-Core Policies (GCoP)框架及其训练方法ExecTune，通过优化策略的可执行性，在数学推理和代码生成任务上实现最高9.2%的精度提升和22.4%的推理成本降低，使Claude Haiku 3.5超越Sonnet 3.5并接近Sonnet 4性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过黑盒API部署时，持续的推理成本往往超过一次性训练成本。现有组合式智能体系统虽然可以将昂贵的推理过程摊销为可复用的中间表示，但现有方法在部署约束下往往无法优化策略的可执行性，导致策略脆弱且计算效率低下。

Method: 提出Guide-Core Policies (GCoP)框架，其中引导模型生成结构化策略，由黑盒核心模型执行。在此基础上提出ExecTune训练方案，结合教师引导的接受采样、监督微调和结构感知强化学习，直接优化语法有效性、执行成功率和成本效率。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试中，结合ExecTune的GCoP相比先前最优基线精度提升高达9.2%，推理成本降低高达22.4%。使Claude Haiku 3.5在数学和代码任务上超越Sonnet 3.5，并以低38%的成本达到Sonnet 4的1.7%绝对精度范围内。

Conclusion: GCoP框架通过优化引导模型平均可执行性，有效解决了黑盒API部署场景下的推理成本问题。ExecTune训练方法通过直接优化策略的可执行性和成本效率，实现了性能与成本的双重提升，同时支持模块化适应（仅更新引导模型而无需重新训练核心模型）。

Abstract: For large language models deployed through black-box APIs, recurring inference costs often exceed one-time training costs. This motivates composed agentic systems that amortize expensive reasoning into reusable intermediate representations. We study a broad class of such systems, termed Guide-Core Policies (GCoP), in which a guide model generates a structured strategy that is executed by a black-box core model. This abstraction subsumes base, supervised, and advisor-style approaches, which differ primarily in how the guide is trained. We formalize GCoP under a cost-sensitive utility objective and show that end-to-end performance is governed by guide-averaged executability: the probability that a strategy generated by the guide can be faithfully executed by the core. Our analysis shows that existing GCoP instantiations often fail to optimize executability under deployment constraints, resulting in brittle strategies and inefficient computation. Motivated by these insights, we propose ExecTune, a principled training recipe that combines teacher-guided acceptance sampling, supervised fine-tuning, and structure-aware reinforcement learning to directly optimize syntactic validity, execution success, and cost efficiency. Across mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, GCoP with ExecTune improves accuracy by up to 9.2% over prior state-of-the-art baselines while reducing inference cost by up to 22.4%. It enables Claude Haiku 3.5 to outperform Sonnet 3.5 on both math and code tasks, and to come within 1.7% absolute accuracy of Sonnet 4 at 38% lower cost. Beyond efficiency, GCoP also supports modular adaptation by updating the guide without retraining the core.

</details>


### [25] [Efficient Matrix Implementation for Rotary Position Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09742)
*Chen Minqi,Zhongqi Yue,Shihao Zhang,Yun Xu,Peng Wu,kaixiang Xu,Zeyi Huang,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RoME（旋转矩阵位置嵌入），通过将RoPE的向量操作重构为统一的矩阵变换，在保持数学等价性的同时显著提升了计算效率和硬件利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）实现依赖向量级的拆分与合并操作，引入了不可忽视的计算开销，且在多维场景（如2D/3D RoPE）中，额外的向量操作和不均匀的特征分区进一步降低了硬件利用率，这一问题在现代NPU上尤为突出。

Method: 提出RoME方法，将RoPE的向量操作替换为统一的矩阵变换，消除了维度特定的操作，简化了实现，并支持在现代NPU的Cube和Vector单元上进行融合并行执行。

Result: 实验表明，RoME在算子层面和全模型层面均实现了显著的加速效果，同时与原RoPE在数学上完全等价。代码已开源。

Conclusion: RoME通过矩阵化重构有效解决了RoPE的计算瓶颈，为语言、视觉和3D领域的Transformer模型提供了一种高效且易于实现的位置编码方案。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) has become a core component of modern Transformer architectures across language, vision, and 3D domains. However, existing implementations rely on vector-level split and merge operations that introduce non-negligible computational overhead, often overlooked in attention optimization. The problem is further amplified in multi-dimensional settings (e.g., 2D and 3D RoPE), where additional vector operations and uneven feature partitions degrade hardware utilization. To overcome these limitations, we propose RoME (Rotary Matrix position Embedding), a mathematically equivalent yet computationally efficient reformulation of RoPE that replaces vector operations with unified matrix transformations. RoME eliminates dimension-specific operations, simplifies implementation, and enables fused parallel execution across Cube and Vector units on modern NPUs. Experiments show that RoME delivers substantial acceleration at both the operator and full-model levels. The implementation is available at https://gitcode.com/cann/ops-transformer/blob/master/experimental/posembedding/rope_matrix/README.md.

</details>


### [26] [Explainable Human Activity Recognition: A Unified Review of Concepts and Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09799)
*Mainak Kundu,Catherine Chen,Rifatul Islam,Ismail Uysal,Ria Kanjilal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对可解释人体活动识别（XAI-HAR）方法进行了全面综述，提出了以机制为中心的分类体系，涵盖可穿戴、环境、生理和多模态感知场景，分析了现有方法的解释目标与局限性，并指出了未来构建可信HAR系统的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习虽然显著提升了人体活动识别（HAR）的性能，但模型通常是不透明的，限制了其在医疗监护、辅助生活等关键领域的信任度和实际部署。因此，可解释人工智能（XAI）成为使HAR系统更加透明和以人为本的关键研究方向。

Method: 提出了一种将可解释性的概念维度与算法解释机制分离的统一视角，以此减少以往综述中的歧义；基于此构建了一个以机制为中心的XAI-HAR方法分类体系，涵盖主要的解释范式。该综述涵盖了可穿戴、环境、生理及多模态感知场景，并审视了这些方法如何处理HAR的时间、多模态和语义复杂性。

Result: 总结了现有XAI-HAR方法的可解释性目标、解释对象及其局限性；分析了当前的评价实践；探讨了在实现可靠且可部署的XAI-HAR过程中面临的关键挑战，并提出了未来的发展方向。

Conclusion: 本文为构建能够更好支持人类理解和决策的可信活动识别系统提供了理论框架与实践指导，指明了未来研究应致力于解决可靠性与部署性问题，推动XAI-HAR的实际应用。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) has become a key component of intelligent systems for healthcare monitoring, assistive living, smart environments, and human-computer interaction. Although deep learning has substantially improved HAR performance on multivariate sensor data, the resulting models often remain opaque, limiting trust, reliability, and real-world deployment. Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) has therefore emerged as a critical direction for making HAR systems more transparent and human-centered. This paper presents a comprehensive review of explainable HAR methods across wearable, ambient, physiological, and multimodal sensing settings. We introduce a unified perspective that separates conceptual dimensions of explainability from algorithmic explanation mechanisms, reducing ambiguities in prior surveys. Building on this distinction, we present a mechanism-centric taxonomy of XAI-HAR methods covering major explanation paradigms. The review examines how these methods address the temporal, multimodal, and semantic complexities of HAR, and summarize their interpretability objectives, explanation targets, and limitations. In addition, we discuss current evaluation practices, highlight key challenges in achieving reliable and deployable XAI-HAR, and outline directions toward trustworthy activity recognition systems that better support human understanding and decision-making.

</details>


### [27] [NeuroFlow: Toward Unified Visual Encoding and Decoding from Neural Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09817)
*Weijian Mai,Mu Nan,Yu Zhu,Jiahang Cao,Rui Zhang,Yuqin Dai,Chunfeng Song,Andrew F. Luo,Jiamin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了NeuroFlow，首个将视觉编码和解码统一在单一流模型中的框架，通过NeuroVAE和跨模态流匹配技术实现了更高效、性能更优的双向建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉编码模型（从刺激预测脑活动）和解码模型（从脑活动重建刺激）被视为独立任务，需要分别训练，这种方式效率低下且无法建模编码与解码过程之间的一致性。

Method: 提出了NeuroFlow统一框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) NeuroVAE作为变分骨干网络，建模神经变异性并建立紧凑、语义结构化的潜在空间；(2) 跨模态流匹配(XFM)，学习视觉和神经潜在分布之间可逆一致的流模型，而非传统的噪声到数据的扩散范式。首次将编码和解码重构为共享潜在空间中时间相关的可逆过程。

Result: 实验结果表明，与任何独立方法相比，NeuroFlow在视觉编码和解码任务中实现了更优的整体性能和更高的计算效率。分析发现了驱动模型实现编码-解码一致性的关键因素，并通过脑功能分析证明NeuroFlow捕获了神经变异性背后的一致激活模式。

Conclusion: NeuroFlow标志着从神经活动进行统一视觉编码和解码的重要进展，为未来双向视觉脑机接口提供了机制性见解，证明了统一建模框架的优越性和实用价值。

Abstract: Visual encoding and decoding models act as gateways to understanding the neural mechanisms underlying human visual perception. Typically, visual encoding models that predict brain activity from stimuli and decoding models that reproduce stimuli from brain activity are treated as distinct tasks, requiring separate models and training procedures. This separation is inefficient and fails to model the consistency between encoding and decoding processes. To address this limitation, we propose NeuroFlow, the first unified framework that jointly models visual encoding and decoding from neural activity within a single flow model. NeuroFlow introduces two key components: (1) NeuroVAE is designed as a variational backbone to model neural variability and establish a compact, semantically structured latent space for bidirectional modeling across visual and neural modalities. (2) Cross-modal Flow Matching (XFM) bypasses the typical paradigm of noise-to-data diffusion guided by a specific modality condition, instead learning a reversibly consistent flow model between visual and neural latent distributions. For the first time, visual encoding and decoding are reformulated as a time-dependent, reversible process within a shared latent space for unified modeling. Empirical results demonstrate that NeuroFlow achieves superior overall performance in visual encoding and decoding tasks with higher computational efficiency compared to any isolated methods. We further analyze principal factors that steer the model toward encoding-decoding consistency and, through brain functional analyses, demonstrate that NeuroFlow captures consistent activation patterns underlying neural variability. NeuroFlow marks a major step toward unified visual encoding and decoding from neural activity, providing mechanistic insights that inform future bidirectional visual brain-computer interfaces.

</details>


### [28] [Below-ground Fungal Biodiversity Can be Monitored Using Self-Supervised Learning Satellite Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09818)
*Robin Young,Michael E. Van Nuland,E. Toby Kiers,Tomáš Větrovský,Petr Kohout,Petr Baldrian,Srinivasan Keshav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用自监督学习（SSL）处理卫星图像，成功预测了地下外生菌根真菌的丰富度。该方法将空间分辨率提高了10,000倍（从1km提升至10m），并首次实现了对地下生物多样性的动态监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菌根真菌对陆地生态系统功能至关重要，但因其位于地下，在大尺度上监测其生物多样性往往因时间和成本限制而不可行。据预测，90%的菌根真菌多样性热点地区缺乏保护，因此迫切需要一种能够广泛且有效绘制地下真菌群落分布的方法。

Method: 研究人员将自监督学习（SSL）应用于卫星图像，提取特征以预测地下外生菌根真菌的丰富度。模型使用了横跨欧洲和亚洲的约12,000个野外样本进行训练和验证，并对比了SSL特征与传统气候、土壤和土地覆盖数据集的预测能力。

Result: 该模型解释了跨越欧洲和亚洲约12,000个实地样本中物种丰富度超过一半的方差。SSL特征被证明是最具信息量的单一预测因子，涵盖了气候、土壤和土地覆盖数据集中包含的大部分信息。该方法实现了比现有技术高10,000倍的空间分辨率（从1km景观平均值提升至10m生境尺度），且几乎无系统性偏差。对英国国家公园林地的研究发现，古老森林的外生菌根真菌多样性可能正在以不成比例的速度丧失。

Conclusion: 研究确立了SSL卫星特征作为一种可扩展工具的地位，能够将稀疏的实地观测扩展为连续、高分辨率的生物多样性地图。这使得在景观尺度上动态监测地下生物多样性成为可能，为保护陆地生态系统中“看不见的一半”提供了关键技术支持。

Abstract: Mycorrhizal fungi are vital to terrestrial ecosystem functioning. Yet monitoring their biodiversity at landscape scales is often unfeasible due to time and cost constraints. Current predictions suggest that 90\% of mycorrhizal diversity hotspots remain unprotected, opening questions of how to broadly and effectively map underground fungal communities. Here, we show that self-supervised learning (SSL) applied to satellite imagery can predict below-ground ectomycorrhizal fungal richness across diverse environments. Our models explain over half the variance in species richness across ~12,000 field samples spanning Europe and Asia. SSL-derived features prove to be the single most informative predictor, subsuming the majority of information contained in climate, soil, and land cover datasets. Using this approach, we achieve a 10,000-fold increase in spatial resolution over existing techniques, moving from 1km landscape averages to 10m habitat-scale observations with nearly no systematic bias. As satellite observations are dynamic rather than static, this enables temporal monitoring of below-ground biodiversity at landscape scales for the first time. We analyze multi-year trends in predicted fungal richness across UK National Park woodlands, finding that ancient forests may be losing ectomycorrhizal diversity at disproportionate rates. These results establish SSL satellite features as a scalable tool for extending sparse field observations to continuous, high-resolution biodiversity maps for monitoring the invisible half of terrestrial ecosystems.

</details>


### [29] [Relational Preference Encoding in Looped Transformer Internal States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09870)
*Jan Kirin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现循环Transformer通过关系编码方式表示人类偏好，轻量级评估头在成对偏好预测上达到95.2%准确率，而独立评估准确率最高仅70%，揭示了模型内部一致性探测的本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究循环Transformer如何在其内部迭代状态中编码人类偏好，理解此类模型的表示学习机制。

Method: 使用2.6B参数的循环Transformer模型Ouro-2.6B-Thinking，从每次循环迭代中提取隐藏状态，训练约5M参数的轻量级评估头来预测Anthropic HH-RLHF数据集上的人类偏好，同时保持基础模型完全冻结。

Result: 成对评估器在8,552个未见样本上达到95.2%测试准确率，超越全批次L-BFGS探针(84.5%)；成对差异的线性探针达84.5%，最优非线性独立评估器仅65%，线性独立分类仅21.75%（低于随机）；独立评分的真实上限为70%；50%参数交换协议使成对训练指标峰值下降约31个百分点。

Conclusion: 循环状态主要以关系方式编码偏好，评估器实质上是模型内部一致性探针，测量模型自身学习的价值系统如何稳定地组织其表示，而非预测嘈杂的人类标注；提出翻转测试作为成对偏好评估器的必要诊断工具。

Abstract: We investigate how looped transformers encode human preference in their internal iteration states. Using Ouro-2.6B-Thinking, a 2.6B-parameter looped transformer with iterative refinement, we extract hidden states from each loop iteration and train lightweight evaluator heads (~5M parameters) to predict human preference on the Anthropic HH-RLHF dataset. Our pairwise evaluator achieves 95.2% test accuracy on 8,552 unseen examples, surpassing a full-batch L-BFGS probe (84.5%) while the base model remains completely frozen.
  Our central finding is that loop states encode preference predominantly relationally: a linear probe on pairwise differences achieves 84.5%, the best nonlinear independent evaluator reaches only 65% test accuracy, and linear independent classification scores 21.75%, below chance and with inverted polarity. Interpreted precisely, the evaluator functions as a model-internal consistency probe, measuring how stably Ouro's own learned value system organizes its representations rather than how well it predicts noisy human annotations.
  We also document a systematic architecture search that established a genuine 70% ceiling for independent scoring, and show how the 50% argument-swap protocol required to prevent degenerate pairwise solutions deflated pairwise training metrics by about 31 points at peak, creating the false appearance that pairwise and pointwise evaluators shared the same ceiling.
  Finally, we show that a cosine learning-rate dead zone at epoch 2 accidentally acted as early stopping, preserving the generalization peak before overfitting degraded test accuracy from 95.2% to 62.4% by epoch 5. Cross-epoch flip-test analysis shows that antisymmetry correlation remains stable while strict sign-flip rate mainly tracks scorer bias. We propose the flip test as a mandatory diagnostic for pairwise preference evaluators.

</details>


### [30] [Efficient Personalization of Generative User Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09876)
*Yi-Hao Peng,Samarth Das,Jeffrey P. Bigham,Jason Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了UI设计偏好存在高度主观性，提出了一种基于已有设计师偏好的高效个性化方法，在偏好预测和界面生成任务上均优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式用户界面虽然带来了个性化适配的机会，但个性化仍面临巨大挑战，因为理想的UI属性具有主观性、难以清晰表述，且从稀疏反馈中推断的成本很高。作者希望通过构建新数据集来直接分析设计偏好的差异，从而解决个性化难题。

Method: 1. 构建数据集：让20位受过训练的设计师对相同的600个生成UI进行成对比较判断。
2. 分析分歧：计算设计师间的一致性（Kappa值），并分析书面理由以理解概念定义和应用的差异。
3. 提出模型：开发一种样本高效的个性化方法，不再使用固定的设计概念标准，而是通过已有设计师的偏好来表征新用户。

Result: 1. 偏好分歧显著：设计师间平均Kappa值仅为0.25，即便使用相似概念（如层次感、整洁度），其定义、优先级和应用方式也存在差异。
2. 模型性能优越：所提出的偏好模型在技术评估中优于预训练的UI评估器和更大的多模态模型，且随反馈增加扩展性更好。
3. 用户验证成功：在个性化生成任务中，12位新设计师更偏好该方法生成的界面，优于包括直接用户提示在内的基线方法。

Conclusion: 轻量级的偏好引导可以作为个性化生成式UI系统的实用基础，该方法有效解决了设计偏好的主观性和稀疏反馈问题，为未来个性化界面设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative user interfaces (UIs) create new opportunities to adapt interfaces to individual users on demand, but personalization remains difficult because desirable UI properties are subjective, hard to articulate, and costly to infer from sparse feedback. We study this problem through a new dataset in which 20 trained designers each provide pairwise judgments over the same 600 generated UIs, enabling direct analysis of preference divergence. We find substantial disagreement across designers (average kappa = 0.25), and written rationales reveal that even when designers appeal to similar concepts such as hierarchy or cleanliness, designers differ in how they define, prioritize, and apply those concepts. Motivated by these findings, we develop a sample-efficient personalization method that represents a new user in terms of prior designers rather than a fixed rubric of design concepts. In a technical evaluation, our preference model outperforms both a pretrained UI evaluator and a larger multimodal model, and scales better with additional feedback. When used to personalize generation, it also produces interfaces preferred by 12 new designers over baseline approaches, including direct user prompting. Our findings suggest that lightweight preference elicitation can serve as a practical foundation for personalized generative UI systems.

</details>


### [31] [SemEnrich: Self-Supervised Semantic Enrichment of Radiology Reports for Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09887)
*Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督数据增强方法，通过语义聚类丰富医学报告中的发现，显著提升了下游任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉-语言数据集通常规模有限，且由于临床医生倾向于报告异常而忽略阳性/中性发现，导致数据存在负向偏差。

Method: 利用报告语句的语义聚类，以自监督方式从不同聚类中添加阳性/中性观察结果来丰富训练集中的医学报告发现；此外，还将语义聚类信息融入GRPO训练的奖励设计中。

Result: 在监督微调中取得了显著提升（COMET、Bert score、Sentence Bleu、CheXbert-F1和RadGraph-F1分别平均提升5.63%、3.04%、7.40%、5.30%和7.47%）；在GRPO训练中进一步提升了性能（COMET、Bert score和Sentence Bleu分别平均提升2.78%、3.14%和12.80%）。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效缓解医学数据集的偏差问题，通过语义聚类驱动的数据增强和奖励设计显著提升了模型性能，消融实验证实了语义聚类的重要性。

Abstract: Medical vision-language datasets are often limited in size and biased toward negative findings, as clinicians report abnormalities mostly but might omit some positive/neutral findings because they might be considered as irrelevant to the patient's condition. We propose a self-supervised data enrichment method that leverages semantic clustering of report sentences. Then we enrich the findings in the medical reports in the training set by adding positive/neutral observations from different clusters in a self-supervised manner. Our approach yields consistent gains in supervised fine-tuning (5.63%, 3.04%, 7.40%, 5.30%, 7.47% average gains on COMET score, Bert score, Sentence Bleu, CheXbert-F1 and RadGraph-F1 scores respectively). Ablation studies confirm that improvements stem from semantic clustering rather than random augmentation. Furthermore, we introduce a way to incorporate semantic cluster information into the reward design for GRPO training, which leads to further performance gains (2.78%, 3.14%, 12.80% average gains on COMET score, Bert score and Sentence Bleu scores respectively). We share our code at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SemEnrich-75CF

</details>


### [32] [Improving Pediatric Emergency Department Triage with Modality Dropout in Late Fusion Multimodal EHR Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09905)
*Tyler Yang,Romal Mitr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合XGBoost和Bio_ClinicalBERT的晚期融合多模态架构，并通过对称模态Dropout技术有效缓解了模态坍塌问题，实现了成人数据训练模型向儿科患者的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的急诊科分诊多模态预测模型存在“模态坍塌”问题，即过度依赖结构化表格数据而忽视临床文本，这严重限制了模型在不同人口统计学群体（特别是儿科患者）间的泛化能力，因为儿科患者的生命体征存在发育差异，临床叙述尤为关键。

Method: 提出一种晚期融合多模态架构：使用XGBoost处理表格型生命体征数据，使用Bio_ClinicalBERT处理非结构化临床文本，通过逻辑回归元分类器进行融合以预测5级急诊严重程度指数。研究仅使用成人数据（MIMIC-IV和NHAMCS数据集）进行训练，并在儿科队列上进行零样本评估；同时引入对称模态Dropout作为正则化手段，防止模型过拟合于成人特定的临床关联。

Result: 该多模态框架显著优于单模态基线模型。最关键的发现是，在训练中应用30-40%的对称模态Dropout率，使模型在未见的儿科队列上取得了显著的性能提升，二次加权Kappa值达到0.351。

Conclusion: 模态Dropout是一种关键的正则化技术，能有效缓解模态坍塌问题，显著增强临床AI模型的跨人口统计学泛化能力，对于解决外部有效性问题具有重要意义。

Abstract: Emergency department triage relies heavily on both quantitative vital signs and qualitative clinical notes, yet multimodal machine learning models predicting triage acuity often suffer from modality collapse by over-relying on structured tabular data. This limitation severely hinders demographic generalizability, particularly for pediatric patients where developmental variations in vital signs make unstructured clinical narratives uniquely crucial. To address this gap, we propose a late-fusion multimodal architecture that processes tabular vitals via XGBoost and unstructured clinical text via Bio_ClinicalBERT, combined through a Logistic Regression meta-classifier to predict the 5-level Emergency Severity Index. To explicitly target the external validity problem, we train our model exclusively on adult encounters from the MIMIC-IV and NHAMCS datasets and evaluate its zero-shot generalization on a traditionally overlooked pediatric cohort. Furthermore, we employ symmetric modality dropout during training to prevent the ensemble from overfitting to adult-specific clinical correlations. Our results demonstrate that the multimodal framework significantly outperforms single-modality baselines. Most notably, applying a 30-40% symmetric modality dropout rate yielded steep performance improvements in the unseen pediatric cohort, elevating the Quadratic Weighted Kappa to 0.351. These findings highlight modality dropout as a critical regularization technique for mitigating modality collapse and enhancing cross-demographic generalization in clinical AI.

</details>


### [33] [Last-Iterate Convergence of Randomized Kaczmarz and SGD with Greedy Step Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09909)
*Michał Dereziński,Xiaoyu Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将光滑二次插值设置下SGD贪婪步长的最后迭代收敛速率从O(1/t^{1/2})改进至O(1/t^{3/4})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（Attia等人）在光滑二次插值设置下仅能证明SGD贪婪步长具有O(1/t^{1/2})的收敛保证，作者旨在解决其提出的开放问题，探索能否获得更快的最后迭代收敛速率。

Method: 引入了随机收缩过程族，其行为可由特定确定性特征值方程的演化描述；通过精细的离散到连续归约方法对该方程进行分析，从而建立收敛理论。

Result: 证明了第t次迭代可达到O(1/t^{3/4})的收敛速率，较已有结果O(1/t^{1/2})获得显著提升。该方法适用于Randomized Kaczmarz算法及多种流行的迭代线性系统求解器。

Conclusion: 通过引入随机收缩过程框架与离散-连续归约技术，成功改进了SGD贪婪步长在光滑二次插值设置下的最后迭代收敛速率，为相关迭代算法的理论分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study last-iterate convergence of SGD with greedy step size over smooth quadratics in the interpolation regime, a setting which captures the classical Randomized Kaczmarz algorithm as well as other popular iterative linear system solvers. For these methods, we show that the $t$-th iterate attains an $O(1/t^{3/4})$ convergence rate, addressing a question posed by Attia, Schliserman, Sherman, and Koren, who gave an $O(1/t^{1/2})$ guarantee for this setting. In the proof, we introduce the family of stochastic contraction processes, whose behavior can be described by the evolution of a certain deterministic eigenvalue equation, which we analyze via a careful discrete-to-continuous reduction.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种改进策略（REINA-SAN和REINA-TAN）以解决同声传译中信息增益策略缺乏时间上下文的问题，在Whisper模型上将流式效率提升高达7.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于信息增益的同声传译策略（REINA）缺乏时间上下文感知，导致策略倾向于读取大部分音频后才开始翻译（写入），无法有效平衡翻译质量与低延迟需求。

Method: 提出了两种改进策略：1) REINA-SAN（监督对齐网络），引入监督对齐机制；2) REINA-TAN（时间步增强网络），增强时间步信息。两种方法均旨在为策略提供时间上下文信息。

Result: 两种方法均显著优于基线并解决了稳定性问题。REINA-TAN在流式效率帕累托前沿上表现略优，REINA-SAN在应对"读取循环"问题上更具鲁棒性。应用于Whisper时，两种方法在标准化流式效率（NoSE）评分上较现有竞争基线提升高达7.1%。

Conclusion: 通过引入时间上下文信息（无论是监督对齐还是时间步增强），可以有效解决信息增益策略的时序偏差问题，实现翻译质量与延迟的更好平衡，为同声传译系统提供了实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Simultaneous Speech Translation (SimulST) requires balancing high translation quality with low latency. Recent work introduced REINA, a method that trains a Read/Write policy based on estimating the information gain of reading more audio. However, we find that information-based policies often lack temporal context, leading the policy to bias itself toward reading most of the audio before starting to write. We improve REINA using two distinct strategies: a supervised alignment network (REINA-SAN) and a timestep-augmented network (REINA-TAN). Our results demonstrate that while both methods significantly outperform the baseline and resolve stability issues, REINA-TAN provides a slightly superior Pareto frontier for streaming efficiency, whereas REINA-SAN offers more robustness against 'read loops'. Applied to Whisper, both methods improve the pareto frontier of streaming efficiency as measured by Normalized Streaming Efficiency (NoSE) scores up to 7.1% over existing competitive baselines.

</details>


### [35] [A Tale of Two Temperatures: Simple, Efficient, and Diverse Sampling from Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09921)
*Theo X. Olausson,Metod Jazbec,Xi Wang,Armando Solar-Lezama,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单有效的温度化重掩码（tempered remasking）启发式方法，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了扩散语言模型采样结果的多样性，缩小了与自回归模型在探索能力上的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型（dLLMs）采样研究主要关注单个样本的速度与质量权衡，而对如何确保样本间的多样性关注不足。作者希望在不牺牲计算效率的前提下，解决现有基于置信度的重掩码方法多样性不足的问题。

Method: 作者引入了一个关于“分叉token”（fork tokens）的理想化形式模型，通过分析重掩码对分叉点期望熵的影响来指导方法设计。基于此，提出了使用软化、温度化版本的置信度重掩码启发式策略，该方法实现简单且保留了原有的计算优势。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的温度化启发式方法有效地缩小了现有基于置信度的采样与自回归采样之间的探索差距（pass@k指标）。在控制计算成本（pass@NFE）的情况下，该方法优于现有的置信度方法和自回归采样。此外，研究还验证了多样性的提升对下游训练后处理和测试时计算扩展的积极影响。

Conclusion: 研究发现，从扩散语言模型中进行简单、高效且多样化的采样是完全可行的。通过调整重掩码策略的温度参数，可以在保持计算效率的同时有效提升样本多样性，为dLLMs的实际应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Much work has been done on designing fast and accurate sampling for diffusion language models (dLLMs). However, these efforts have largely focused on the tradeoff between speed and quality of individual samples; how to additionally ensure diversity across samples remains less well understood. In this work, we show that diversity can be increased by using softened, tempered versions of familiar confidence-based remasking heuristics, retaining their computational benefits and offering simple implementations. We motivate this approach by introducing an idealized formal model of fork tokens and studying the impact of remasking on the expected entropy at the forks. Empirically, the proposed tempered heuristics close the exploration gap (pass@k) between existing confidence-based and autoregressive sampling, hence outperforming both when controlling for cost (pass@NFE). We further study how the increase in diversity translates to downstream post-training and test-time compute scaling. Overall, our findings demonstrate that simple, efficient, and diverse sampling from dLLMs is possible.

</details>


### [36] [K-STEMIT: Knowledge-Informed Spatio-Temporal Efficient Multi-Branch Graph Neural Network for Subsurface Stratigraphy Thickness Estimation from Radar Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09922)
*Zesheng Liu,Maryam Rahnemoonfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了K-STEMIT，一种融合物理先验知识的多分支时空图神经网络，用于从雷达图像中准确估计极地冰盖内部层厚度，相比现有方法RMSE降低了21.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于卷积神经网络的雷达图像分析方法对相干噪声和采集伪影敏感，且纯数据驱动方法未能充分利用物理知识，导致在时空外推时产生不合理的厚度估计结果。

Method: 开发了K-STEMIT模型，采用多分支架构：结合几何空间学习框架与时序卷积捕捉时间动态，并融合来自Model Atmospheric Regional物理天气模型的同步物理数据；引入自适应特征融合策略动态组合不同分支学习到的特征。

Result: 在知识引导和非知识引导设置下与最先进方法对比实验表明，K-STEMIT在保持近最优效率的同时实现了最高精度。引入自适应特征融合和物理先验后，RMSE降低21.01%，计算成本几乎无增加；在各年份相对MAE上持续表现更低。

Conclusion: K-STEMIT能够实现大空间区域内积雪累积变化的可靠、连续时空评估，验证了融合物理先验知识与自适应特征融合策略在冰盖层厚度估计任务中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Subsurface stratigraphy contains important spatio-temporal information about accumulation, deformation, and layer formation in polar ice sheets. In particular, variations in internal ice layer thickness provide valuable constraints for snow mass balance estimation and projections of ice sheet change. Although radar sensors can capture these layered structures as depth-resolved radargrams, convolutional neural networks applied directly to radar images are often sensitive to speckle noise and acquisition artifacts. In addition, purely data-driven methods may underuse physical knowledge, leading to unrealistic thickness estimates under spatial or temporal extrapolation. To address these challenges, we develop K-STEMIT, a novel knowledge-informed, efficient, multi-branch spatio-temporal graph neural network that combines a geometric framework for spatial learning with temporal convolution to capture temporal dynamics, and incorporates physical data synchronized from the Model Atmospheric Regional physical weather model. An adaptive feature fusion strategy is employed to dynamically combine features learned from different branches. Extensive experiments have been conducted to compare K-STEMIT against current state-of-the-art methods in both knowledge-informed and non-knowledge-informed settings, as well as other existing methods. Results show that K-STEMIT consistently achieves the highest accuracy while maintaining near-optimal efficiency. Most notably, incorporating adaptive feature fusion and physical priors reduces the root mean-squared error by 21.01% with negligible additional cost compared to its conventional multi-branch variants. Additionally, our proposed K-STEMIT achieves consistently lower per-year relative MAE, enabling reliable, continuous spatiotemporal assessment of snow accumulation variability across large spatial regions.

</details>


### [37] [A Hybrid Intelligent Framework for Uncertainty-Aware Condition Monitoring of Industrial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09932)
*Maryam Ahang,Todd Charter,Masoud Jalayer,Homayoun Najjaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合故障监测框架，通过融合传感器数据、时间特征和基于物理的残差，在特征级和模型级两种集成策略下均提升了故障诊断精度和预测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动方法在工业故障监测中缺乏可靠性。为了提高故障诊断的准确性并改善不确定性管理能力，作者希望结合数据驱动方法与物理模型知识。

Method: 提出了两种混合集成策略：1. 特征级融合方法，将物理残差和时间滞后特征扩展到输入空间；2. 模型级集成方法，在决策层组合不同特征类型训练的机器学习分类器。在全混流反应器（CSTR）基准上进行验证，并应用共形预测评估预测可靠性。

Result: 两种混合方法均比单一数据源基线提高了诊断准确率，最佳模型级集成相比最佳基线集成提升了2.9%的准确率。混合集成在相同覆盖率下产生了更小且校准更好的预测集，增强了不确定性管理能力。

Conclusion: 轻量级的物理残差、时间增强和集成学习可以有效结合，在非线性工业系统中同时提高故障诊断精度和决策可靠性。

Abstract: Hybrid approaches that combine data-driven learning with physics-based insight have shown promise for improving the reliability of industrial condition monitoring. This work develops a hybrid condition monitoring framework that integrates primary sensor measurements, lagged temporal features, and physics-informed residuals derived from nominal surrogate models.
