By evaluating the LF-based agentic HITL CPS, we identify practical challenges in reintroducing determinism into such agentic HITL CPS and present pathways to address them.

</details>


### [328] [A Mamba-Based Multimodal Network for Multiscale Blast-Induced Rapid Structural Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11709)
*Wanli Ma,Sivasakthy Selvakumaran,Dain G. Farrimond,Adam A. Dennis,Samuel E. Rigby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Mamba的多模态网络，通过融合多尺度爆炸荷载信息与光学遥感影像，实现了快速准确的结构损伤评估，在2020年贝鲁特爆炸案例中性能显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统现场勘察方法在灾后结构损伤评估中面临可达性差、安全风险高和时间成本大的限制，尤其是大规模爆炸事件后。现有基于机器学习的遥感方法虽然具有可扩展性，但依赖大量训练数据和标注，且忽视了爆炸荷载的物理特性，限制了其在实际场景中的应用效果。

Method: 提出一种基于Mamba架构的多模态网络，将多尺度爆炸荷载物理信息与光学遥感影像相结合，构建端到端的结构损伤评估框架。该方法充分利用爆炸荷载的物理特征，减少对大规模训练数据的依赖。

Result: 在2020年贝鲁特爆炸事件数据集上的实验表明，该方法在结构损伤评估任务上显著优于当前最先进的方法，验证了融合物理信息与遥感影像的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过整合爆炸荷载物理特性与多模态深度学习，所提方法实现了更快速、准确且实用的结构损伤评估，为灾后应急响应和救援决策提供了有效的技术支撑。

Abstract: Accurate and rapid structural damage assessment (SDA) is crucial for post-disaster management, helping responders prioritise resources, plan rescues, and support recovery. Traditional field inspections, though precise, are limited by accessibility, safety risks, and time constraints, especially after large explosions. Machine learning with remote sensing has emerged as a scalable solution for rapid SDA, with Mamba-based networks achieving state-of-the-art performance. However, these methods often require extensive training and large datasets, limiting real-world applicability. Moreover, they fail to incorporate key physical characteristics of blast loading for SDA. To overcome these challenges, we propose a Mamba-based multimodal network for rapid SDA that integrates multi-scale blast-loading information with optical remote sensing images. Evaluated on the 2020 Beirut explosion, our method significantly improves performance over state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at: https://github.com/IMPACTSquad/Blast-Mamba

</details>


### [329] [SWE-AGILE: A Software Agent Framework for Efficiently Managing Dynamic Reasoning Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11716)
*Shuquan Lian,Juncheng Liu,Yazhe Chen,Yuhong Chen,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SWE-AGILE框架，通过动态推理上下文策略（滑动窗口+推理摘要）解决SWE任务中保留完整推理历史导致上下文爆炸、丢弃则需冗余重推理的困境，在SWE-Bench-Verified上取得7B-8B模型最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReAct风格的SWE方法缺乏显式System-2推理能力；而直接应用扩展思维链的多轮推理面临两难：保留完整历史导致上下文爆炸和“中间迷失”问题，丢弃历史则迫使每步冗余重推理。

Method: 提出动态推理上下文策略，维护一个详细推理的滑动窗口以保证即时连续性、防止冗余重分析，同时将历史推理内容压缩为简洁的推理摘要，从而在推理深度、效率和上下文约束间取得平衡。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified基准上，仅使用2.2k轨迹和896任务，SWE-AGILE为7B-8B规模模型设立了新的性能标准。

Conclusion: SWE-AGILE成功弥合了推理深度、效率与上下文约束之间的差距，为自主软件工程任务提供了一种高效的推理框架方案。

Abstract: Prior representative ReAct-style approaches in autonomous Software Engineering (SWE) typically lack the explicit System-2 reasoning required for deep analysis and handling complex edge cases. While recent reasoning models demonstrate the potential of extended Chain-of-Thought (CoT), applying them to the multi-turn SWE task creates a fundamental dilemma: retaining full reasoning history leads to context explosion and ``Lost-in-the-Middle'' degradation, while discarding it would force the agent to redundantly re-reason at every step. To address these challenges, we propose SWE-AGILE, a novel software agent framework designed to bridge the gap between reasoning depth, efficiency, and context constraints. SWE-AGILE introduces a Dynamic Reasoning Context strategy, maintaining a ``sliding window'' of detailed reasoning for immediate continuity to prevent redundant re-analyzing, while compressing historical reasoning content into concise Reasoning Digests. Empirically, SWE-AGILE sets a new standard for 7B-8B models on SWE-Bench-Verified using only 2.2k trajectories and 896 tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/KDEGroup/SWE-AGILE.

</details>


### [330] [Collaborative Multi-Agent Scripts Generation for Enhancing Imperfect-Information Reasoning in Murder Mystery Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11741)
*Keyang Zhong,Junlin Xie,Hefeng Wu,Haofeng Li,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型在非完美信息多人博弈（如谋杀解谜游戏）中多跳推理能力不足的问题，提出了一个协作式多智能体框架，用于生成高质量角色驱动的游戏剧本，并通过两阶段训练策略（思维链微调与基于智能体奖励塑形的强化学习）显著提升了模型在叙事推理、隐藏事实提取和抗欺骗理解方面的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在感知任务上表现优异，但在具有非完美信息和欺骗性信息的复杂多人博弈环境中，其多跳推理能力会显著下降。为了解决这一挑战，本文选择了具有代表性的“谋杀解谜游戏”作为研究任务，该任务要求模型根据不同意图角色提供的部分线索推断隐藏的真相，这对模型的推理能力提出了更高要求。

Method: 本文提出了一个协作式多智能体框架，用于评估和合成高质量、角色驱动的多人游戏剧本，生成包含角色背景、视觉文本线索和多跳推理链的丰富多模态语境。设计了包含两个阶段的智能体监控训练策略：（1）基于思维链的微调，使用精心策划和合成的数据集来建模不确定性和欺骗性；（2）基于GRPO的强化学习，结合智能体监控的奖励塑形，鼓励模型发展角色特定的推理行为和有效的多模态多跳推理能力。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，该方法显著提升了视觉语言模型在叙事推理、隐藏事实提取和抗欺骗理解方面的性能。实验结果验证了该框架在不确定、对抗性和社会复杂性条件下训练和评估VLM的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为在不确定、对抗性和社会复杂条件下训练和评估视觉语言模型提供了一个可扩展的解决方案，为未来在非完美信息下进行多模态多跳推理的基准研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities in perceptual tasks, yet they degrade in complex multi-hop reasoning under multiplayer game settings with imperfect and deceptive information. In this paper, we study a representative multiplayer task, Murder Mystery Games, which require inferring hidden truths based on partial clues provided by roles with different intentions. To address this challenge, we propose a collaborative multi-agent framework for evaluating and synthesizing high-quality, role-driven multiplayer game scripts, enabling fine-grained interaction patterns tailored to character identities (i.e., murderer vs. innocent). Our system generates rich multimodal contexts, including character backstories, visual and textual clues, and multi-hop reasoning chains, through coordinated agent interactions. We design a two-stage agent-monitored training strategy to enhance the reasoning ability of VLMs: (1) chain-of-thought based fine-tuning on curated and synthetic datasets that model uncertainty and deception; (2) GRPO-based reinforcement learning with agent-monitored reward shaping, encouraging the model to develop character-specific reasoning behaviors and effective multimodal multi-hop inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly boosts the performance of VLMs in narrative reasoning, hidden fact extraction, and deception-resilient understanding. Our contributions offer a scalable solution for training and evaluating VLMs under uncertain, adversarial, and socially complex conditions, laying the groundwork for future benchmarks in multimodal multi-hop reasoning under imperfect information.

</details>


### [331] [Retrieval Is Not Enough: Why Organizational AI Needs Epistemic Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11759)
*Federico Bottino,Carlo Ferrero,Nicholas Dosio,Pierfrancesco Beneventano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OIDA框架，通过引入认知结构（承诺强度、矛盾状态、组织无知）提升组织AI的认知保真度，其QUESTION机制能识别组织未知事项并通过统计验证（p=0.0325）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织AI的知识检索系统缺乏认知结构，无法区分已确定的决策与被放弃的假设、有争议的主张与已解决的结论、已知事实与未解决问题，限制了组织AI的能力上限。

Method: 提出OIDA框架，将组织知识结构化为带有认知类别、重要性分数（含类别特定衰减）和有符号矛盾边的类型化知识对象。构建知识引力引擎以确定性维护分数并提供收敛性证明。引入QUESTION原语表示已建模的无知，具有反向衰减特性。提出认知质量评分（EQS）五组分评估方法。

Result: 在对照比较中（n=10），OIDA的RAG条件（3,868 tokens）EQS为0.530，全上下文基线（108,687 tokens）EQS为0.848；token预算差异28.1倍是主要混杂因素。QUESTION机制通过统计验证（Fisher p=0.0325，OR=21.0）。收敛性条件：最大度数<7（理论上），实证中度数达43仍稳健。

Conclusion: 组织AI的能力瓶颈在于认知保真度而非检索保真度。OIDA框架成功引入认知结构表示，QUESTION机制填补了现有系统对组织无知表示的空白。形式化性质已建立，等token预算的消融实验已预注册待执行。

Abstract: Organizational knowledge used by AI agents typically lacks epistemic structure: retrieval systems surface semantically relevant content without distinguishing binding decisions from abandoned hypotheses, contested claims from settled ones, or known facts from unresolved questions. We argue that the ceiling on organizational AI is not retrieval fidelity but \emph{epistemic} fidelity--the system's ability to represent commitment strength, contradiction status, and organizational ignorance as computable properties.
  We present OIDA, a framework that structures organizational knowledge as typed Knowledge Objects carrying epistemic class, importance scores with class-specific decay, and signed contradiction edges. The Knowledge Gravity Engine maintains scores deterministically with proved convergence guarantees (sufficient condition: max degree $< 7$; empirically robust to degree 43). OIDA introduces QUESTION-as-modeled-ignorance: a primitive with inverse decay that surfaces what an organization does \emph{not} know with increasing urgency--a mechanism absent from all surveyed systems. We describe the Epistemic Quality Score (EQS), a five-component evaluation methodology with explicit circularity analysis. In a controlled comparison ($n{=}10$ response pairs), OIDA's RAG condition (3,868 tokens) achieves EQS 0.530 vs.\ 0.848 for a full-context baseline (108,687 tokens); the $28.1\times$ token budget difference is the primary confound. The QUESTION mechanism is statistically validated (Fisher $p{=}0.0325$, OR$=21.0$). The formal properties are established; the decisive ablation at equal token budget (E4) is pre-registered and not yet run.

</details>


### [332] [Detecting Safety Violations Across Many Agent Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11806)
*Adam Stein,Davis Brown,Hamed Hassani,Mayur Naik,Eric Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Meerkat系统，通过聚类和智能搜索来识别AI代理追踪中的安全违规，在检测稀疏且复杂的安全违规方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计方法在识别AI代理的安全违规时面临多重挑战：单一追踪判断器会遗漏跨追踪才能发现的违规，简单的智能审计无法扩展到大型追踪集合，固定监视器对未预见行为缺乏鲁棒性。这些违规往往稀少、复杂，甚至可能被对抗性隐藏，包括误用活动、隐蔽破坏、奖励黑客和提示注入等场景。

Method: Meerkat结合了聚类技术与智能搜索，通过结构化搜索和对有潜力区域的自适应调查，发现用自然语言描述的违规行为。该方法不依赖种子场景、固定工作流或穷举枚举，能够高效地在大量代理追踪中发现稀疏的安全违规。

Result: 在误用、不对齐和任务博弈等场景测试中，Meerkat相比基线监视器显著提高了安全违规的检测率；在一个顶级代理基准测试中发现了开发者作弊的普遍问题；在CyBench上发现的奖励黑客案例数量是之前审计的近4倍。

Conclusion: Meerkat为AI代理安全审计提供了一种可扩展且高效的方法，能够发现传统方法难以检测的安全违规，证明了结合聚类和智能搜索在处理大规模、复杂追踪数据方面的优势，为AI系统的安全监控提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: To identify safety violations, auditors often search over large sets of agent traces. This search is difficult because failures are often rare, complex, and sometimes even adversarially hidden and only detectable when multiple traces are analyzed together. These challenges arise in diverse settings such as misuse campaigns, covert sabotage, reward hacking, and prompt injection. Existing approaches struggle here for several reasons. Per-trace judges miss failures that only become visible across traces, naive agentic auditing does not scale to large trace collections, and fixed monitors are brittle to unanticipated behaviors. We introduce Meerkat, which combines clustering with agentic search to uncover violations specified in natural language. Through structured search and adaptive investigation of promising regions, Meerkat finds sparse failures without relying on seed scenarios, fixed workflows, or exhaustive enumeration. Across misuse, misalignment, and task gaming settings, Meerkat significantly improves detection of safety violations over baseline monitors, discovers widespread developer cheating on a top agent benchmark, and finds nearly 4x more examples of reward hacking on CyBench than previous audits.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [333] [Nanvix: A Multikernel OS Design for High-Density Serverless Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11669)
*Carlos Segarra,Pedro Henrique Penna,Enrique Saurez,Íñigo Goiri,Peter Pietzuch,Shan Lu,Rodrigo Fonseca*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Nanvix是一种新型多内核操作系统，通过将临时执行状态与长期持久状态分离，在保持强隔离性的同时显著提高了无服务器环境下的部署密度，相比现有系统减少20-100倍的服务器需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器提供商追求高资源利用率，但高部署密度与应用性能和隔离性之间存在矛盾。跨租户共享组件面临侧信道攻击风险，同租户内共享组件可能导致资源竞争，影响性能。如何在保证强隔离的同时实现高部署密度是一个开放挑战。

Method: 采用拆分设计的多内核OS架构：1）用户VM运行微内核，处理应用执行的线程和内存；2）系统VM运行宏内核，提供丰富的设备驱动，由同租户所有应用调用共享。临时执行状态（每个应用调用独有）与长期持久状态（同租户调用间共享）分离。所有I/O请求复用到系统VM。

Result: 通过系统级协同设计，Nanvix实现了数量级更低的应用启动时间，I/O开销适中。在生产负载回放测试中，相比最先进系统减少20-100倍的主机服务器需求，显著提升部署密度。

Conclusion: Nanvix的拆分设计成功实现了跨租户的强虚拟机隔离，同时不牺牲应用性能，并通过复用I/O请求到系统VM减少了同租户内的资源竞争，为无服务器环境下的高密度部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Serverless providers strive for high resource utilization by optimizing deployment density: how many applications can be deployed per host server. However, achieving high deployment density without compromising application performance or isolation remains an open challenge. High density can be achieved by sharing components across applications, yet applications from different tenants must be strongly isolated from each other due to the risk of side-channel attacks. Sharing components across applications from the same tenant, if done naively, can introduce contention on host resources thus negatively affecting application performance.
  We describe Nanvix, a new multikernel OS that disaggregates ephemeral execution state, unique per application invocation, from long-lived persistent state, shared among invocations from the same tenant. Applications in Nanvix execute inside a lightweight user VM running a micro-kernel that implements threads and memory, and forwards all I/O requests to a system VM. The system VM runs a macro-kernel with a rich set of device drivers and is shared among all invocations from the same tenant. Nanvix' split design achieves strong hypervisor isolation across tenants without sacrificing application performance, and reduces same-tenant contention by multiplexing all I/O requests to the system VM. Thanks to a system-wide co-design, Nanvix achieves order-of-magnitude lower application start up times with moderate I/O overheads. When replaying a production trace, Nanvix needs 20-100x fewer host servers compared to state-of-the-art systems, improving deployment density

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [334] [Para-B&B: Load-Balanced Deterministic Parallelization of Solving MIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09556)
*Jinyu Zhang,Di Huang,Yue Liu,Shuo Wang,Zhenyu Pu,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文为高性能MIP求解器HiGHS提出了首个完全开源的确定性并行分支定界实现，通过数据并行架构保证严格确定性，并采用AI驱动的负载均衡机制，在8线程下实现2.17倍的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数规划（MIP）虽广泛应用于生产计划、物流调度和资源分配，但属于NP难问题。分支定界作为基本精确方法，在商业应用中因计算异构性和严格确定性要求面临显著的并行化挑战，缺乏有效的开源解决方案。

Method: 提出数据并行架构，通过在工作线程间复制完整求解器状态、消除非确定性同步原语来保证严格确定性；引入AI驱动的负载均衡机制，采用多阶段工作负载预测模型（基于结构特征和历史性能数据估计节点计算复杂度）与动态参数调整策略；执行包含并发下潜操作、系统化数据整合和智能节点选择的编排并行阶段。

Result: 在80个MIPLIB 2017基准实例上进行全面评估，8线程下达到几何平均加速比2.17，同时保持完全确定性保证；节点数越多性能提升越显著，计算密集型实例加速比达5.12，线程空闲率平均为34.7%。

Conclusion: 成功实现了首个开源确定性并行分支定界框架，证明了数据并行架构与AI驱动负载均衡在MIP求解器并行化中的有效性，为商业应用中严格确定性要求的MIP并行求解提供了可行的开源解决方案。

Abstract: Mixed-integer programming (MIP) extends linear programming by incorporating both continuous and integer decision variables, making it widely used in production planning, logistics scheduling, and resource allocation. However, MIP remains NP-hard and cannot generally be solved to optimality in polynomial time. Branch-and-bound, a fundamental exact method, faces significant parallelization challenges due to computational heterogeneity and strict determinism requirements in commercial applications. This paper presents the first fully open-source implementation of deterministic parallel branch-and-bound for HiGHS, a high-performance MIP solver. Our approach introduces a novel data-parallel architecture ensuring strict determinism by replicating complete solver state across worker threads and eliminating non-deterministic synchronization primitives. A key innovation is our AI-driven load balancing mechanism employing multi-stage workload prediction models that estimate node computational complexity based on structural characteristics and historical performance data, coupled with dynamic parameter adjustment strategies. The framework executes orchestrated parallel phases including concurrent dive operations, systematic data consolidation, and intelligent node selection. Comprehensive experimental evaluation on 80 MIPLIB 2017 benchmark instances demonstrates effectiveness, achieving a geometric mean speedup of 2.17 using eight threads while maintaining complete deterministic guarantees. Performance gains become increasingly pronounced for higher node counts, with speedup factors reaching 5.12 for computationally intensive instances and thread idle rates averaging 34.7%.

</details>


### [335] [Interferences within a certifiable design methodology for high-performance multi-core platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09559)
*Mohamed Amine Khelassi,Felix Suchert,Abderaouf Amalou,Benjamin Lesage,Anika Christmann,Robin Hapka,Jeronimo Castrillon,Mihail Asavoae,Mathieu Jan,Claire Pagetti,Selma Saidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨抽象层次的方法论，通过整合硬件建模、机器学习代码分析、MLIR编译优化和系统级资源管控，解决多核平台共享资源干扰问题，提升安全关键系统的可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空电子和汽车系统采用高性能多核平台时，共享资源干扰导致难以保证可预测执行。现有方法仅从单一角度（如硬件层面或软件监控）研究干扰，但硬件行为、程序结构和系统配置之间存在复杂交互，单一抽象层次无法提供完整视图，需要跨层次的综合解决方案。

Method: 该方法整合了四个不同抽象层次的工具：(1)最底层使用PHYLOG对硬件进行形式化建模，通过微架构事务识别可能的干扰通道；(2)程序层利用机器学习分析定位对共享资源竞争最敏感的代码段；(3)编译层采用基于MLIR的变换技术重塑内存访问模式，降低共享资源压力；(4)系统层通过Linux cgroups强制执行静态约束，防止高干扰任务同时运行。

Result: 该方法能够有效减少内存干扰，提升系统可预测性，从而简化安全关键领域多核系统的认证过程。

Conclusion: 跨抽象层次的综合分析方法是解决多核系统共享资源干扰问题的关键，通过整合不同层次的技术手段，可以建立完整的干扰理解与缓解框架。

Abstract: The adoption of high-performance multi-core platforms in avionics and automotive systems introduces significant challenges in ensuring predictable execution, primarily due to shared resource interferences. Many existing approaches study interference from a single angle-for example, through hardware-level analysis or by monitoring software execution. However, no single abstraction level is sufficient on its own. Hardware behavior, program structure, and system configuration all interact, and a complete view is needed to understand where interferences come from and how to reduce them. In this paper, we present a methodology that brings together several tools that operate at different abstraction levels. At the lowest level, PHYLOG provides a formal model of the hardware and identifies possible interference channels using micro-architectural transactions. At the program level, machine learning analysis locates the exact parts of the code that are most sensitive to shared-resource contention. At the compilation level, MLIR-based transformations use this information to reshape memory access patterns and reduce pressure on shared resources. Finally, at the system level, Linux cgroups enforce static execution constraints to prevent highly interfering tasks from running together. The goal of our approach is to reduce memory interference and improve the system's predictability, thereby easing the certification process of multi-core systems in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [336] [SPEED-Bench: A Unified and Diverse Benchmark for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09557)
*Talor Abramovich,Maor Ashkenazi,Carl,Putterman,Benjamin Chislett,Tiyasa Mitra,Bita Darvish Rouhani,Ran Zilberstein,Yonatan Geifman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出了SPEED-Bench，一个用于标准化评估推测解码(SD)的综合基准套件，解决了现有基准任务多样性有限、缺乏面向吞吐量评估、以及未能反映生产环境的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码(SD)是加速大语言模型推理的关键技术，其性能本质上依赖于数据。然而现有基准存在三大缺陷：任务多样性有限、缺乏对面向吞吐量评估的支持、依赖无法反映生产环境的高层实现，这阻碍了对SD有效性的准确测量。

Method: 引入SPEED-Bench综合评估套件，包含两个核心数据划分：(1) Qualitative数据划分，通过优先考虑样本间语义多样性进行精选；(2) Throughput数据划分，支持从延迟敏感的低批量设置到面向吞吐量的高负载场景的加速比评估。该基准与vLLM和TensorRT-LLM等生产引擎集成，能够揭示被其他基准掩盖的系统行为。

Result: 通过SPEED-Bench发现了多个重要现象：量化了合成输入会高估真实世界吞吐量；识别了依赖于批大小的最优推测长度和低多样性数据中的偏差；分析了最先进推测模型中词汇剪枝的注意事项。

Conclusion: SPEED-Bench为推测解码算法的实际比较建立了统一的评估标准，使从业者能够准确评估SD在多样化语义域和真实服务场景中的性能表现。

Abstract: Speculative Decoding (SD) has emerged as a critical technique for accelerating Large Language Model (LLM) inference. Unlike deterministic system optimizations, SD performance is inherently data-dependent, meaning that diverse and representative workloads are essential for accurately measuring its effectiveness. Existing benchmarks suffer from limited task diversity, inadequate support for throughput-oriented evaluation, and a reliance on high-level implementations that fail to reflect production environments. To address this, we introduce SPEED-Bench, a comprehensive suite designed to standardize SD evaluation across diverse semantic domains and realistic serving regimes. SPEED-Bench offers a carefully curated Qualitative data split, selected by prioritizing semantic diversity across the data samples. Additionally, it includes a Throughput data split, allowing speedup evaluation across a range of concurrencies, from latency-sensitive low-batch settings to throughput-oriented high-load scenarios. By integrating with production engines like vLLM and TensorRT-LLM, SPEED-Bench allows practitioners to analyze system behaviors often masked by other benchmarks. We highlight this by quantifying how synthetic inputs overestimate real-world throughput, identifying batch-size dependent optimal draft lengths and biases in low-diversity data, and analyzing the caveats of vocabulary pruning in state-of-the-art drafters. We release SPEED-Bench to establish a unified evaluation standard for practical comparisons of SD algorithms.

</details>


### [337] [EdgeWeaver: Accelerating IoT Application Development Across Edge-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09592)
*Pawissanutt Lertpongrujikorn,Juahn Kwon,Hai Duc Nguyen,Mohsen Amini Salehi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出EdgeWeaver平台，通过统一的“对象”抽象和声明式QoS规范，在边缘-云连续体环境中自动化函数部署与资源管理，显著提升开发效率与系统可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FaaS平台在处理边缘-云连续体环境的复杂性、异构性和间歇性连接方面存在局限，开发者需手动管理集成与QoS保障，导致开发复杂且成本高昂。

Method: 引入统一的“对象”抽象封装应用逻辑、状态与QoS，利用Raft、CRDTs等分布式算法实现“类”在边缘与云端的自动部署，并通过声明式QoS需求驱动资源分配、函数放置与运行时自适应。

Result: 原型评估显示，EdgeWeaver提升开发效率31%，在声明式保障强一致性的同时实现9个9的可用性（比现有标准高10,000倍），且性能影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: EdgeWeaver有效简化了边缘-云连续体环境下的应用开发，实现了高可用性与强一致性的声明式保障，具有实用价值。

Abstract: The rise of complex, latency-sensitive IoT applications across the Edge-Cloud continuum exposes the limitations of current Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) platforms in seamlessly addressing the complexity, heterogeneity, and intermittent connectivity of Edge-Cloud environments. Developers are left to manage integration and Quality of Service (QoS) enforcement manually, rendering application development complicated and costly. To overcome these limitations, we introduce the EdgeWeaver platform that offers a unified "object" abstraction that is seamlessly distributed across the continuum to encapsulate application logic, state, and QoS. EdgeWeaver automates "class" deployment across edge and cloud by composing established distributed algorithms (e.g., Raft, CRDTs)-enabling developers to declaratively express QoS (e.g., availability and consistency) desires that, in turn, guide internal resource allocation, function placement, and runtime adaptation to fulfill them. We implement a prototype of EdgeWeaver and evaluate it under diverse settings and using human subjects. Results show that EdgeWeaver boosts development productivity by 31%, while declaratively enforcing strong consistency and achieving 9 nines availability, 10,000X higher than the current standard, with negligible performance impact.

</details>


### [338] [VTC: DNN Compilation with Virtual Tensors for Data Movement Elimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09558)
*Muyan Hu,Ahan Gupta,Jiachen Yuan,Vima Gupta,Taeksang Kim,Xin Xu,Janardhan Kulkarni,Ofer Dekel,Vikram Adve,Charith Mendis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出VTC张量编译框架，首次通过虚拟张量概念消除深度神经网络中所有不必要的数据移动，在NVIDIA GPU上实现最高1.93倍加速和60%的内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算与内存操作延迟差距的扩大，现有深度学习编译器的数据移动优化（如布局变换和算子融合）仅针对部分张量算子，错失了大量减少数据移动的机会，尤其在大语言模型等现代工作负载中问题突出。

Method: 提出VTC框架，引入虚拟张量概念，通过索引映射而非昂贵的全局内存物理数据传输来追踪计算算子间的数据移动；该框架可与现有计算内核无缝互操作并处理任意张量算子组合；同时提出新的数据移动消除算法自动识别最优虚拟张量创建策略。

Result: 在多种深度神经网络上的评估表明，VTC在NVIDIA GPU上相比现有机器学习编译器实现最高1.93倍加速（平均1.28倍），推理内存节省最高达60%（平均17.5%）。

Conclusion: VTC通过全面覆盖数据移动算子并消除不必要数据传输，有效解决了现有编译器优化的局限性，为当代深度神经网络工作负载提供了显著的性能提升和内存优化。

Abstract: With the widening gap between compute and memory operation latencies, data movement optimizations have become increasingly important for DNN compilation. Current optimizations such as layout transformations and operator fusion only target a subset of tensor operators and consequently miss important opportunities for reducing data movement in contemporary DNN workloads, including large language models.
  We introduce VTC, a novel tensor compilation framework that for the first time eliminates all unnecessary data movement by targeting the full spectrum of data movement operators. VTC proposes the concept of virtual tensors to track data movement between compute operators via index mappings rather than expensive physical data transfers to and from global memory, which can seamlessly interoperate with existing computation kernels and handle arbitrary tensor operator compositions. We also introduce a novel data movement elimination algorithm to automatically identify a profitable virtual tensor creation strategy. Evaluation on a variety of DNNs shows that VTC can outperform existing ML compilers by up to 1.93x (1.28x on average) on NVIDIA GPUs with up to 60% (17.5% on average) inference memory savings.

</details>


### [339] [StreamServe: Adaptive Speculative Flows for Low-Latency Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09562)
*Satyam Kumar,Arpit Singh Gautam,Kailash Talreja,Saurabh Jha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了StreamServe，一种分离式预填充-解码服务架构，通过跨计算通道的度量感知路由和在线自适应推测解码，在4 GPU配置下相比tensor parallel vLLM基线减少11-18倍延迟，吞吐量最高达2235 tokens/s，且TPOT保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统在处理多样化、突发性工作负载时，难以有效平衡吞吐量和延迟，因此需要一种能够动态适应工作负载变化的架构。

Method: 提出StreamServe架构，包含四个核心组件：(1) StreamScheduler用于请求编排；(2) FlowGuard用于多信号路由；(3) PipeServe Engine用于多GPU上的分离式预填充-解码执行；(4) SpecuStream用于运行时自适应推测。该架构结合了度量感知路由与在线自适应推测解码，后者根据运行时信号动态调整推测深度。

Result: 在4块A800 40GB GPU配置下（2个stream pairs），使用ALPACA、GSM8K、HUMANEVAL和SUM四个基准测试（共320个查询）进行评估。相比tensor parallel vLLM基线，延迟降低11-18倍；在摘要任务上吞吐量达2235 tokens/s；每输出token时间（TPOT）在各配置下保持稳定，表明性能提升源于架构效率而非token质量下降。

Conclusion: 在分离式框架内联合自适应路由和推测机制可为LLM推理创造独特的运行模式，尽管仅在单节点4 GPU设置上评估，但结果表明该架构的有效性。

Abstract: Efficient LLM serving must balance throughput and latency across diverse, bursty workloads. We introduce StreamServe, a disaggregated prefill decode serving architecture that combines metric aware routing across compute lanes with adaptive speculative decoding that tunes speculation depth online from runtime signals. StreamServe comprises four components: StreamScheduler for request orchestration, FlowGuard for multi signal routing, PipeServe Engine for disaggregated prefill decode execution on multi GPU, and SpecuStream for runtime adaptive speculation. We evaluate StreamServe on four benchmarks ALPACA, GSM8K, HUMANEVAL, and SUM with 80 queries each and 320 total using 4 A800 40GB GPUs configured as two stream pairs. Across these workloads, StreamServe reduces latency by 11 to 18 times relative to tensor parallel vLLM baselines and reaches throughput up to 2235 tokens per second on summarization tasks. Time per output token remains stable across configurations, indicating that the gains arise from architectural efficiency rather than token quality degradation. Although evaluated on a single node 4 GPU setup, these results suggest that jointly adapting routing and speculation within a disaggregated framework creates a distinct operating regime for LLM inference.

</details>


### [340] [ACE-Bench: A Lightweight Benchmark for Evaluating Azure SDK Usage Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09564)
*Wenxing Zhu,Simeng Qi,Junkui Chen,Yan Xie,Min Huang,Jingkan He,Xiao Wang,Cheng Chen,Sijing Meng,Tianqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ACE-Bench，一个针对LLM编码代理使用Azure SDK正确性的无执行基准测试，通过原子化标准验证解决方案，实现了低成本、可重复且可扩展的SDK评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对LLM编码代理的评估通常需要配置云资源并维护脆弱的端到端测试环境，这导致评估成本高昂、难以复现且难以在日常工作流中扩展。因此，需要一个快速、可复现且无需实际执行环境的基准测试来评估模型是否正确使用了SDK。

Method: 将官方Azure SDK文档示例转化为自包含的编码任务，并使用任务特定的原子化标准进行验证：包括用于强制要求API使用模式的确定性正则表达式检查，以及捕获语义工作流约束的基于参考的LLM评判检查。该设计避免了云资源调配和实际执行环境维护。

Result: 使用轻量级编码代理对多个最先进的LLM进行了基准测试，量化了在MCP增强设置中检索的益处，结果显示访问文档能带来持续的增益，同时也揭示了模型间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: ACE-Bench成功实现了在日间开发和CI流程中切实可行的SDK评估，显著降低了评估成本，提高了可重复性，并能随着文档演进扩展到新的SDK和编程语言。

Abstract: We present ACE-Bench (Azure SDK Coding Evaluation Benchmark), an execution-free benchmark that provides fast, reproducible pass or fail signals for whether large language model (LLM)-based coding agents use Azure SDKs correctly-without provisioning cloud resources or maintaining fragile end-to-end test environments. ACE-Bench turns official Azure SDK documentation examples into self-contained coding tasks and validates solutions with task-specific atomic criteria: deterministic regex checks that enforce required API usage patterns and reference-based LLM-judge checks that capture semantic workflow constraints. This design makes SDK-centric evaluation practical in day-to-day development and CI: it reduces evaluation cost, improves repeatability, and scales to new SDKs and languages as documentation evolves. Using a lightweight coding agent, we benchmark multiple state-of-the-art LLMs and quantify the benefit of retrieval in an MCP-enabled augmented setting, showing consistent gains from documentation access while highlighting substantial cross-model differences.

</details>


### [341] [AEG: A Baremetal Framework for AI Acceleration via Direct Hardware Access in Heterogeneous Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09565)
*Hua Jiang,Sayan Mandal,Brandon Kirincich,Govind Varadarajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的、硬件无关的裸机运行时架构，通过"控制即数据"范式和RCB机制，在异构加速器上实现了高性能ML推理，相比Linux-based方案计算效率提升9.2倍且延迟方差接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的边缘部署框架（如TinyML）依赖实时操作系统（RTOS），引入了不必要的复杂性和性能瓶颈，无法充分发挥异构加速器（如AIE阵列）的性能潜力。

Method: 提出统一裸机运行时架构：1）将复杂控制逻辑扁平化为线性可执行的运行时控制块（RCBs），实现"控制即数据"范式；2）通过最小化运行时硬件抽象层（RHAL）解耦硬件细节；3）集成运行时平台管理（RTPM）处理系统级编排；4）引入运行时内存文件系统（RIMFS）管理无OS环境下的数据。

Result: 在ResNet-18实现中：计算效率相比Linux版Vitis AI提升9.2倍；数据移动开销减少3-7倍；延迟方差接近零（CV=0.03%）；仅用28个AIE tiles（Vitis AI需304个）在ImageNet上达到68.78% Top-1准确率。

Conclusion: 该统一裸机架构在不牺牲正确性的前提下，显著提升了异构加速器上ML推理的效率和性能确定性，为无OS环境的边缘AI部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a unified, hardware-independent baremetal runtime architecture designed to enable high-performance machine learning (ML) inference on heterogeneous accelerators, such as AI Engine (AIE) arrays, without the overhead of an underlying real-time or general-purpose operating system. Existing edge-deployment frameworks, such as TinyML, often rely on real-time operating systems (RTOS), which introduce unnecessary complexity and performance bottlenecks. To address this, our solution fundamentally decouples the runtime from hardware specifics by flattening complex control logic into linear, executable Runtime Control Blocks (RCBs). This "Control as Data" paradigm allows high-level models, including Adaptive Data Flow (ADF) graphs, to be executed by a generic engine through a minimal Runtime Hardware Abstraction Layer (RHAL). We further integrate Runtime Platform Management (RTPM) to handle system-level orchestration (including a lightweight network stack) and a Runtime In-Memory File System (RIMFS) to manage data in OS-free environments. We demonstrate the framework's efficacy with a ResNet-18 image classification implementation. Experimental results show 9.2$\times$ higher compute efficiency (throughput per AIE tile) compared to Linux-based Vitis AI deployment, 3--7$\times$ reduction in data movement overhead, and near-zero latency variance (CV~$=0.03\%$). The system achieves 68.78\% Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet using only 28 AIE tiles compared to Vitis AI's 304 tiles, validating both the efficiency and correctness of this unified bare-metal architecture.

</details>


### [342] [Simplicity Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09591)
*Andrew Sampson,Yuta Saito,Ronny Chan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Bebop序列化格式，通过固定字节数编码消除条件分支，实现比Protocol Buffers快9-213倍的解码速度，并构建了支持批量流水线的传输无关RPC协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流数据交换格式（Protocol Buffers、JSON）采用可变长度编码，解码器需要逐字节检查续延位或逐字符解析，产生数据依赖的条件分支，导致现代CPU流水线停顿，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 设计Bebop序列化格式，所有数据类型采用固定字节数编码（如32位整数固定4字节），解码仅需单次内存读取且无条件分支；基于相同线路格式构建传输无关的RPC协议，引入批量流水线技术实现单次往返执行跨服务依赖调用。

Result: 在19个解码工作负载中，Bebop解码速度比Protocol Buffers快9-213倍。在1536维嵌入向量上，Bebop解码耗时2.8纳秒，而Protocol Buffers需111纳秒，simdjson需4.69微秒，差距达1675倍。64KB以上记录的解码器达到峰值内存带宽的86%，CPU不再是瓶颈。

Conclusion: Bebop通过固定长度编码消除了解码过程中的条件分支开销，显著提升序列化性能，使内存带宽成为新的限制因素；其RPC协议消除了HTTP/2依赖，解决了gRPC在无服务器平台和浏览器中的部署限制问题。

Abstract: The dominant data interchange formats encode integers using a variable number of bytes or represent floating-point numbers as variable-length UTF-8 strings. The decoder must inspect each byte for a continuation bit or parse each character individually, producing data-dependent branches that stall modern CPU pipelines. Protocol Buffers pays this cost on every integer, field tag, and length prefix. JSON pays it on every value.
  We present Bebop, a serialization format where every data type uses a fixed number of bytes. A 32-bit integer is always four bytes. Decoding becomes a single memory read with no conditionals. Across 19 decode workloads, Bebop decodes 9--213$\times$ faster than Protocol Buffers. On a 1536-dimension embedding vector, Bebop decodes in 2.8 nanoseconds versus 111 nanoseconds for Protocol Buffers and 4.69 microseconds for simdjson, a 1,675$\times$ gap. On records above 64 KB, the decoder achieves 86% of peak memory bandwidth. The CPU is no longer the bottleneck.
  We also present a transport-agnostic RPC protocol built on the same wire format. The protocol introduces batch pipelining, where dependent cross-service calls execute in a single round trip with server-side dependency resolution. It deploys over HTTP/1.1, HTTP/2, and binary transports without proxies, removing the HTTP/2 requirement that limits gRPC on serverless platforms and in browsers.

</details>


### [343] [Bipartite matching under communication constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10744)
*Moonmoon Mohanty,Gautham Bolar,Preetam Patil,Ayalvadi Ganesh,Jean-Francois Chamberland,Parimal Parag*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对数据中心网络的大规模分布式调度问题，提出了一种基于度偏采样和随机稀疏化的单轮概率匹配算法，在稀疏和密集场景下均能提升匹配规模，并有效扩大网络稳定区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代数据中心网络中，数千台主机竞争共享链路容量，系统规模庞大导致集中式调度不可行。因此需要研究在通信约束下，仅利用本地信息进行分布式调度的双分图匹配问题。

Method: 将调度建模为通信约束下的双分图匹配问题，提出一类单轮概率匹配算法。核心思想包括：1）度偏采样，发送方利用接收方的度信息指导随机选择；2）随机稀疏化，发送方仅报告其连接的一个随机子集。结合稀疏化至度二与贪婪选择，得到一种无需参数调优的算法。

Result: 在随机图模型上建立了性能分析保证。在稀疏场景下，度偏采样比现有通信约束算法获得更高的期望匹配规模；在密集场景下，发现了一个反直觉现象，即通过稀疏化故意限制可用连接能增加期望匹配数。结合稀疏化与贪婪选择的算法在数据包级仿真中显著扩展了网络稳定区域。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式匹配算法框架不仅适用于数据中心网络调度，还可推广至其他具有本地信息约束的资源分配场景，为大规模系统的分布式调度提供了理论和实践支持。

Abstract: In modern data center networks, thousands of hosts contend for shared link capacity; the scale of these systems makes centralized scheduling impractical. This article models such scheduling as a bipartite matching problem under communication constraints: senders express interest in forming connections, and receivers respond using only locally available information. A class of single-round probabilistic matching algorithms is proposed, built on two key ideas: degree-biased sampling, in which senders use receiver degrees to inform their random selection, and random thinning, in which senders report only a random subset of their connections. Analytical performance guarantees are established for random graph models. In sparse regimes, degree-biased sampling yields a higher expected matching size than prior communication-constrained algorithms; in denser settings, a counterintuitive phenomenon emerges where deliberately restricting available connections through thinning increases the expected number of matches. Combining thinning to degree two with greedy selection produces an algorithm that requires no parameter tuning and, in packet-level simulations with production traffic traces, significantly extends the network stability region. Although motivated by data center network scheduling, the underlying framework of bipartite matching under local information constraints is portable to other resource allocation settings.

</details>


### [344] [Benchmarking Compound AI Applications for Hardware-Software Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09593)
*Paramuth Samuthrsindh,Angel Cervantes,Varun Gohil,Gohar Irfan Chaudhry,Christina Delimitrou,Adam Belay*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于复合AI应用（由LLM、ML模型、外部工具和数据源交互组成）的跨栈基准测试套件，旨在填补系统社区缺乏标准化基准的空白，并指导软硬件协同设计以提高资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI应用正迅速成为数据中心的重要工作负载，其组件多样且应用场景广泛，导致从应用层、服务软件层到硬件层的部署堆栈存在巨大的配置空间。然而，目前系统社区缺乏标准化的基准测试来分析这一复杂的设计空间并指导系统设计。

Method: 开发了一个专用于复合AI应用的跨栈分析基准测试套件，用于探索和分析跨越多个堆栈层的配置空间及其对性能、成本和资源消耗的影响。

Result: 利用该基准测试套件，导出了跨越堆栈多个层的关键结论和设计原则，这些发现可用于指导软硬件协同设计。

Conclusion: 通过软硬件协同设计，可以解锁更高的资源效率，该基准测试套件为复合AI应用的系统设计和优化提供了重要的指导依据。

Abstract: Compound AI applications, composed from interactions between Large Language Models (LLMs), Machine Learning (ML) models, external tools and data sources are quickly becoming an integral workload in datacenters. Their diverse sub-components and use-cases present a large configuration-space across the deployment stack -- ranging from applications and serving software down to hardware -- each of which may influence the application performance, deployment cost, and/or resource consumption. Despite their rapid adoption, however, the systems community lacks a standardized benchmark for analyzing this complicated design-space and guiding in system design. In this work, we present our benchmarking suite used for cross-stack analysis of Compound AI applications. Using this, we derive key takeaways and design principles spanning several layers of the stack for hardware-software co-design to unlock higher resource-efficiency.

</details>


### [345] [Why Smaller Is Slower? Dimensional Misalignment in Compressed LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09595)
*Jihao Xin,Tian Lyu,Qilong Pan,Kesen Wang,Marco Canini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出GAC框架，通过解决GPU维度不对齐问题，使压缩后的大语言模型在保持质量的同时恢复高达1.5倍的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练压缩技术虽然能减少大语言模型的参数量，但会产生不规则张量维度，导致GPU性能下降（称为"维度不对齐"现象）。例如，ASVD压缩的Llama-3-8B虽然参数减少15%，但运行速度并未提升，因为95%的维度存在不对齐问题。

Method: 提出GAC（GPU对齐压缩）新范式，该框架可封装任何降维压缩器，通过多选择背包优化在相同参数预算下重新选择硬件对齐的维度。研究从框架、库和硬件三个层面进行全栈分析，追踪性能下降的根本原因。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B上使用ASVD和LLM-Pruner进行评估，GAC实现100%的维度对齐，在保持模型质量的同时恢复高达1.5倍的加速效果。

Conclusion: 维度不对齐是限制压缩模型GPU性能的关键瓶颈，GAC通过硬件感知的维度优化成功解决了这一问题，为大语言模型压缩提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Post-training compression reduces LLM parameter counts but often produces irregular tensor dimensions that degrade GPU performance -- a phenomenon we call \emph{dimensional misalignment}. We present a full-stack analysis tracing root causes at three levels: framework, library, and hardware. The key insight is that model inference becomes slower because the resulting dimensions are unfriendly with the GPU execution stack. For example, compressing Llama-3-8B with activation-aware singular value decomposition (ASVD) has 15\% fewer parameters yet runs no faster than the uncompressed baseline, because 95\% of its dimensions are misaligned.
  We propose \textbf{GAC} (GPU-Aligned Compression), a new compression paradigm that wraps any dimension-reducing compressor and re-selects hardware-aligned dimensions via multi-choice knapsack optimization under the same parameter budget. We evaluate GAC on Llama-3-8B with ASVD and LLM-Pruner, achieving 100\% alignment and recovering up to 1.5$\times$ speedup while preserving model quality.

</details>


### [346] [Duration-Informed Workload Scheduler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09599)
*Daniela Loreti,Davide Leone,Andrea Borghesi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合机器学习预测模块的工作负载调度器，在Tier-0超级计算机的实际负载数据上测试，成功将所有作业的平均等待时间减少了约11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算系统的行为受众多子系统控制，其中工作负载调度器对作业的及时执行至关重要。制定高质量的调度决策需要预先获知作业的执行时长，但这对于用户而言是一项非平凡的任务，因此需要一种有效的方法来预测作业时长以优化调度。

Method: 设计了一种增强型工作负载调度器，该调度器集成了基于机器学习构建的作业时长预测模块。研究者利用来自Tier-0超级计算机的真实工作负载轨迹数据对该系统进行了评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该增强型调度器能够有效优化调度决策。与未使用预测模块的基准相比，该方法使所有作业的平均等待时间降低了约11%。

Conclusion: 更低的等待时间直接意味着从用户角度看服务质量得到提升，同时从系统角度看周转效率得到提高。证明了将机器学习技术集成到高性能计算调度系统中具有显著的实际应用价值。

Abstract: High-performance computing systems are complex machines whose behaviour is governed by the correct functioning of its many subsystems. Among these, the workload scheduler has a crucial impact on the timely execution of the jobs continuously submitted to the computing resources. Making high-quality scheduling decisions is contingent on knowing the duration of submitted jobs before their execution--a non-trivial task for users that can be tackled with Machine Learning.
  In this work, we devise a workload scheduler enhanced with a duration prediction module built via Machine Learning. We evaluate its effectiveness and show its performance using workload traces from a Tier-0 supercomputer, demonstrating a decrease in mean waiting time across all jobs of around 11%. Lower waiting times are directly connected to better quality of service from the users' point of view and higher turnaround from the system's perspective.

</details>


### [347] [FlexVector: A SpMM Vector Processor with Flexible VRF for GCNs on Varying-Sparsity Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10113)
*Bohan Li,Shengmin Li,Xinyu Shi,Enyi Yao,Francky Catthoor,Simei Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了FlexVector，一种基于向量处理器的GCN推理加速架构，通过软硬件协同设计解决稀疏矩阵乘法中的不规则计算问题，实现了3.78倍加速和40.5%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图卷积网络（GCN）加速器在处理具有幂律节点度分布特征的图数据时，面临不规则计算负载的挑战，导致性能受限。作者旨在解决GCN推理中两阶段稀疏-稠密矩阵乘法（SpMM）执行效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出FlexVector架构，采用基于行乘积的数据流来规范化SpMM执行；引入软件管理的灵活向量寄存器文件（VRF）以适应不规则数据访问模式；开发了图感知预处理和节点分区策略，重构不规则图工作负载以更好地匹配数据流和VRF容量。

Result: 在五个真实GCN数据集上的实验表明，与最先进的以缓存为中心的基线相比，FlexVector实现了3.78倍的加速，能耗降低40.5%，且面积开销相当。

Conclusion: 通过硬件-软件协同设计，FlexVector有效地解决了GCN推理中SpMM计算的不规则性问题，显著提升了性能和能效，为图神经网络加速提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) are widely adopted for tasks involving relational or graph-structured data and can be formulated as two-stage sparse-dense matrix multiplication (SpMM) during inference. However, existing accelerators often struggle with the irregular workloads induced by power-law node degree distributions. In this work, we propose FlexVector, a vector-processor-based architecture that efficiently accelerates SpMM for GCN inference. To address irregular computation patterns, FlexVector adopts a row-wise, product-based dataflow that regularizes SpMM execution and exposes vector parallelism through full-row access to vector registers, eliminating the need for multi-banked register file designs. Building on this dataflow, it introduces software-managed, flexible vector register files (VRFs) that adapt to irregular data access patterns, without sacrificing memory access efficiency. To further exploit these architectural capabilities, we develop a graph-aware preprocessing and node partitioning strategy that restructures irregular graph workloads to better match the row-wise dataflow and VRF capacity. This hardware-software co-design reduces memory traffic, leading to significant performance and energy efficiency gains on real-world GCN workloads. Experimental results on five real-world GCN datasets show that the VRF-centric FlexVector achieves a 3.78x speedup and 40.5% lower energy at comparable area cost relative to a state-of-the-art cache-centric baseline with buffers of the same size.

</details>


### [348] [ECHO: Elastic Speculative Decoding with Sparse Gating for High-Concurrency Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09603)
*Xinyi Hu,Yuhao Shen,Baolin Zhang,Hengxin Zhang,Jun Dai,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingcheng Wan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ECHO框架，通过将投机解码重构为预算调度问题，解决了高并发场景下的计算瓶颈，实现了最高5.35倍的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法在生产线级别的高并发场景下性能显著下降。由于验证计算成为主要瓶颈，现有方法面临两难境地：静态树结构导致大量验证浪费，而动态树结构则存在累积误判和内核不兼容问题。

Method: 引入ECHO框架，将其集成到SGLang中，将投机执行重构为预算调度问题。采用稀疏置信门控机制将批处理作为统一的超级树进行管理，在深度和宽度之间弹性分配预算，协同优化减少全局验证步骤与最大化单步效率之间的权衡。

Result: 在包括工业级Qwen3-235B在内的多种模型规模上进行了广泛评估。ECHO在低负载和高负载场景下均持续超越现有最先进方法，实现了高达5.35倍的walltime加速，并带来超过20%的相对加速提升。

Conclusion: ECHO成功解决了高并发场景下投机解码的性能退化问题，为大规模语言模型的生产级服务提供了有效的加速方案，证明了预算调度方法在投机解码中的优越性。

Abstract: Speculative Decoding promises to accelerate the inference of Large Language Models, yet its efficacy often degrades in production-grade serving. Existing evaluations typically overlook the compute-bound nature of high-concurrency regimes, where verification compute becomes the dominant bottleneck. Consequently, prior methods face a dilemma: static trees incur massive verification waste, while dynamic trees suffer from cumulative misjudgments and kernel incompatibility. To bridge this gap, we introduce ECHO, a high concurrency-oriented framework integrated into SGLang that reformulates speculative execution as a budgeted scheduling problem. Crucially, ECHO employs sparse confidence gating to manage the batch as a unified super-tree, elastically pivoting budget between depth and width to co-optimize the trade-off between reducing global verification steps and maximizing per-step efficiency. Extensive evaluations across diverse model scales-particularly the industrial-grade Qwen3-235B-demonstrate that ECHO consistently outperforms SOTA methods in both low-load and high-load scenarios, achieving up to 5.35x walltime speedup and delivering over 20% relative speedup gain.

</details>


### [349] [LLM-assisted Agentic Edge Intelligence Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09607)
*Chinmaya Kumar Dehury,Siddharth Singh Kushwaha,Qiyang Zhang,Alaa Saleh,Praveen Kumar Donta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM辅助的边缘智能(LEI)框架，利用云端大语言模型自动生成和更新边缘设备端逻辑，解决了传统边缘分析硬编码、难以适应变化的问题，同时保持低资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘智能系统存在适应性差的问题：分析逻辑通常硬编码，当数据模式变化或新需求出现时，工程师需要编写新脚本并推送更新，导致迭代缓慢和运营成本增加。这种有限的适应性降低了大规模、异构、资源受限边缘部署的可扩展性和自主性。另一方面，大语言模型(LLM)虽然能够解释指令并生成代码，但其计算和内存需求通常无法直接部署在边缘设备上。

Method: 提出LLM辅助的边缘智能(LEI)框架，消除手动指定业务逻辑的需求。框架采用云端托管的LLM协调设备端逻辑的创建和更新：系统生成候选轻量级程序，基于可用数据和约束进行验证，然后将选定版本部署到每个设备。每个设备根据样本数据、元数据、上下文和当前资源限制接收定制化程序。

Result: 在四个异构数据集(空气质量、温湿度、风力和土壤数据集)上使用多个LLM后端进行评估。实验结果表明，框架在执行期间保持低平均CPU和内存利用率，能够高效适应变化的条件，同时保持资源效率。

Conclusion: LEI框架成功弥合了LLM强大能力与边缘设备资源限制之间的差距，实现了边缘智能系统的自动适应性和可扩展性，减少了对人工监督的依赖，为大规模异构边缘部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Edge intelligence delivers low-latency inference, yet most edge analytics remain hard-coded and must be redeployed as conditions change. When data patterns shift or new questions arise, engineers often need to write new scripts and push updates to devices, which slows iteration and raises operating costs. This limited adaptability reduces scalability and autonomy in large, heterogeneous, and resource-constrained edge deployments, and it increases reliance on human oversight. Meanwhile, large language models (LLMs) can interpret instructions and generate code, but their compute and memory requirements typically prevent direct deployment on edge devices. We address this gap with the LLM-assisted Edge Intelligence (LEI) framework, which removes the need for manually specified business logic. In LEI, a cloud-hosted LLM coordinates the creation and update of device-side logic as requirements evolve. The system generates candidate lightweight programs, checks them against available data and constraints, and then deploys the selected version to each device. This lets each device receive a tailored program based on sample data, metadata, context, and current resource limits. We evaluate LEI on four heterogeneous datasets, including air quality, temperature \& humidity, wind, and soil datasets using multiple LLM backends. The experimental results show that the framework maintains low average CPU and memory utilization during the execution. These results indicate that the framework adapts efficiently to changing conditions while maintaining resource efficiency.

</details>


### [350] [Characterizing Performance-Energy Trade-offs of Large Language Models in Multi-Request Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09611)
*Md. Monzurul Amin Ifath,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统刻画了多请求LLM推理的性能-能耗权衡，发现批处理大小是最关键的调节杠杆，不同工作负载下的效果差异显著；vLLM在解码密集型任务中表现更优，而Parrot在严格功耗约束下更节能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注单请求评估或单独的LLM推理系统，忽视了多请求工作流中特有的依赖关系和跨请求交互，且对此类相互依赖的LLM调用的能耗问题探索不足，缺乏对性能-能耗权衡的系统理解。

Method: 开发了四种代表性工作负载（顺序、交互、代理和复合模式），在NVIDIA A100测试平台上使用vLLM和Parrot两个先进服务系统，通过调控批处理大小、GPU功耗上限和输出长度等关键能耗参数，分析其对延迟、吞吐量和组件级能耗的影响。

Result: 批处理大小影响最大但效果依赖工作负载类型：对共享大提示词的工作负载效果显著，对顺序摘要无效，对多代理编程仅部分有效；GPU功耗上限节省有限但可预测；输出长度导致线性能耗增长；vLLM的引擎级优化在解码密集型任务中保持更高GPU利用率，Parrot的工作流感知调度在严格功耗约束下实现更低能耗。

Conclusion: 研究为开发者和系统运维人员设计性能与能耗感知的LLM服务系统提供了可操作的指导原则，有助于在新兴的多请求工作流场景中优化系统配置，实现性能与能效的平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in applications forming multi-request workflows like document summarization, search-based copilots, and multi-agent programming. While these workflows unlock richer functionality, they also amplify latency and energy demand during inference. Existing measurement and benchmarking efforts either focus on assessing LLM inference systems or consider single-request evaluations, overlooking workflow dependencies and cross-request interactions unique to multi-request workflows. Moreover, the energy usage of such interdependent LLM calls remains underexplored.
  To address these gaps, this paper presents the first systematic characterization of performance-energy trade-offs in multi-request LLM inference. We develop four representative workloads capturing sequential, interactive, agentic, and composite patterns common in modern deployments. Using an NVIDIA A100 testbed with state-of-the-art serving systems (vLLM and Parrot), we analyze how key energy knobs affect latency, throughput, and component-level energy use. Our findings reveal batch size as the most impactful lever, though benefits are workload dependent. While optimal batching benefits workloads with large shared prompts, it is ineffective for sequential summarization and only partially effective for multi-agent coding. GPU power capping provides modest but predictable savings, while output length induces linear energy scaling with limited efficiency gains. We further show that engine-level optimizations in vLLM maintain higher GPU utilization and efficiency, especially for decode-heavy workloads, while Parrot's workflow-aware scheduling achieves lower energy consumption under strict power constraints. These findings offer actionable guidelines for developers and system operators designing performance- and energy-aware LLM serving systems in emerging multi-request workflows.

</details>


### [351] [Token-Budget-Aware Pool Routing for Cost-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09613)
*Huamin Chen,Xunzhuo Liu,Junchen Jiang,Bowei He,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于token预算感知的池路由策略，通过将请求分流到短上下文高吞吐池或长上下文高容量池，解决了vLLM生产集群因配置-流量不匹配导致的资源浪费问题，实现了17-39%的GPU实例节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的vLLM集群通常按最坏情况的上下文长度配置实例，导致80-95%的短请求浪费了4-8倍的并发能力，同时引发KV-cache失败（OOM崩溃、抢占风暴、请求拒绝）。这两个问题的根本原因都是配置与流量特征不匹配。

Method: 提出token预算感知池路由策略：1）使用自校准的每类别的bytes-per-token比率估计每个请求的总token预算；2）根据预算将请求分发到两个vLLM池之一——面向短请求的高吞吐池或面向长请求的高容量池；3）通过usage.prompt_tokens反馈在线学习该比率（指数移动平均），无需tokenizer；4）提出闭式成本模型预测GPU节省量。

Result: 在Azure LLM推理数据集和LMSYS-Chat-1M的trace上，使用A100 GPU服务Llama-3-70B，GPU实例减少17-39%（在1000 req/s下节省$1.2-2.0M/年）。针对AMD MI300X上Qwen3-235B-A22B的案例研究（10000 req/s）显示节省$15.4M/年。算法仅增加O(1)调度开销，可与PagedAttention、连续批处理和预填充-解码分离等技术组合使用。

Conclusion: Token预算路由策略能够有效解决vLLM集群的配置-流量不匹配问题，显著降低GPU成本。该方法自校准、开销低、与现有优化技术兼容，为大规模LLM推理服务提供了一种实用的成本优化方案。

Abstract: Production vLLM fleets provision every instance for worst-case context length, wasting 4-8x concurrency on the 80-95% of requests that are short and simultaneously triggering KV-cache failures -- OOM crashes, preemption storms, and request rejections. Both problems share a single root cause: configuration-traffic mismatch.
  We propose token-budget-aware pool routing: estimate each request's total token budget using a self-calibrating per-category bytes-per-token ratio, then dispatch it to one of two vLLM pools -- a high-throughput short pool or a high-capacity long pool -- each right-sized for its workload class. The ratio is learned online via exponential moving average from usage.prompt_tokens feedback, requiring no tokenizer. A closed-form cost model, savings = alpha * (1 - 1/rho), predicts fleet-level GPU savings from two observable quantities: the short-traffic fraction alpha and the throughput gain ratio rho.
  On traces from the Azure LLM Inference Dataset and LMSYS-Chat-1M serving Llama-3-70B on A100 GPUs, token-budget routing reduces GPU instances by 17-39% (\$1.2-2.0M/yr at 1,000 req/s), with savings verified by a self-contained discrete-event simulator. A case study projecting Qwen3-235B-A22B on AMD MI300X at 10,000 req/s shows \$15.4M/yr in savings. The algorithm adds O(1) dispatch overhead, self-calibrates across content types without a tokenizer, and composes with PagedAttention, continuous batching, and prefill-decode disaggregation.

</details>


### [352] [Calibrating Microgrid Simulations for Energy-Aware Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09615)
*Marvin Steinke*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Vessim的自校准能耗感知软件测试平台，通过集成真实计算节点和可再生能源模拟器，解决了碳感知计算研究中开发环境匮乏的问题，实现了较为准确的功耗建模与校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心电力需求激增，预计2026年将超过1000 TWh，碳感知计算可通过将能耗与低碳可再生能源生产对齐来减轻环境影响，但面临开发环境稀缺的挑战。现有方案要么依赖昂贵复杂的物理系统架构，难以集成和维护；要么依赖缺乏真实性的全仿真方案，忽略了系统开销和实时功耗波动。

Method: 提出基于Software-in-the-Loop协同仿真框架Vessim的自校准能耗感知软件测试平台，集成可再生能源生产模拟器和真实计算节点。首先使用Kepler框架近似估算应用级功耗，然后利用外部插座功率计作为系统级权威测量源，在Vessim微电网仿真中对功耗进行校准。

Result: Kepler框架对整个计算节点的功耗近似较为准确，平均回归系数为1.01，R²值为0.95，但某些机器学习工作负载偏差较大。回归线平均静态y截距约5.23 W表明空闲功耗近似存在不准确性。动态进程级功耗校准使GPU工作负载准确度提升约50%，CPU工作负载提升约3.5%。

Conclusion: 所提出的自校准能耗感知测试平台有效弥补了现有碳感知计算开发环境的不足，通过集成真实节点和校准机制实现了较为准确的功耗建模，为碳感知计算研究提供了实用且经济的开发环境。

Abstract: The surge for computing resource demand is increasing global electricity consumption in data centers which is expected to exceed 1000 TWh by 2026, mainly attributable to adoption of new AI technologies. Carbon-aware computing strategies can mitigate their environmental impact by aligning power consumption with the production of low-carbon renewable energy, but they face challenges due to the scarcity of development environments. Existing solutions either rely on costly and complex physical system architectures that are difficult to integrate and maintain or on full simulations that, while more economical, often lack realism by ignoring system overheads, and real-time node power consumption and resource fluctuations. This thesis remediates these issues by proposing a self-calibrating energy-aware software testbed that uses the Software-in-the-Loop co-simulation framework Vessim to integrate renewable energy production simulators, while including real computing nodes. The application-level power consumption of these are first approximated by the Kepler framework and then calibrated within Vessim's microgrid simulation using an external socket power meter as a definitive measurement source on the system-level. The evaluation of the testbed with GPU and CPU intensive workloads reveal fairly accurate power approximation of the whole computing node by the Kepler framework, with an average regression coefficient of 1.01 and R^2 values of 0.95, though certain machine learning workloads showed higher deviation. The average static y-intercept of the regression line of ~5.23 W indicate inaccuracies in the idle power approximation. Calibration of dynamic per-process power consumption improved accuracy for GPU workloads by ~50%, while CPU workloads saw a modest improvement of ~3.5%.

</details>


### [353] [DCGen 1.1 Technical Report: Generating Datacenter Configurations (including IT, Power, Cooling)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09616)
*Wedan Emmanuel Gnibga,Andrew A. Chien*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DCGen，这是一个能够生成多样化、现实的数据中心配置的工具，用于支持数据中心硬件设计、运行动力学、冷却机制及其与电网交互的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数字应用和工作负载的多样化，数据中心规模和功率密度不断增加，设计现实的数据中心模型变得愈发困难。现有研究常依赖过时或不切实际的设计，缺乏对数据中心各层级及其交互的全面理解，因此亟需一个能生成真实配置的工具。

Method: DCGen工具通过以下方式生成数据中心配置：1) 针对AI训练、AI推理和云服务等用例选择参考IT硬件配置；2) 根据功率、计算能力和面积目标进行设计；3) 从生产设备目录中选择冷却和配电基础设施组件，优化空间或功率效率；4) 在机架和数据中心层面捕捉IT、冷却和供电基础设施的功率与空间特性。

Result: DCGen能够生成包括IT硬件、冷却和配电基础设施在内的多样化数据中心配置，支持不同规模（如10 MW、100 MW、1 GW）和计算能力目标，并优化空间或功率效率，实现了对功率、能源和空间的精确建模。

Conclusion: DCGen通过"假设"场景探索支持使用现实数据中心设计的研究，包括功率密度演化、电网互联容量规划、数据中心-电网交互和空间管理等研究，为数据中心研究领域提供了重要的基础设施支持。

Abstract: Diversification of digital applications and workloads has driven the development of diverse datacenter architectures on ever-larger scales. These datacenters consist of complex IT, power, and cooling systems with interdependencies that influence configuration and performance. As datacenters scale and power density increase, designing realistic models becomes more difficult, particularly for research, because it requires understanding all layers of the datacenter and how they interact. Consequently, many studies rely on outdated or unrealistic designs.
  To support research in datacenter hardware design principles, operational dynamics, cooling mechanisms, and interactions of these facilities with the electrical grid, we have designed DCGen, a tool which can generate a variety of datacenter configurations (including IT hardware, cooling and power distribution infrastructures) at various electrical power, compute capability, and area targets.The tool captures power and space characteristics of IT, cooling, and power infrastructures at both the rack and datacenter levels, enabling modeling of power, energy, and space.
  DCGen leverages specific use cases such as AI training, AI inference, and cloud services, to select reference and canonical IT hardware configurations, producing realistic mixes of server types. It can target datacenter scale in terms of both power (e.g., 10 MW, 100 MW, 1 GW) and compute capability. For cooling and power distribution infrastructures, DCGen chooses components from a production equipment catalog that optimizes for space or power efficiency while meeting the datacenter capacity requirements. This tool supports research using realistic datacenter designs through ``what-if'' scenario exploration, including studies of power density evolution over time, grid interconnection capacity planning, datacenter-grid interactions, and space management.

</details>


### [354] [HearthNet: Edge Multi-Agent Orchestration for Smart Homes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09618)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Krinos Li,Yefan Zhang,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了HearthNet，一个面向智能家居的边缘多智能体编排系统，通过持久化、角色专精化的LLM代理及明确的边界划分，解决了自然语言控制场景下的持久性、事件驱动及易失效等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能家居用户倾向于使用自然语言而非手动配置进行控制，但现实部署面临设备故障、集成中断及需人工干预等脆弱性问题。现有的智能体工具包适用于会话级委托，但智能家居控制具有持久性、事件驱动、易失效及与物理设备绑定且无共享上下文窗口等不同特征，因此需要新的解决方案。

Method: HearthNet在家居中心部署一组持久化、角色专精化的LLM代理，通过MQTT、Git支持的共享状态以及根颁发的致动租约进行协调，通过薄适配器管理异构设备。该设计将上下文外部化，保留执行历史，并在明确边界上分离规划、验证、授权和致动。原型运行于商用边缘硬件和Android设备，在本地保留编排、状态管理和设备控制，使用托管LLM API进行推理。

Result: 在商用边缘硬件和Android设备上实现了原型系统；通过三个现场场景进行了演示：从模糊自然语言进行意图驱动的多智能体协调、基于时间线的冲突解决与追踪、以及在设备致动前拒绝陈旧或未授权的命令。

Conclusion: HearthNet证明了在智能家居边缘环境中部署持久化多LLM代理编排系统的可行性，能够有效支持自然语言控制、冲突解决及安全约束，为智能家居自动化提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Smart-home users increasingly want to control their homes in natural language rather than assemble rules, dashboards, and API integrations by hand. At the same time, real deployments are brittle: devices fail, integrations break, and recoveries often require manual intervention. Existing agent toolkits are effective for session-scoped delegation, but smart-home control operates under a different scenario: it is persistent, event-driven, failure-prone, and tied to physical devices with no shared context window. We present HearthNet, an edge multi-agent orchestration system for smart homes. HearthNet deploys a small set of persistent, role-specialized LLM agents at the home hub, where they coordinate through MQTT, Git-backed shared state, and root-issued actuation leases to govern heterogeneous devices through thin adapters. This design externalizes context, preserves execution history, and separates planning, verification, authorization, and actuation across explicit boundaries. Our current prototype runs on commodity edge hardware and Android devices; it keeps orchestration, state management, and device control on-premise while using hosted LLM APIs for inference. We demonstrate the system through three live scenarios: intent-driven multi-agent coordination from ambiguous natural language, conflict resolution with timeline-based tracing, and rejection of stale or unauthorized commands before device actuation.

</details>


### [355] [Hardware Utilization and Inference Performance of Edge Object Detection Under Fault Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09631)
*Faezeh Pasandideh,Mehdi Azarafza,Achim Rettberg*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上对TensorRT优化的YOLO模型进行大规模故障注入实验，系统分析了资源受限边缘平台上深度学习模型的硬件行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型在自动驾驶系统的资源受限边缘平台上部署，迫切需要了解硬件在资源降级条件下的行为特征，以确保系统可靠性。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson Nano平台上，针对TensorRT优化的YOLOv10s、YOLOv11s和YOLO2026n模型，开展大规模故障注入实验，覆盖车道跟随和目标检测两类任务。利用大语言模型（LLMs）和潜在扩散模型（LDMs）生成故障数据，系统监测CPU负载、GPU利用率、内存消耗、功耗、吞吐量和热行为等硬件指标。

Result: 实验结果表明：在两类任务和模型中，推理引擎保持GPU占用率稳定，温升可控，功耗维持在安全范围内；内存使用在初始预热阶段后进入一致的释放模式。目标检测任务在内存和热行为方面表现出更大的变异性，但总体上TensorRT优化流程在输入数据严重降级情况下仍能保持良好的硬件稳定性。

Conclusion: TensorRT优化的YOLO模型在边缘平台的资源降级场景下展现出良好的硬件可靠性，为边缘推理性能研究提供了重要的硬件层面视角补充。

Abstract: As deep learning models are deployed on resource constrained edge platforms in autonomous driving systems, reli able knowledge of hardware behavior under resource degradation becomes an essential requirement. Therefore, we introduce a systematic characterization of CPU load, GPU utilization, RAM consumption, power draw, throughput, and thermal behaviour of TensorRT-optimized YOLOv10s, YOLOv11s and YOLO2026n pipelines running on NVIDIA Jetson Nano under a large-scale fault injection campaign targeting both lane-following and ob ject detection tasks. Faults are synthesized using a decoupled framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) and latent diffusion models (LDMs), based on original data from our JetBot platform data collection. Results show that across both tasks and both models the inference engines keep GPU occupancy stable, temperature rise under control, and power consumption within safe limits, while memory usage settles into a consistent release pattern after the initial warm-up phase. Object detection tends to show somewhat more variability in memory and thermal behavior, yet both tasks point to the same conclusion: the TensorRT pipelines hold up well even when the input data is heavily degraded. These findings offer a hardware-level view of model reliability that sits alongside, rather than against, the broader body of work focused on inference performance at the edge.

</details>


### [356] [Hotspot-Aware Scheduling of Virtual Machines with Overcommitment for Ultimate Utilization in Cloud Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09667)
*Jiaxi Wu,Pavel Popov,Wenquan Yang,Andrei Gudkov,Elizaveta Ponomareva,Xinming Han,Yunzhe Qiu,Jie Song,Stepan Romanov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动态CPU利用率和鲁棒性理论的在线虚拟机调度方法，通过概率k装箱模型和CloseRadiusFit算法有效解决了数据中心资源利用率不足和热点形成问题，性能接近理论最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决云数据中心运营过程中资源利用率不足的问题，特别是针对在线虚拟机调度这一挑战。传统方法仅基于静态配置进行调度，无法适应动态负载变化，导致资源浪费和热点问题。

Method: 采用Γ-鲁棒性理论管理动态特性，提出了概率k装箱的新型变体，在指定概率α下理论上保护物理机免受热点形成。开发了CloseRadiusFit调度算法和基于冷启动AI的预测算法。为验证解的质量，设计了混合整数线性规划(MILP)模型并结合数值上下界求解离线PkBP问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，CloseRadiusFit算法与下界和上界相比分别仅存在1.6%和3.1%的窄差距，证明了该方法接近最优解的优异性能。

Conclusion: 通过考虑动态CPU利用率而非静态配置，结合鲁棒性理论和概率装箱模型，所提出的在线虚拟机调度方法能够有效提高数据中心资源利用率，同时在理论保证下避免热点形成，实验验证了其接近最优的性能表现。

Abstract: We address the problem of under-utilization of resources in datacenters during cloud operations, specifically focusing on the challenge of online virtual machine (VM) scheduling. Rather than following the traditional approach of scheduling VMs based solely on their static flavors, we take into account their dynamic CPU utilization. We employ $Γ$-robustness theory to manage the dynamic nature and introduce a novel variant of bin packing - Probabilistic k-Bins Packing (PkBP), which theoretically protects the Physical Machines (PMs) from hotspots formation within a specified probability $α$. We develop a scheduling algroithm named CloseRadiusFit and cold-start AI based prediction algorithms for the online version of PkBP. To verify the quality of our approach towards the optimal solutions, we solve the Offline PkBP problem by designing a novel Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model and a combination of numerical upper and lower bounds. Our experimental results demonstrate that CloseRadiusFit achieves narrow gaps of 1.6% and 3.1% when compared to the lower and upper bounds, respectively.

</details>


### [357] [Agentic Compilation: Mitigating the LLM Rerun Crisis for Minimized-Inference-Cost Web Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09718)
*Jagadeesh Chundru*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Compile-and-Execute架构，通过将LLM推理与浏览器执行解耦，将重复性任务的推理成本从O(M×N)降至摊销O(1)，成本从~150美元降至<0.1美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于连续推理循环的LLM网页代理存在"重运行危机"（Rerun Crisis）：token支出和API延迟随执行频率线性增长，导致重复任务成本过高（5步流程500次迭代约150美元），严重限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出Compile-and-Execute架构：单次LLM调用处理DOM净化模块(DSM)生成的语义表示，输出确定性JSON工作流蓝图；轻量级运行时驱动浏览器执行，无需后续模型查询。JSON中间表示支持人在回路(HITL)补丁修复。

Result: 零样本编译成功率80-94%；单次编译成本0.002-0.092美元（跨5个前沿模型）；HITL补丁可将执行可靠性提升至近100%。实证验证涵盖数据提取、表单填写和指纹识别任务。

Conclusion: 确定性编译范式将推理成本从O(M×N)降至摊销O(1)，实现了此前连续架构下经济上不可行的规模化自动化，为LLM驱动的网页代理提供了经济可行的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-driven web agents operating through continuous inference loops -- repeatedly querying a model to evaluate browser state and select actions -- exhibit a fundamental scalability constraint for repetitive tasks. We characterize this as the Rerun Crisis: the linear growth of token expenditure and API latency relative to execution frequency. For a 5-step workflow over 500 iterations, a continuous agent incurs approximately 150.00 USD in inference costs; even with aggressive caching, this remains near 15.00 USD. We propose a Compile-and-Execute architecture that decouples LLM reasoning from browser execution, reducing per-workflow inference cost to under 0.10 USD. A one-shot LLM invocation processes a token-efficient semantic representation from a DOM Sanitization Module (DSM) and emits a deterministic JSON workflow blueprint. A lightweight runtime then drives the browser without further model queries. We formalize this cost reduction from O(M x N) to amortized O(1) inference scaling, where M is the number of reruns and N is the sequential actions. Empirical evaluation across data extraction, form filling, and fingerprinting tasks yields zero-shot compilation success rates of 80-94%. Crucially, the modularity of the JSON intermediate representation allows minimal Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) patching to elevate execution reliability to near-100%. At per-compilation costs between 0.002 USD and 0.092 USD across five frontier models, these results establish deterministic compilation as a paradigm enabling economically viable automation at scales previously infeasible under continuous architectures.

</details>


### [358] [ConfigSpec: Profiling-Based Configuration Selection for Distributed Edge--Cloud Speculative LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09722)
*Xiangchen Li,Saeid Ghafouri,Jiakun Fan,Babar Ali,Hans Vandierendonck,Dimitrios S. Nikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ConfigSpec框架，用于边缘-云端分布式推测LLM推理的配置选择。研究发现吞吐量、成本效率和能耗效率三个目标存在结构性冲突，无法通过单一配置同时优化，验证了基于性能分析的配置选择的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码系统虽然展示了性能和成本优势，但实际部署需要在草稿模型变体、量化级别、推测长度和异构边缘设备等庞大配置空间中进行选择。由于缺乏有效的配置选择机制，难以在实际场景中实现最优部署。

Method: 提出ConfigSpec框架，通过分析边缘设备特性和草稿-目标模型对齐度，建模草稿吞吐量、接受率和功耗，评估联合配置空间下的有效吞吐量、验证成本效率和能量效率。在三个边缘平台和两个LLM系列上进行了全面分析。

Result: 发现三个目标存在结构性冲突：有效吞吐量由最小最快的草稿模型在设备依赖的推测长度（K*=2-10）下最大化；成本效率和能耗效率都收敛到K=2，但成本效率偏好具有高接受率的最大草稿模型，而能耗效率偏好具有低功耗的最小草稿模型。

Conclusion: 单一固定配置无法同时优化所有目标，证实了在分离式边缘-云端LLM推理中基于性能分析的配置选择的必要性。这为实际部署推测解码系统提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Speculative decoding enables collaborative Large Language Model (LLM) inference across cloud and edge by separating lightweight token drafting from heavyweight verification. While prior systems show performance and cost benefits, practical deployment requires navigating a large configuration space spanning draft model variants, quantisation levels, speculative lengths, and heterogeneous edge devices. This paper presents ConfigSpec, a configurationselection framework for distributed speculative LLM serving. ConfigSpec profiles edge devices and draft-target alignment, and models drafting throughput, acceptance rate, and power to evaluate goodput, verification cost efficiency, and energy efficiency across the joint configuration space. Our analysis across three edge platforms and two LLM families reveals structurally conflicting optima. Firstly, goodput is maximised by the smallest, fastest draft model at device-dependent speculative lengths (K*=2-10). Secondly, both cost and energy efficiency converge to K=2 due to a dominant bonus-token effect-with cost favouring the largest drafter for its high acceptance rate and energy favouring the smallest for its low power draw. These conflicts confirm that no single fixed configuration can simultaneously optimise all objectives, underscoring the need for profiling-based configuration selection in disaggregated edge-cloud LLM inference.

</details>


### [359] [SMART: When is it Actually Worth Expanding a Speculative Tree?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09731)
*Lifu Wang,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SMART框架，通过硬件感知的边际分析优化推测解码的树构建，解决了大计算开销导致的加速失效问题，在MLLMs和LLMs上分别实现20.0%和15.4%的额外加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于树的推测解码方法在最大化令牌接受率时忽略了"效率悖论"：大型树的构建和验证计算开销呈超线性增长，在批处理规模增大或硬件饱和时反而导致负加速。

Method: 提出SMART系统感知边际分析框架，将树扩展重构为硬件感知优化问题。通过边际收益-成本规则，仅当节点的边际收益-成本比超过树级加速时才扩展节点，直接优化端到端加速。该框架无需训练，可作为即插即用控制器集成到现有框架中。

Result: 在LLaVA、Qwen2-VL等三个MLLMs和Llama-3.1、DeepSeek-R1等四个LLMs上验证，SMART持续优于现有最优基线。在计算密集型批处理场景和多种GPU架构下，MLLMs平均额外加速20.0%，LLMs平均额外加速15.4%，无性能损失。

Conclusion: SMART通过硬件感知的边际分析有效解决了推测解码中的效率悖论问题，实现了无需训练的即插即用加速，为大规模部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Tree-based speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by verifying a branching tree of draft tokens in a single target-model forward pass. However, existing methods prioritize maximizing token-level likelihood or the number of accepted tokens while ignoring a critical ``efficiency paradox'': the computational overhead of drafting and verifying big trees can grow super-linearly, particularly at scale. This often leads to negative wall-clock speedup when batch sizes increase or hardware saturation limits are reached. To address this, we propose SMART, a system-aware marginal analysis framework for runtime tree construction. SMART reformulates tree expansion as a hardware-aware optimization problem that directly maximizes end-to-end speedup. By applying a principled marginal benefit--cost rule at inference time, SMART expands a node only when its marginal benefit--cost ratio exceeds the tree-level speedup. SMART is training-free and serves as a plug-and-play controller for existing frameworks like MSD and EAGLE. Extensive evaluations across three MLLMs (e.g., LLaVA, Qwen2-VL) and four LLMs (e.g., Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-R1) demonstrate that SMART consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. It delivers an average additional speedup of 20.0\% for MLLMs and 15.4\% for LLMs across compute-bound batching regimes and diverse GPU architectures without performance loss.

</details>


### [360] [A-IO: Adaptive Inference Orchestration for Memory-Bound NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09752)
*Chen Zhang,Yan Ding,Haotian Wang,Chubo Liu,Keqin Li,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM在异构NPU上部署的"模型缩放悖论"，分析了细粒度投机解码的同步开销问题，提出了宏微观协同优化的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在异构NPU平台(如昇腾910B)上的自回归解码阶段面临严重的内存受限挑战，而静态部署单一尺寸模型和纯微观加速算法存在局限性，需要深入分析根本问题。

Method: 分析揭示了"模型缩放悖论"，指出细粒度投机解码在NPU计算图编译下的核同步开销问题，以及单纯依赖Prompt LookUp Decoding(PLD)等微观加速算法的严重局限性。

Result: 识别出了现有方法的三大问题：静态部署单一尺寸模型导致的模型缩放悖论、细粒度投机解码在NPU上的核同步开销、纯微观加速算法的局限性。

Conclusion: 现有的LLM在NPU部署策略存在系统性缺陷，需要重新思考部署范式，可能需要宏观架构与微观算法的协同优化方案。

Abstract: During the deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs), the autoregressive decoding phase on heterogeneous NPU platforms (e.g., Ascend 910B) faces severe memory-bound challenges. This study reveals the ``Model Scaling Paradox'' caused by the static deployment of single-sized models. It also points out the kernel synchronization overhead of fine-grained speculative decoding \cite{leviathan2023fast, chen2023speculative} under NPU computational graph compilation, and the severe limitations of purely relying on micro-level acceleration algorithms like Prompt LookUp Decoding (PLD)

</details>


### [361] [Rebooting Microreboot: Architectural Support for Safe, Parallel Recovery in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09963)
*Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个三代理架构，通过将规划与执行分离并定义具有显式副作用的七动作指令集架构（ISA），结合微内核的事务性验证执行，实现了微服务环境下安全微重启。在线推断恢复边界仅需21ms（P99），仿真中减少95%的代理造成的损害，在线环境中实现零损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重启虽然能通过仅重启故障组件实现快速恢复，但在现代微服务中，由于密集的依赖关系，简单的重启操作可能不安全，会中断众多调用者。此外，自主修复代理直接执行原始基础设施命令，缺乏安全保证，可能造成更大范围的故障。

Method: 提出三代理架构（诊断、规划、验证），定义包含七个动作的指令集架构（ISA），明确每个动作的副作用语义；开发小型微内核对修复计划进行事务性验证和执行；在线从分布式追踪中推断恢复边界，计算最小重启组和顺序约束；显式不信任代理，安全性由ISA和微内核保证。

Result: 在阿里巴巴和Meta的工业追踪数据以及DeathStarBench注入故障的实验中，恢复组推断在P99情况下仅需21毫秒；类型化执行在仿真中减少95%的代理造成的损害，在线环境中实现0%损害。LLM推理开销增加了具有快速自动重启服务的恢复时间（TTR）。

Conclusion: 该方法的核心价值在于安全性而非速度。通过将规划与执行分离、定义严格的ISA以及微内核验证，在保证微服务环境下微重启安全性的同时，也为LLM等自主代理的安全修复提供了可行方案。对于已有快速自动重启机制的服务，LLM推理开销可能会延长恢复时间。

Abstract: Microreboot enables fast recovery by restarting only the failing component, but in modern microservices naive restarts are unsafe: dense dependencies mean rebooting one service can disrupt many callers. Autonomous remediation agents compound this by actuating raw infrastructure commands without safety guarantees. We make microreboot practical by separating planning from actuation: a three-agent architecture (diagnosis, planning, verification) proposes typed remediation plans over a seven-action ISA with explicit side-effect semantics, and a small microkernel validates and executes each plan transactionally. Agents are explicitly untrusted; safety derives from the ISA and microkernel. To determine where restart is safe, we infer recovery boundaries online from distributed traces, computing minimal restart groups and ordering constraints. On industrial traces (Alibaba, Meta) and DeathStarBench with fault injection, recovery-group inference runs in 21 ms at P99; typed actuation reduces agent-caused harm by 95% in simulation and achieves 0% harm online. The primary value is safety, not speed: LLM inference overhead increases TTR for services with fast auto-restart.

</details>


### [362] [Tessera: Unlocking Heterogeneous GPUs through Kernel-Granularity Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10180)
*Tiancheng Hu,Jin Qin,Zheng Wang,Junhao Hu,Yuzheng Wang,Lei Chen,Yizhou Shan,Mingxing Zhang,Ting Cao,Chunwei Xia,Huimin Cui,Tao Xie,Chenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Tessera，这是首个内核级 disaggregation 系统，通过将大模型推理任务映射到异构GPU集群，相比现有方法在服务吞吐量和成本效益上分别提升高达2.3倍和1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 disaggregation 方案粒度过粗且与特定模型架构紧耦合，限制了异构GPU集群的性能潜力，无法充分利用硬件能力。

Method: 提出内核级 disaggregation 系统 Tessera，该方法：(1) 从 PTX 中提取精确的内核间依赖关系以确保正确性；(2) 通过流水线执行模型重叠通信与计算；(3) 采用负载感知调度与轻量级运行时自适应，实现离线分析与在线适应的集成。

Result: 在5种异构GPU和4种模型架构上（扩展至16个GPU）的广泛评估表明，与现有方法相比，Tessera将服务吞吐量提升高达2.3倍，成本效益提升高达1.6倍，并能推广到先前方法不适用的模型架构。

Conclusion: 内核是匹配计算需求与硬件能力的最佳粒度；令人惊讶的是，在 Tessera 管理下，一对异构GPU甚至能以更低成本超越两个同构高端GPU的吞吐量。

Abstract: Disaggregation maps parts of an AI workload to different types of GPUs, offering a path to utilize modern heterogeneous GPU clusters. However, existing solutions operate at a coarse granularity and are tightly coupled to specific model architectures, leaving much room for performance improvement. This paper presents Tessera, the first kernel disaggregation system to improve performance and cost efficiency on heterogeneous GPUs for large model inference. Our key insight is that kernels within a single application exhibit diverse resource demands, making them the most suitable granularity for aligning computation with hardware capabilities. Tessera integrates offline analysis with online adaptation by extracting precise inter-kernel dependencies from PTX to ensure correctness, overlapping communication with computation through a pipelined execution model, and employing workload-aware scheduling with lightweight runtime adaptation. Extensive evaluations across five heterogeneous GPUs and four model architectures, scaling up to 16 GPUs, show that Tessera improves serving throughput and cost efficiency by up to 2.3x and 1.6x, respectively, compared to existing disaggregation methods, while generalizing to model architectures where prior approaches do not apply. Surprisingly, a heterogeneous GPU pair under Tessera can even exceed the throughput of two homogeneous high-end GPUs at a lower cost.

</details>


### [363] [RF-LEGO: Modularized Signal Processing-Deep Learning Co-Design for RF Sensing via Deep Unrolling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10183)
*Luca Jiang-Tao Yu,Chenshu Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出RF-LEGO框架，通过深度展开技术将可解释的信号处理算法转化为可训练的深度学习模块，实现了模块化、可级联且物理可解释的无线感知，在多种任务和数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度无线感知模型多为端到端、任务特定设计，缺乏可重用性和可解释性；而传统信号处理方法虽具可解释性但依赖人工调参、性能受限。如何结合两者优势，构建兼具高性能、可重用性与可解释性的无线感知模型是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出RF-LEGO模块化协同设计框架，利用深度展开（deep unrolling）技术，将传统信号处理算法中的手工调参替换为可学习参数，同时保留核心处理结构与数学算子。设计了三个深度展开模块：频率变换、空间角度估计和信号检测，确保模块的可级联性与结构对齐的可解释性。

Result: 在Wi-Fi、毫米波、UWB和6G感知的真实数据集上进行了广泛实验，RF-LEGO作为独立模块或集成到多个下游任务时，均显著优于现有信号处理和深度学习基线方法。

Conclusion: RF-LEGO开创了一种基于深度展开的信号处理-深度学习协同设计新范式，为构建高效、可解释的深度无线感知解决方案提供了新思路和有效途径。

Abstract: Wireless sensing, traditionally relying on signal processing (SP) techniques, has recently shifted toward data-driven deep learning (DL) to achieve performance breakthroughs. However, existing deep wireless sensing models are typically end-to-end and task-specific, lacking reusability and interpretability. We propose RF-LEGO, a modular co-design framework that transforms interpretable SP algorithms into trainable, physics-grounded DL modules through deep unrolling. By replacing hand-tuned parameters with learnable ones while preserving core processing structures and mathematical operators, RF-LEGO ensures modularity, cascadability, and structure-aligned interpretability. Specifically, we introduce three deep-unrolled modules for critical RF sensing tasks: frequency transform, spatial angle estimation, and signal detection. Extensive experiments using real-world data for Wi-Fi, millimeter-wave, UWB, and 6G sensing demonstrate that RF-LEGO significantly outperforms existing SP and DL baselines, both standalone and when integrated into multiple downstream tasks. RF-LEGO pioneers a novel SP-DL co-design paradigm for wireless sensing via deep unrolling, shedding light on efficient and interpretable deep wireless sensing solutions. Our code is available at https://github.com/aiot-lab/RF-LEGO.

</details>


### [364] [Verifying In-Network Computing Systems for Design Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10186)
*Tianyu Bai,Ying Zhang,Ying Zhang,Wenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了INCGuard，这是首个用于验证网内计算（INC）系统的通用工具，通过高级规范语言和模型检测技术，成功识别并验证了七个INC系统中的潜在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
