Motivation: 网内计算虽然提升了网络性能，但破坏了传统网络的透明性，导致数据包丢失和乱序等异常可能破坏应用属性（如缓存一致性和锁互斥）。常规测试无法穷尽这些异常，影响了INC系统在工业界的部署信任度。

Method: INCGuard提供高级规范语言（平均节省67.2%代码量），允许开发者表达INC特有的正确性属性；将系统转换为状态转移表示，执行模型检测以发现设计风险，并报告违规追踪。针对INC场景优化以缓解状态空间爆炸问题。

Result: 成功建模并验证了七个INC系统，在数秒内识别出潜在风险，并在真实系统中复现了这些风险，验证了检测结果的正确性。

Conclusion: INCGuard有效解决了INC系统验证难题，提升了系统正确性保障，为INC技术在工业界的部署提供了有力支持。

Abstract: The emergence of programmable switches has brought in-network computing (INC) into the spotlight in recent years. By offloading computation directly onto the data transmission process, INC improves network utilization, reduces latency to sub-RTT levels, saves link bandwidth, and maintains throughput. However, INC disrupts the transparency of traditional networks, forcing developers to consider network exceptions like packet loss and out-of-order. If not properly handled, these exceptions can lead to violations of application properties, such as cache consistency and lock exclusion. Usual testing cannot exhaustively cover these exceptions, raising doubts about the correctness of INC systems and hindering their deployment in the industry. This paper presents INCGuard, the first general-purpose tool for verifying INC systems. INCGuard provides a high-level specification language and saves developers 67.2% lines of code on average. To help better understand the behavior of the system, INCGuard offers configurable network environments. INCGuard enables developers to express INC-specific correctness properties. INCGuard translates developer-specified systems into state transition representations, performs model checking to detect potential design risks, and reports violation traces to developers. We propose optimizations for INC-specific scenarios to address the challenge of state space explosion. We modeled seven INC systems and identified their risks with INCGuard in seconds. We further reproduce them in real systems to confirm the validity of our verification result.

</details>


### [365] [Icicle: Scalable Metadata Indexing and Real-Time Monitoring for HPC File Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10295)
*Haochen Pan,Ryan Chard,Song Young Oh,Maxime Gonthier,Valérie Hayot-Sasson,Geoffrey Lentner,Joe Bottigliero,Rachana Ananthakrishnan,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Icicle，一个可扩展的文件系统元数据持续索引与监控框架，通过结合批量快照和实时事件流摄入，解决了超大规模HPC文件系统中传统工具无法高效查询和监控的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代HPC文件系统包含数十亿文件和数百PB数据，传统文件系统工具（如find、du）无法扩展至该规模，现有的外部索引工具（如GUFI、Brindexer）虽提升了查询性能，但仍以批处理为主，难以适应异构、快速变化的环境，缺乏实时性和持续监控能力。

Method: 构建了基于Apache Kafka和Apache Flink的Icicle框架，采用混合摄入策略：结合周期性快照摄入进行批量元数据更新，以及基于事件的摄入实现来自Lustre和IBM Storage Scale等生产系统的实时同步。系统将元数据事件摄入到两个互补的搜索索引中，分别支持个体文件发现和按用户、组、目录的聚合统计摘要。

Result: 在生产级HPC数据集上的实验评估表明，相较于现有监控和索引方法，Icicle实现了数量级的吞吐量提升，并提供可调选项以平衡一致性、延迟和元数据新鲜度。该架构能够高效支持针对数十亿对象的粗粒度管理查询和交互式分析。

Conclusion: Icicle成功为超大规模HPC文件系统提供了一个统一、实时更新、可查询的状态视图，在保持高吞吐量、容错性和水平扩展能力的同时，有效解决了大规模文件系统元数据索引和持续监控的可扩展性难题。

Abstract: Modern HPC file systems can contain billions of files and hundreds of petabytes of data, making even simple questions increasingly intractable to answer. Traditional file system utilities such as find and du fail to scale to these sizes. While external indexing tools like GUFI and Brindexer improve query performance, they remain batch-oriented and unsuitable for heterogeneous, rapidly evolving environments.
  We present Icicle, a scalable framework for continuous file system metadata indexing and monitoring. Icicle maintains a unified, up-to-date, and queryable view of file system state while supporting both periodic snapshot-based ingestion for bulk metadata updates and event-based ingestion for real-time synchronization from production systems such as Lustre and IBM Storage Scale. Built on Apache Kafka and Apache Flink, Icicle provides high-throughput, fault-tolerant, and horizontally scalable ingestion of metadata events into two complementary search indexes, enabling both individual file discovery and aggregate summary statistics by user, group, and directory.
  This architecture enables efficient support for both coarse-grained administrative queries and interactive analytics over billions of objects. Our experimental evaluation on production-scale HPC datasets demonstrates order-of-magnitude throughput improvements over existing monitoring and indexing approaches, with tunable options for balancing consistency, latency, and metadata freshness.

</details>


### [366] [Leveraging Mathematical Reasoning of LLMs for Efficient GPU Thread Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10387)
*Jose Maureira,Cristóbal A. Navarro,Hector Ferrada,Luis Veas-Castillo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）的符号推理能力，自动化推导GPU非矩形域线程映射函数。现代开源LLM能成功推断复杂2D/3D域的精确映射方程，表现远超传统符号回归方法。虽一次性推导能耗较高，但生成的优化内核能带来数千倍的加速与能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPU计算中，将并行线程映射到非矩形域是一项已知挑战，若能高效处理可避免因分配不必要计算资源而导致的严重性能损失。目前实现最优效率需要大量人工时间和精力为每种特定几何形状手动推导定制映射函数。研究旨在自动化这一数学推导过程。

Method: 提出一种新颖方法，利用大语言模型（LLM）的符号推理能力，通过上下文学习完全自动化数学推导过程。聚焦最先进的开源权重模型，在复杂度递增的空间域上进行了严格的比较分析。同时对高性能基础设施上的能耗可行性进行了分析。

Result: 现代本地LLM成功为复杂2D/3D密集域和2D分形推断出精确的O(1)和O(log N)映射方程，大幅超越传统符号回归方法（后者在此离散任务上系统性地失败）。生成的分析内核完全消除了块浪费，在实际GPU工作负载中带来高达4833倍加速和2890倍能耗降低。但在高度递归的3D分形（如门格海绵）上存在"推理上限"。

Conclusion: 该方法虽一次性方程推导会产生较高能耗（尤其是推理型模型如DeepSeek-R1），但这是单次前期投入。一旦集成，后续可产生巨大的重复性能耗和时间节省。当前模型在高度递归3D分形上的局限为开源架构的成熟度建立了清晰基准，为全自动、高能效GPU资源优化描绘了一条可行且自主的路径。

Abstract: Mapping parallel threads onto non-box-shaped domains is a known challenge in GPU computing that, if done efficiently, can prevent severe performance penalties from allocating unnecessary computational resources. Currently, achieving this optimal efficiency requires significant analytical human time and effort to manually derive bespoke mapping functions for each specific geometry. This work introduces a novel approach leveraging the symbolic reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate this mathematical derivation process entirely through in-context learning. Focusing on state-of-the-art open-weights models, we conducted a rigorous comparative analysis across spatial domains of increasing complexity. Our results demonstrate that modern local LLMs successfully infer exact O(1) and O(log N) mapping equations for complex 2D/3D dense domains and 2D fractals, vastly outperforming traditional symbolic regression methods which systematically failed at this discrete task. Crucially, we profile the energetic viability of this approach on high-performance infrastructure, drawing a clear distinction between the code-generation and execution phases. While the one-time inference of the equation incurs a high energy penalty -- particularly for reasoning-focused models like DeepSeek-R1 -- this is a single upfront investment. Once integrated, the generated analytical kernels eliminate block waste entirely, yielding massive repeated energy and time savings (e.g., up to 4833x speedup and 2890x energy reduction) during actual GPU workloads. Finally, we identify a current "reasoning ceiling" when these models face highly recursive 3D fractals tested in this work (e.g., the Menger Sponge). This limitation establishes a clear benchmark for the maturity of open-weight architectures, charting a viable and sovereign path toward fully automated, energy-efficient GPU resource optimization.

</details>


### [367] [CIR: Lightweight Container Image for Cross-Platform Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10411)
*Fengzhi Li,Xiaohui Peng,Qingru Xu,Qisong Shi,Tuo Zhou,Yongxuan Dai,Yifan Wang,Ninghui Sun,Zhiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CIR（容器中间表示），一种面向解释型语言应用的新型容器镜像格式，通过延迟构建策略将平台特定构建推迟到部署阶段，实现跨平台部署，镜像体积减少95%，部署时间减少40-60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统容器镜像需要为每个目标部署平台构建和维护单独的镜像，对于Python和R等解释型语言应用而言，虽然代码本身跨平台，但运行时依赖高度依赖平台。随着天空计算和边缘计算等新兴计算范式的出现，跨平台部署和工作负载迁移需求日益增长，传统镜像方式在存储和网络使用方面效率低下，并给开发者带来沉重负担。

Method: 提出一种延迟构建（lazy-build）方法，将平台特定构建延迟到部署阶段。引入新的镜像格式CIR（容器中间表示），包含预构建器（pre-builder）和延迟构建器（lazy-builder）。CIR仅存储应用程序直接依赖的标识符，将平台适配工作交给延迟构建器，在部署时将实际依赖组装成可运行的容器。

Result: CIR镜像体积相比传统打包所有依赖的镜像减少95%；与预构建镜像相比，部署时间减少40-60%；在性能上优于Docker、Buildah和Apptainer等最先进的系统。

Conclusion: CIR通过延迟构建策略成功实现了容器镜像的跨平台部署能力，显著减少了镜像体积和部署时间，为解释型语言应用在异构分布式基础设施中的部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: In modern cloud and heterogeneous distributed infrastructures, container images are widely used as the deployment unit for machine learning applications. An image bundles the application with its entire platform-specific execution environment and can be directly launched into a container instance. However, this approach forces developers to build and maintain separate images for each target deployment platform. This limitation is particularly evident for widely used interpreted languages such as Python and R in data analytics and machine learning, where application code is inherently cross-platform, yet the runtime dependencies are highly platform-specific. With emerging computing paradigms such as sky computing and edge computing, which demand seamless workload migration and cross-platform deployment, traditional images not only introduce inefficiencies in storage and network usage, but also impose substantial burdens on developers, who must repeatedly craft and manage platform-specific builds.
  To address these challenges, we propose a lazy-build approach that defers platform-specific construction to the deployment stage, thus keeping the image itself cross-platform. To enable this, we introduce a new image format, CIR (Container Intermediate Representation), together with its pre-builder and lazy-builder. CIR targets interpreted-language applications and only stores the identifiers of the application's direct dependencies, leaving platform adaptation to the lazy-builder, which at deployment time assembles the actual dependencies into runnable containers. A single CIR can therefore be deployed across heterogeneous platforms while reducing image size by 95% compared to conventional images that bundle all dependencies. In our evaluation, CIR reduces deployment time by 40-60% compared with pre-built images, outperforming state-of-the-art systems such as Docker, Buildah, and Apptainer.

</details>


### [368] [FEDBUD: Joint Incentive and Privacy Optimization for Resource-Constrained Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10499)
*Tao Liu,Xuehe Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了FEDBUD系统，一种基于两阶段Stackelberg博弈的联邦学习隐私感知激励机制，联合考虑数据量和噪声水平，实现了云服务器与边缘节点的双赢策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在隐私保护和边缘计算领域应用广泛，但在防御差分攻击和设计激励机制方面仍面临瓶颈。现有工作较少同时考虑数据量和噪声水平对激励策略的影响。

Method: 提出FEDBUD联邦学习系统，将隐私和经济因素相结合，建模为两阶段Stackelberg博弈。云服务器控制对边缘节点的支付，边缘节点控制数据量和噪声水平，利用平均场估计器和虚拟队列推导纳什均衡，确定双方的最优策略。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验结果表明，FEDBUD系统具有优越的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过联合考虑数据量和噪声水平对激励策略的影响，FEDBUD系统成功平衡了隐私保护与经济效益，为联邦学习中的隐私感知激励机制设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning has become a popular paradigm for privacy protection and edge-based machine learning. However, defending against differential attacks and devising incentive strategies remain significant bottlenecks in this field. Despite recent works on privacy-aware incentive mechanism design for federated learning, few of them consider both data volume and noise level. In this paper, we propose a novel federated learning system called FEDBUD, which combines privacy and economic concerns together by considering the joint influence of data volume and noise level on incentive strategy determination. In this system, the cloud server controls monetary payments to edge nodes, while edge nodes control data volume and noise level that potentially impact the model performance of the cloud server. To determine the mutually optimal strategies for both sides, we model FEDBUD as a two-stage Stackelberg Game and derive the Nash Equilibrium using the mean-field estimator and virtual queue. Experimental results on real-world datasets demonstrate the outstanding performance of FEDBUD.

</details>


### [369] [Understanding Communication Backends in Cross-Silo Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10859)
*Amir Ziashahabi,Chaoyang He,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对跨孤岛联邦学习，深入基准测试了MPI、gRPC和PyTorch RPC等通信后端，并提出gRPC+S3混合后端，在地理分布式部署中实现高达3.8倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习（FL）应用场景多样，网络配置差异大，且大模型需求日益增长。选择和配置合适的通信后端对确保FL系统最优性能至关重要，但目前缺乏针对跨孤岛FL通信后端的深入基准测试与指导。

Method: 针对跨孤岛联邦学习，对MPI、gRPC和PyTorch RPC等多种通信后端进行深入基准测试。提出gRPC+S3混合后端以克服现有方法在地理分布式部署中传输大模型的局限。测试覆盖多种模型规模，并在现实网络条件下评估点对点和端到端性能。

Result: 提出的gRPC+S3混合后端在地理分布式部署中传输大模型时，相比gRPC实现了高达3.8倍的端到端加速。基准测试覆盖了多种模型规模，并在现实网络条件下评估了各后端的点对点和端到端性能。

Conclusion: 研究为根据具体的联邦学习任务和网络配置选择及配置合适的通信后端提供了实用见解和指导，有助于提升跨孤岛联邦学习系统的性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has emerged as a practical means for privacy-preserving distributed machine learning. FL's versatile design makes it suitable for various training settings, from IoT edge devices in cross-device FL to powerful servers in cross-silo FL. A key consequence of this versatility is the high level of diversity found in the networking configuration of FL applications. Coupled with the rising demand for large-scale models such as large language models, well-informed selection and configuration of communication backends become crucial for ensuring optimal performance in FL systems. This work focuses on cross-silo federated learning, presenting in-depth benchmarks of various communication backends, including MPI, gRPC, and PyTorch RPC. In addition, we introduce gRPC+S3, a hybrid backend designed to overcome the limitations of existing approaches, particularly for transmitting large models across geo-distributed deployments, achieving up to $3.8\times$ end-to-end speedup over gRPC. Our benchmarks examine point-to-point and end-to-end performance for a broad range of model sizes running under realistic network conditions. Our findings provide practical insights for selecting and configuring suitable communication backends tailored to the specific federated learning tasks and network configurations.

</details>


### [370] [NimbusGuard: A Novel Framework for Proactive Kubernetes Autoscaling Using Deep Q-Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11017)
*Chamath Wanigasooriya,Indrajith Ekanayake*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了NimbusGuard，一个基于深度强化学习和LSTM的Kubernetes主动扩缩容系统，相比传统被动扩缩容方法，在性能和成本效率上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Kubernetes扩缩容控制器是被动响应式的，仅在检测到集群内需求后才调整资源，不包含任何预测措施。这可能导致过度配置（增加成本）或配置不足（性能下降），无法充分利用云环境的弹性优势。

Method: 提出NimbusGuard开源系统，基于Kubernetes构建。核心是利用深度强化学习（DRL）智能体实现主动扩缩容，并使用长短期记忆网络（LSTM）预测未来工作负载模式来增强智能体的感知能力。

Result: 通过将NimbusGuard与内置的水平Pod扩缩容器（HPA）和事件驱动扩缩容器KEDA进行对比评估，实验结果表明NimbusGuard的主动框架在性能和成本效率方面均优于现有的被动方法。

Conclusion: NimbusGuard成功证明了在Kubernetes环境中引入预测机制的主动扩缩容方法能够有效提升资源管理效率，在保证性能的同时优化成本，为云原生应用提供了更优的扩缩容解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud native architecture is about building and running scalable microservice applications to take full advantage of the cloud environments. Managed Kubernetes is the powerhouse orchestrating cloud native applications with elastic scaling. However, traditional Kubernetes autoscalers are reactive, meaning the scaling controllers adjust resources only after they detect demand within the cluster and do not incorporate any predictive measures. This can lead to either over-provisioning and increased costs or under-provisioning and performance degradation. We propose NimbusGuard, an open-source, Kubernetes-based autoscaling system that leverages a deep reinforcement learning agent to provide proactive autoscaling. The agents perception is augmented by a Long Short-Term Memory model that forecasts future workload patterns. The evaluations were conducted by comparing NimbusGuard against the built-in scaling controllers, such as Horizontal Pod Autoscaler, and the event-driven autoscaler KEDA. The experimental results demonstrate how NimbusGuard's proactive framework translates into superior performance and cost efficiency compared to existing reactive methods.

</details>


### [371] [Record-Remix-Replay: Hierarchical GPU Kernel Optimization using Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11109)
*Daniel Nichols,Konstantinos Parasyris,Caetano Melone,Tal Ben-Nun,Giorgis Georgakoudis,Harshitha Menon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了R^3框架，通过结合LLM演化搜索、贝叶斯优化和记录-重放编译技术，实现跨源代码实现、编译器优化和运行时配置的GPU内核端到端自动优化，优于传统方法且搜索速度快近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU硬件快速迭代，高性能计算和AI应用需要大量人工调优才能适配新架构。现有优化方法只能独立搜索部分优化空间（如启动配置或编译器设置），无法有效覆盖算法设计、源码实现、编译器标志与pass序列、内核启动参数等全维度优化空间，亟需一种端到端的自动优化解决方案。

Method: 提出Record-Remix-Replay (R^3)分层优化框架，整合三大技术：(1) LLM驱动的演化搜索，(2) 贝叶斯优化，(3) 记录-重放编译技术。该框架能够从源码级实现选择到编译器pass排序和运行时配置进行快速、可扩展的候选评估，实现端到端的跨维度联合优化搜索。

Result: 实验表明，R^3框架在完整科学应用优化上优于传统的内核参数和编译器标志优化方法，同时相比现代演化搜索方法，优化速度提升了近一个数量级。该方法成功实现了对通常被分开处理的多个优化维度的联合搜索。

Conclusion: R^3框架通过整合LLM、贝叶斯优化和编译技术，有效解决了GPU内核多维度优化空间联合搜索的挑战，为科学应用在新硬件架构上的快速适配提供了实用的自动化解决方案，显著降低了调优时间和人力成本。

Abstract: As high-performance computing and AI workloads become increasingly dependent on GPUs, maintaining high performance across rapidly evolving hardware generations has become a major challenge. Developers often spend months tuning scientific applications to fully exploit new architectures, navigating a complex optimization space that spans algorithm design, source implementation, compiler flags and pass sequences, and kernel launch parameters. Existing approaches can effectively search parts of this space in isolation, such as launch configurations or compiler settings, but optimizing across the full space still requires substantial human expertise and iterative manual effort.
  In this paper, we present Record-Remix-Replay (R^3), a hierarchical optimization framework that combines LLM-driven evolutionary search, Bayesian optimization, and record-replay compilation techniques to efficiently explore GPU kernel optimizations from source-level implementation choices down to compiler pass ordering and runtime configuration. By making candidate evaluation fast and scalable, our approach enables practical end-to-end search over optimization dimensions that are typically treated separately. We show that Record-Remix-Replay can optimize full scientific applications better than traditional approaches over kernel parameters and compiler flags, while also being nearly an order of magnitude faster than modern evolutionary search approaches.

</details>


### [372] [Characterizing the Impact of Congestion in Modern HPC Interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11432)
*Lorenzo Piarulli,Marco Faltelli,Dirk Pleiter,Karthee Sivalingam,Dancheng Zhang,Kexue Zhao,Matteo Turisini,Francesco Iannone,Aldo Artigiani,Daniele De Sensi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对EDR/HDR/NDR InfiniBand、Cray Slingshot及新兴以太网 fabric 等四大类HPC互连架构的拥塞行为进行了全面评估，揭示了稳态与突发流量下的性能差异及其随系统规模变化的特性，为未来系统设计与优化提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高性能计算（HPC）系统日益支持大规模AI训练与模拟，混合工作负载导致网络流量异构，网络拥塞成为关键瓶颈。理解现代互连技术对拥塞的响应，对设定合理性能预期与指导系统设计至关重要。

Method: 在四种主流HPC fabric（EDR/HDR/NDR InfiniBand、Cray Slingshot及新兴以太网）上系统评估稳态拥塞与多维度突发模式（持续时间、强度、暂停时间），覆盖不同系统规模，分析拥塞行为与规模依赖特性。

Result: 揭示了不同互连架构在稳态与突发负载下的拥塞响应差异，识别出影响集合通信性能的规模相关行为，系统梳理了各种应力条件下出现的挑战与潜在优化方向。

Conclusion: 研究结果为HPC架构师与研究人员提供了对现有网络技术拥塞行为的实践性理解，有助于设计更高效的拥塞控制机制与网络负载均衡策略，从而优化未来大规模系统性能。

Abstract: High-performance computing (HPC) systems increasingly support both scalable AI training and large-scale simulation workloads. Both typically rely heavily on collective communication operations. On modern supercomputers, however, network congestion has emerged as a major limitation, driven by heterogeneous traffic patterns resulting from diverse workload mixes. As system scale and active users continue to grow, understanding how today's interconnect technologies respond to congestion is essential for establishing realistic performance expectations and informing future system design. This paper presents a comprehensive characterization of congestion behavior across four major HPC fabrics: EDR InfiniBand, HDR InfiniBand, NDR InfiniBand, Cray Slingshot, and emerging Ethernet fabrics. These fabrics span high-performance proprietary interconnects as well as adaptive Ethernet-based designs aligned with emerging standards such as Ultra Ethernet. We evaluate their responses to both steady congestion and a wide range of bursty patterns that vary in duration, intensity, and pause length, capturing the bursty communication typical of AI workloads. Our study covers multiple scales, examining how congestion manifests differently as system size increases and identifying scale-dependent behaviors that influence collective performance. By analyzing the challenges that arise under these controlled stress conditions, we aim to provide a practical overview of congestion issues and possible optimizations. The insights derived from this evaluation can guide researchers and HPC architects in designing more effective congestion-control mechanisms and network load-balancing strategies.

</details>


### [373] [OpenDT: Exploring Datacenter Performance and Sustainability with a Self-Calibrating Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11445)
*Radu Nicolae,Jules van der Toorn,Stavriana Kraniti,Houcen Liu,Alexandru Iosup*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenDT，首个用于数据中心运营的开源数字孪生系统，通过持续集成周期实现监控与运营，实验证明其在线重校准能将数字孪生精度（MAPE）从现有工作的7.86%提升至4.39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心运营面临诸多挑战，尽管数字孪生技术在航空和自动驾驶领域已成功应用，但面向数据中心的开源数字孪生系统尚未向社区展示。研究旨在填补这一空白，使数字孪生成为数据中心运营的主要工具，辅助重大运营决策并在人机协同下实时调整ICT基础设施。

Method: 设计并实现OpenDT系统，采用持续集成周期架构，包含三个核心阶段：(1)实时连续遥测数据采集；(2)基于物理ICT实时遥测的离散事件仿真，具备自校准能力；(3)面向SLO且经人工审批的反馈机制至物理ICT。系统遵循FAIR/FOSS原则并开源。

Result: 通过主要覆盖周期第1、2阶段的原型进行轨迹驱动实验，结果表明：(i) OpenDT能够复现同行评审实验并扩展性能与能效分析；(ii) 在线重校准显著提升数字孪生精度，MAPE从现有工作的7.86%降至4.39%。

Conclusion: OpenDT作为首个开源数据中心数字孪生系统，成功验证了数字孪生技术在数据中心运营中的可行性与优越性，为社区提供了可复用、可扩展的研究平台，推动了数据中心智能化运营的发展。

Abstract: Datacenters are the backbone of our digital society, but raise numerous operational challenges. We envision digital twins becoming primary instruments in datacenter operations, continuously and autonomously helping with major operational decisions and with adapting ICT infrastructure, live, with a human-in-the-loop. Although fields such as aviation and autonomous driving successfully employ digital twins, an open-source digital twin for datacenters has not been demonstrated to the community. Addressing this challenge, we design, implement, and experiment using OpenDT, an Open-source, Digital Twin for monitoring and operating datacenters through a continuous integration cycle that includes: (1) live and continuous telemetry data; (2) discrete-event simulation using live telemetry from the physical ICT, with self-calibration; and (3) SLO-aware and human-approved feedback to physical ICT. Through trace-driven experiments with a prototype mainly covering stages 1 and 2 of the cycle, we show that (i) OpenDT can be used to reproduce peer-reviewed experiments and extend the analysis with performance and energy-efficiency results; (ii) OpenDT's online re-calibration can increase digital-twinning accuracy, quantified to a MAPE of 4.39% vs. 7.86% in peer-reviewed work. OpenDT adheres to FAIR/FOSS principles and is available at: https://github.com/atlarge-research/opendt/tree/hcp.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [374] [ML-Based Real-Time Downlink Performance Prediction in Standalone 5G NR Using Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09632)
*Md Mahfuzur Rahman,Jareen Shuva,Nishith Tripathi,Jeffrey H. Reed,Lingjia Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于机器学习的框架，利用商用现成UE设备采集的实时测量数据，实现了对5G网络下行链路吞吐量和误块率的高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G网络性能预测对于网络优化和用户体验保障至关重要，但现有研究多依赖仿真或专用设备，缺乏基于商用现成（COTS）用户设备在真实场景下的实时性能预测研究。

Method: 搭建了基于srsRAN 5G NR协议栈的实验平台（3.4 GHz频段），使用两台Google Pixel 7a智能手机采集物理层特征（CQI、MCS、比特率、TTI、BLER）作为预测变量。在LOS和nLOS条件下进行静止和移动性测量，测试数据涵盖全球Ookla服务器、iperf TCP/UDP流量及YouTube视频流，并包含多UE干扰场景。评估了五种监督回归模型：线性回归、决策树、随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM。

Result: 实验结果表明，利用COTS硬件和标准机器学习技术，能够在多样化的真实5G场景中准确预测吞吐量和BLER。

Conclusion: 证明了基于商用现成设备和标准机器学习方法进行5G网络下行性能预测的可行性和有效性，为5G网络实时性能监测和优化提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a machine learning (ML)-based framework for downlink performance prediction in 5G networks using real-time measurements from commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) user equipment (UE). Our experimental platform integrates the srsRAN 5G New Radio (NR) stack deployed on a Dell desktop serving as the 5G next generation nodeB (gNB), operating at 3.4 GHz. Two Google Pixel 7a smartphones are used to collect physical layer characteristics such as channel quality indicator (CQI), modulation and coding scheme (MCS), bit rate, transmission time interval (TTI), and block error rate (BLER), which are leveraged as predictors in model training. We use commercial-grade traffic generation tools, including Ookla, for stationary and mobility measurements under line-of-sight (LOS) and non-line-of-sight (nLOS) conditions. Test data includes global Ookla servers (e.g., USA, Portugal, Ghana, Egypt, Japan), iperf TCP/UDP data, and video streaming sessions from YouTube. To analyze inter-user interference, we also include scenarios with multiple UEs at the same location. We evaluate the predictive performance of five supervised regression models - linear regression, decision tree regression, random forest regression, extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost), light gradient boosting machine (LightGBM). Our results demonstrate that throughput and BLER can be accurately predicted using COTS hardware and standard ML techniques in diverse real-world 5G scenarios.

</details>


### [375] [NetAgentBench: A State-Centric Benchmark for Evaluating Agentic Network Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09678)
*Ahmed Twabi,Yepeng Ding,Tohru Kondo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了NetAgentBench，一个基于有限状态机形式化的动态基准测试框架，用于评估LLM代理在网络管理任务中的多轮交互表现，发现现有模型在专家级配置任务中存在严重的探索失败和一致性崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理网络管理的普及，现有的静态、一次性测试评估框架已无法满足需求，亟需能够评估复杂、多轮操作行为的动态基准测试框架。

Method: 引入NetAgentBench动态基准测试，采用有限状态机(FSM)形式化方法，保证评估的确定性、正确性和有界执行，从而为网络领域提供测量复杂多轮操作行为的严格基础。

Result: 对四个最先进的LLM代理进行多样化网络配置任务的实证评估，发现代理虽能解决基础任务，但在专家级配置过程中存在严重的探索失败(exploration meltdowns)和一致性崩溃(coherence collapse)问题。

Conclusion: 系统性地评估多轮行为稳定性是实现可信赖、完全自主网络不可或缺的步骤，NetAgentBench为此提供了重要的评估基础。

Abstract: As agentic network management gains popularity, there is a critical need for evaluation frameworks that transcend static, one-shot testing. To address this, we introduce NetAgentBench, a dynamic benchmark that evaluates agent interactions through a Finite State Machine (FSM) formalization guaranteeing determinism, correctness, and bounded execution. This provides the networking landscape with a rigorous foundation to measure complex, multi-turn operational behaviors. Our empirical evaluation of four state-of-the-art LLM agents through diverse network configuration tasks reveals stark deficiencies: while agents can solve basic tasks, they suffer severe exploration meltdowns and coherence collapse during expert-level configurations. Ultimately, NetAgentBench demonstrates that systematically evaluating multi-turn behavioral stability is an indispensable step toward realizing trustworthy, fully autonomous networks.

</details>


### [376] [Hybrid Hierarchical Federated Learning over 5G/NextG Wireless Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09680)
*Haiyun Liu,Jiahao Xue,Jie Xu,Yao Liu,Zhuo Lu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对现代无线网络中CoMP技术与传统HFL架构不兼容的问题，本文提出混合分层联邦学习（HHFL），允许客户端同时连接多个边缘服务器进行模型聚合。实验证明，该方法在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据场景下，收敛速度提升高达2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的5G及下一代无线网络正采用协作多点（CoMP）技术，允许客户端同时由多个基站服务。然而，传统的分层联邦学习（HFL）架构限制每个客户端同一时间只能关联一个边缘服务器。若继续沿用传统架构，将无法利用CoMP技术的优势，导致训练效率提升空间受限。

Method: 提出混合分层联邦学习（HHFL）架构。该架构突破了传统单一关联限制，允许处于重叠区域的客户端同时与多个边缘服务器（ESs）通信以进行模型聚合。通过增强边缘服务器间的知识共享，HHFL能够有效缓解模型发散问题。作者还提供了严格的理论收敛分析及收敛上界以验证方法的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，HHFL优于传统HFL，尤其是在跨边缘服务器数据呈非独立同分布（non-IID）的情况下表现突出。在特定设定下（每个ES仅主导10个类别中的2个，且57个客户端中有15个可连接多个ES），HHFL在相同配置下的收敛速度提升高达2倍。

Conclusion: HHFL为当前及下一代无线网络中的联邦学习模型训练提供了一种可扩展且高效的解决方案，成功将无线网络的CoMP技术优势融入联邦学习训练过程，有效提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Today's 5G and NextG wireless networks are moving toward using the coordinated multi-point (CoMP) transmission and reception technique, where a client can be simultaneously served by multiple base stations (BSs) for better communication performance. However, traditional hierarchical federated learning (HFL) architectures impose the constraint that each client can be associated with only one edge server (ES) at a time. If we keep using the traditional HFL architectures in modern hierarchical networks for model training, the benefits of the CoMP technique would remain unexploited and leave room for further improvements in training efficiency. To address this issue, we propose hybrid hierarchical federated learning (HHFL), which allows clients in overlapping regions to simultaneously communicate with multiple edge servers (ESs) for model aggregation. HHFL is able to enhance inter-ES knowledge sharing, thereby mitigating model divergence and improving training efficiency. We provide a rigorous theoretical convergence analysis with a convergence upper bound to validate its effectiveness. Experimental results show that HHFL outperforms traditional HFL, particularly when the data across different ESs is not independent and identically distributed (non-IID). For example, when each ES is dominated by only two of the ten classes and 15 out of the 57 clients can connect to multiple ESs, HHFL achieves up to 2x faster convergence under the same configuration. These results demonstrate that HHFL provides a scalable and efficient solution for FL model training in today's and NextG wireless networks.

</details>


### [377] [R2E-VID: Two-Stage Robust Routing via Temporal Gating for Elastic Edge-Cloud Video Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09681)
*Zheming Yang,Lulu Zuo,Shun Lu,Yangyu Zhang,Zhicheng Li,Xiangyang Li,Yang You*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了R2E-VID框架，一种通过时间门控机制实现的弹性边缘-云视频推理鲁棒路由方法，显著降低了成本和延迟并提升了推理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模视频分析应用的快速增长，边缘-云协同系统已成为实时推理的主流范式。然而，现有方法无法动态适应异构的视频内容和波动的资源条件，导致路由效率低下和计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出R2E-VID两阶段鲁棒路由框架：第一阶段引入时间门控机制，对输入视频流的时间一致性和运动动态进行建模，预测每个片段的最优路由模式，实现边缘和云节点之间推理工作负载的自适应划分；第二阶段通过鲁棒路由优化模块，利用多模型适应细化分配，在动态网络和工作负载变化下联合最小化推理延迟和资源消耗。

Result: 在公共数据集上的大量实验表明，与以云为中心的基线相比，R2E-VID将总体成本降低了高达60%；与最先进的边缘-云解决方案相比，延迟降低了35-45%，推理精度提高了2-7%。

Conclusion: R2E-VID框架有效解决了边缘-云视频推理中的动态适应性问题，实现了细粒度的时空弹性，在降低成本和延迟的同时提升了推理准确性。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of large-scale video analytics applications, edge-cloud collaborative systems have become the dominant paradigm for real-time inference. However, existing approaches often fail to dynamically adapt to heterogeneous video content and fluctuating resource conditions, resulting in suboptimal routing efficiency and high computational costs. In this paper, we propose R2E-VID, a two-stage robust routing framework via temporal gating for elastic edge-cloud video inference. In the first stage, R2E-VID introduces a temporal gating mechanism that models the temporal consistency and motion dynamics of incoming video streams to predict the optimal routing pattern for each segment. This enables adaptive partitioning of inference workloads between edge and cloud nodes, achieving fine-grained spatiotemporal elasticity. In the second stage, a robust routing optimization module refines the allocation through multi-model adaptation, jointly minimizing inference delay and resource consumption under dynamic network and workload variations. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate that R2E-VID achieves up to 60% reduction in overall cost compared to cloud-centric baselines, and delivers 35-45% lower delay while improving inference accuracy by 2-7% over state-of-the-art edge-cloud solutions.

</details>


### [378] [Decision-Theoretic Safety Assessment of Persona-Driven Multi-Agent Systems in O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09682)
*Zeinab Nezami,Syed Ali Raza Zaidi,Maryam Hafeez,Louis Powell,Vara Prasad Talari,Mallik Tatipamula*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于角色的多智能体框架，用于Open RAN中的自主网络管理，并开发了三维评估框架来验证系统性能，发现角色配置显著影响个体性能和多智能体协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统采用同质化策略，且缺乏系统性的部署前验证机制，难以在Open RAN自主网络管理中处理相互冲突的优化目标。

Method: 1. 提出角色驱动的多智能体框架，通过可配置的行为角色（包含优化优先级、风险容忍度和决策风格）影响五个专业智能体（规划、协调、资源分配、代码生成、分析）；2. 建立基于决策理论的三维评估框架，测量规范性合规性、规范性一致性和行为动态；3. 在两个ORAN优化挑战（节能资源分配和网络负载均衡）上评估486种角色配置。

Result: 1. 角色与智能体的对齐显著影响个体性能（提升14.3%）和涌现的多智能体协调；2. 检索架构（GraphRAG vs. RAG）从根本上约束定制化效果；3. 单智能体角色修改通过级联效应传播至整个系统；4. 某些角色组合存在可检测的根本不兼容性。

Conclusion: 该框架为在关键任务电信基础设施中部署基于LLM的自动化提供了系统性的验证机制，有助于实现更可靠的自主网络管理。

Abstract: Autonomous network management in Open Radio Access Networks requires intelligent decision making across conflicting objectives, yet existing LLM based multi agent systems employ homogeneous strategies and lack systematic predeployment validation. We introduce a persona driven multi agent framework where configurable behavioral personas structured specifications encoding optimization priorities, risk tolerance, and decision making style influence five specialized agents (planning, coordination, resource allocation, code generation, analysis). To enable rigorous validation, we develop a three dimensional evaluation framework grounded in decision theory, measuring normative compliance (optimality adherence), prescriptive alignment (behavioral guideline consistency), and behavioral dynamics (emergent system properties). We evaluate 486 persona configurations across two ORAN optimization challenges (energy efficient resource allocation and network load balancing). Results demonstrate that persona agent alignment significantly impacts both individual performance (14.3 percent) and emergent multi agent coordination, with retrieval architecture (GraphRAG vs. RAG) fundamentally constraining customization effectiveness. Single agent persona modifications propagate system wide through cascading effects, with certain combinations exhibiting detectable fundamental incompatibilities. Our framework provides systematic validation mechanisms for deploying LLM based automation in mission critical telecommunications infrastructure.

</details>


### [379] [Cayley Graph Optimization for Scalable Multi-Agent Communication Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09703)
*Jingkai Luo,Yulin Shao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出CayleyTopo，一种通过强化学习优化的循环Cayley图族，用于解决大规模多智能体通信的可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多智能体通信面临可扩展性瓶颈：全连接网络需要二次复杂度，而现有的稀疏拓扑依赖手工设计的规则，缺乏优化。

Method: 将通信图本身作为设计变量，提出基于循环Cayley图的CayleyTopo。开发轻量级强化学习框架，引入数论先验以偏好结构丰富的生成元，并结合消息传播评分在构建过程中提供密集连接反馈，优化生成元集合以最小化直径。

Result: CayleyTopo在信息传播速度、链路故障鲁棒性和通信负载方面均优于现有手工拓扑，同时接近理论Moore界。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来多智能体系统提供了可扩展、鲁棒且高效的通信基础，使图结构本身成为可优化的对象，而非固定约束。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-agent communication has long faced a scalability bottleneck: fully connected networks require quadratic complexity, yet existing sparse topologies rely on hand-crafted rules. This paper treats the communication graph itself as a design variable and proposes CayleyTopo, a family of circulant Cayley graphs whose generator sets are optimized to minimize diameter, directly targeting worst-case information propagation speed. To navigate the enormous search space of possible generator sets, we develop a lightweight reinforcement learning framework that injects a number-theoretic prior to favor structurally rich generators, alongside a message-propagation score that provides dense connectivity feedback during construction. The resulting CayleyTopo consistently outperforms existing hand-crafted topologies, achieving faster information dissemination, greater resilience to link failures, and lower communication load, all while approaching the theoretical Moore bound. Our study opens the door to scalable, robust, and efficient communication foundations for future multi-agent systems, where the graph itself becomes optimizable rather than a fixed constraint.

</details>


### [380] [Sustainability-Constrained Workload Orchestration for Sovereign AI Infrastructure: A Joint Compute-Network Optimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09705)
*Sergio Cruzes*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可持续性约束编排框架，引入可行主权运营区（FSOR）概念，将碳排放强度、水资源使用和电力容量作为严格约束，通过联合优化计算部署与光网络路由，实现AI基础设施在物理和环境限制下的闭环可持续运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基础设施已从以软件为中心的范式转变为受物理和环境限制紧密约束的系统。能源可用性、冷却能力和网络连接性现在施加了无法仅通过软件优化缓解的硬性运营边界，现有方法缺乏对这些物理约束的严格处理。

Method: 提出可持续性约束编排框架，将碳排放强度、水资源使用和电力容量视为严格的可行性约束而非可调惩罚项；引入可行主权运营区（FSOR）概念，表征基础设施在物理和监管约束下实际可承载的工作负载集合；在单一闭环系统中联合优化计算部署和光网络路由。

Result: 基于场景的分析表明，相较于基准方案，联合优化方法产生了更低的环境影响；不可行事件并非优化器失败，而是基于遥测的精确信号，表明主权AI运营需要基础设施投资或工作负载缩减。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将可持续性约束整合到AI基础设施编排中，证明了将物理和环境限制作为硬约束处理的可行性，为AI基础设施的可持续运营提供了新的理论框架和实践指导。

Abstract: AI infrastructure has transitioned from a software-centric paradigm to a system tightly bound by physical and environmental limits. Energy availability, cooling capacity, and network connectivity now impose hard operational boundaries that cannot be relaxed through software optimization alone. This paper proposes a sustainability-constrained orchestration framework that treats carbon intensity, water usage, and power capacity as strict feasibility constraints rather than tunable penalties, and that jointly optimizes compute placement and optical network routing in a single closed-loop system. We introduce the Feasible Sovereign Operating Region (FSOR) - a conceptual and operational construct that characterizes the set of workloads a given infrastructure can actually sustain under its physical and regulatory endowment. Scenario-based analysis demonstrates that joint optimization yields lower environmental impact relative to baseline formulations. Infeasibility events, rather than being optimizer failures, constitute precise, telemetry-grounded signals that sovereign AI operation requires infrastructure investment or workload reduction.

</details>


### [381] [RouterWise: Joint Resource Allocation and Routing for Latency-Aware Multi-Model LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10907)
*Hossein Hosseini Kasnavieh,Christopher Leckie,Adel N. Toosi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出RouterWise框架，通过联合优化资源分配和路由策略，解决了多模型LLM服务中因资源竞争导致的动态延迟问题，显著提升了输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模型LLM路由方法假设模型延迟是固定的，但在实际部署中，多个模型共享有限的GPU资源，模型延迟高度依赖于分配的资源量和路由策略产生的负载。路由决策与资源分配紧密耦合，忽略这种依赖关系会导致性能次优。

Method: 提出RouterWise框架，将问题形式化为部署配置和路由比例的约束联合优化问题。该方法结合了用于最大化分数路由的双价格公式，以及通过系统分析得出的特定配置延迟模型。RouterWise搜索可行的系统配置，并在延迟目标约束下为每个固定配置计算最佳路由策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，在相同的GPU集群上，不同配置方案下的可达到输出质量分数差异高达87%，这突显了资源分配是决定路由性能的关键因素。

Conclusion: 资源分配对多模型LLM路由性能具有决定性影响，联合优化资源分配和路由策略对于在满足延迟目标的同时最大化输出质量至关重要。RouterWise框架能有效解决这一耦合优化问题。

Abstract: Multi-model LLM routing has emerged as an effective approach for reducing serving cost and latency while maintaining output quality by assigning each prompt to an appropriate model. However, prior routing methods typically assume that each model has a fixed latency. In real deployments, this assumption is inaccurate: multiple models often share limited GPU resources, and a model's latency depends strongly on both its allocated resources and the request load induced by the routing policy. Consequently, routing and resource allocation are tightly coupled.
  In this work, we study joint resource allocation and routing for latency-aware multi-model LLM serving in GPU clusters. Given a set of deployed models and a latency service-level objective (SLO), we seek a system setup and routing policy that maximize overall output quality while satisfying the latency target. We formalize this problem as a constrained joint optimization over deployment setup and routing fractions, and propose RouterWise, which combines a dual-price formulation for score-maximizing routing with setup-specific latency models derived from system profiling. RouterWise searches over feasible system setups and, for each fixed setup, computes the best routing policy under the latency target. Our results show that even on the same GPU cluster, achievable output-quality score can vary by up to 87% across retained setups, highlighting that resource allocation is a key determinant of routing performance.

</details>


### [382] [Programmable Packet Scheduling with Dynamic Reordering at Line Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11453)
*Zekun Wang,Binghao Yue,Yichen Deng,Weitao Pan,Jiangyi Shi,Yue Hao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出UIFO可编程调度模型，通过两级抽象实现缓冲数据包的动态重排序，兼容现有PIFO/PIEO模型，在100Gbps线速下支持pFabric等动态排序算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可编程硬件调度模型（如PIFO和PIEO）仅支持包级排序，无法对已缓冲数据包进行动态重排序，限制了pFabric等动态排序算法的支持能力。

Method: 提出UIFO（Update-In-First-Out）可编程调度模型，引入类别和包的两级抽象机制。在类别层面实现调度顺序的动态更新，同时在每个类别内部保持有序的包调度，从而支持对已缓冲数据包的动态重排序。基于优先队列设计实现硬件原型。

Result: 在FPGA平台和28nm ASIC工艺上完成实现与评估，UIFO在保持良好可扩展性的同时能够维持100Gbps线速吞吐量，显著增强了调度表达能力。

Conclusion: UIFO成功突破了现有可编程调度模型的语义限制，在保持与PIFO/PIEO完全兼容并对其进行泛化的基础上，实现了对动态排序算法的有效支持，为高速交换机数据包调度提供了更具表达力的可编程解决方案。

Abstract: High-speed switch packet scheduling demands both line-rate performance and programmability. Existing programmable hardware scheduling models, such as PIFO and PIEO, can express a broad range of scheduling algorithms; however, their semantics are restricted to packet-level ordering and cannot dynamically reorder buffered packets, which limits the support for dynamic-ordering algorithms such as pFabric.
  To overcome this limitation, we propose UIFO (Update-In-First-Out), a new programmable scheduling model that introduces a two-level abstraction over classes and packets. UIFO enables dynamic updates to the scheduling order at the class level while preserving in-order packet scheduling within each class, thereby supporting dynamic reordering of already-buffered packets. Furthermore, UIFO remains fully compatible with and generalizes existing PIFO and PIEO models.
  We implement a hardware prototype of UIFO based on priority-queue designs and evaluate it on an FPGA platform and in a 28 nm ASIC process. Overall, UIFO significantly enhances scheduling expressiveness and maintains favorable scalability while sustaining 100 Gbps line-rate throughput.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [383] [Heterogeneous Consensus-Progressive Reasoning for Efficient Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09679)
*Yiqing Liu,Hantao Yao,Wu Liu,Allen He,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出HCP-MAD框架，利用共识作为动态信号实现渐进式推理，通过三阶段机制根据任务复杂度自适应调整协作规模，在提升准确性的同时大幅降低token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体辩论方法分别优化轮内拓扑和轮间交互，导致无论任务复杂度如何都会产生高昂的token成本。多数简单任务可通过轻量级双智能体辩论有效解决，而复杂任务才需要扩展协作。

Method: 提出三阶段渐进式推理机制：1) 异构共识验证，使用异构智能体对进行快速共识验证以实现早停；2) 异构双智能体辩论，应用自适应停止准则动态终止对记录推理轨迹的相互批评；3) 升级集体投票，聚合额外智能体的多样化视角处理未解决任务。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，HCP-MAD显著提升了准确性，同时大幅降低了token成本。

Conclusion: HCP-MAD通过共识驱动的渐进式推理实现了效率与性能的平衡，为多智能体辩论框架的资源优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) is a collaborative framework in which multiple agents iteratively refine solutions through the generation of reasoning and alternating critique cycles. Current work primarily optimizes intra-round topologies and inter-round interactions separately, which still results in high token costs regardless of task complexity. This work introduces Heterogeneous Consensus-Progressive Reasoning for Efficient Multi-Agent Debate (HCP-MAD), leveraging consensus as a dynamic signal to facilitate progressive reasoning. The core motivation is that a majority of straightforward tasks can be effectively resolved via lightweight pair-agent debates, while complex tasks require expanded collaboration. Consequently, HCP-MAD employs a three-stage progressive reasoning mechanism to develop adaptive solutions across varying task complexities. Firstly, Heterogeneous Consensus Verification conducts rapid consensus verification using a pair of heterogeneous agents for early stopping. Next, the Heterogeneous Pair-Agent Debate applies an adaptive stopping criterion to dynamically terminate mutual critique of recorded reasoning traces. Finally, the unresolved tasks are addressed through Escalated Collective Voting by aggregating diverse perspectives from additional agents. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that HCP-MAD significantly enhances accuracy while substantially reducing token costs.

</details>


### [384] [MPAC: A Multi-Principal Agent Coordination Protocol for Interoperable Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09744)
*Kaiyang Qian,Xinmin Fang,Zhengxiong Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了MPAC（多主体智能体协调协议），解决了现有MCP和A2A协议无法处理跨独立主体（个人/组织）智能体协调共享状态的问题。在代码审查基准测试中，协调开销降低95%，实际执行速度提升4.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体协议（MCP和A2A）均假设单一控制主体，当属于不同主体的智能体需要协调共享状态时（如不同工程师的编程智能体编辑同一代码库、家庭成员共同规划行程、跨组织智能体协商联合决策），现有协议无法适用，协调工作退化为临时聊天、手动合并或静默覆盖。

Method: 提出MPAC应用层协议，包含五层架构：会话层、意图层、操作层、冲突层和治理层。核心机制包括：将意图声明作为行动前提、将冲突表示为一级结构化对象、通过可插拔治理层支持人在回路仲裁。规范定义了21种消息类型、三个带规范性转换表的状态机、Lamport时钟因果水印、两种执行模型、三种安全配置文件，以及对共享状态的乐观并发控制。

Result: 发布了两个互操作的参考实现（Python和TypeScript），包含223个测试、JSON Schema套件和7个实时多智能体演示。在受控的三智能体代码审查基准测试中，与序列化人工调解基线相比，协调开销减少95%，实际执行速度提升4.8倍，同时保持各智能体决策时间不变。加速来源于消除协调等待，而非压缩模型调用。

Conclusion: MPAC成功填补了跨独立主体智能体协调的协议空白，通过明确的协调语义实现了显著的效率提升。规范、实现和演示均已开源。

Abstract: The AI agent ecosystem has converged on two protocols: the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for tool invocation and Agent-to-Agent (A2A) for single-principal task delegation. Both assume a single controlling principal, meaning one person or organization that owns every agent. When independent principals' agents must coordinate over shared state, such as engineers' coding agents editing the same repository, family members planning a shared trip, or agents from different organizations negotiating a joint decision, neither protocol applies, and coordination collapses to ad-hoc chat, manual merging, or silent overwrites. We present MPAC (Multi-Principal Agent Coordination Protocol), an application-layer protocol that fills this gap with explicit coordination semantics across five layers: Session, Intent, Operation, Conflict, and Governance. MPAC makes intent declaration a precondition for action, represents conflicts as first-class structured objects, and supports human-in-the-loop arbitration through a pluggable governance layer. The specification defines 21 message types, three state machines with normative transition tables, Lamport-clock causal watermarking, two execution models, three security profiles, and optimistic concurrency control on shared state. We release two interoperable reference implementations in Python and TypeScript with 223 tests, a JSON Schema suite, and seven live multi-agent demos. A controlled three-agent code review benchmark shows a 95 percent reduction in coordination overhead and a 4.8 times wall-clock speedup versus a serialized human-mediated baseline, with per-agent decision time preserved. The speedup comes from eliminating coordination waits, not compressing model calls. Specification, implementations, and demos are open source.

</details>


### [385] [CONSCIENTIA: Can LLM Agents Learn to Strategize? Emergent Deception and Trust in a Multi-Agent NYC Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09746)
*Aarush Sinha,Arion Das,Soumyadeep Nag,Charan Karnati,Shravani Nag,Chandra Vadhan Raj,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Suranjana Trivedy,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模多智能体模拟研究了LLM代理的战略行为涌现，发现蓝方代理任务成功率从46.0%提升至57.3%，但仍高度易受对抗性说服影响（70.7%），揭示了LLM代理战略能力有限且存在安全-有用性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地作为自主代理部署，理解多智能体环境中战略行为如何涌现已成为重要的对齐挑战，需要构建可控环境来直接观察和测量这种行为。

Method: 构建纽约市简化模型的大规模多智能体模拟环境，蓝方代理旨在高效到达目的地，红方代理通过说服性语言将蓝方引导至广告牌密集路线。采用Kahneman-Tversky优化(KTO)的迭代模拟管道更新代理策略，蓝方优化减少广告牌曝光同时保持导航效率，红方适应性地利用剩余弱点。

Result: 最佳蓝方策略将任务成功率从46.0%提高到57.3%，但易感性仍高达70.7%。后期策略表现出更强的选择性合作同时保持轨迹效率。然而，持续的安全-有用性权衡仍然存在：更好抵抗对抗性引导的策略不能同时最大化任务完成率。

Conclusion: LLM代理能够表现出有限的战略行为，包括选择性信任和欺骗，但仍高度易受对抗性说服的影响，存在持久的安全-有用性权衡问题。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, understanding how strategic behavior emerges in multi-agent environments has become an important alignment challenge. We take a neutral empirical stance and construct a controlled environment in which strategic behavior can be directly observed and measured. We introduce a large-scale multi-agent simulation in a simplified model of New York City, where LLM-driven agents interact under opposing incentives. Blue agents aim to reach their destinations efficiently, while Red agents attempt to divert them toward billboard-heavy routes using persuasive language to maximize advertising revenue. Hidden identities make navigation socially mediated, forcing agents to decide when to trust or deceive. We study policy learning through an iterative simulation pipeline that updates agent policies across repeated interaction rounds using Kahneman-Tversky Optimization (KTO). Blue agents are optimized to reduce billboard exposure while preserving navigation efficiency, whereas Red agents adapt to exploit remaining weaknesses. Across iterations, the best Blue policy improves task success from 46.0% to 57.3%, although susceptibility remains high at 70.7%. Later policies exhibit stronger selective cooperation while preserving trajectory efficiency. However, a persistent safety-helpfulness trade-off remains: policies that better resist adversarial steering do not simultaneously maximize task completion. Overall, our results show that LLM agents can exhibit limited strategic behavior, including selective trust and deception, while remaining highly vulnerable to adversarial persuasion.

</details>


### [386] [Toward Explanatory Equilibrium: Verifiable Reasoning as a Coordination Mechanism under Asymmetric Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09917)
*Feliks Bańka,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出“解释性均衡”设计原则，通过让LLM多智能体系统中的代理交换可审计的结构化推理产物并配合有界验证，解决了自然语言推理不可靠且计算昂贵的问题，在金融场景中实现了高效协调与低错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于LLM的多智能体系统中，代理常附带自然语言推理进行决策协调。然而，推理不仅产生计算成本，若缺乏验证机制，还可能退化为不可靠的“廉价空谈”，导致系统在信息不对称下协调失败或错误批准。

Method: 提出了“解释性均衡”作为多智能体系统的设计原则。核心机制是让代理交换结构化的推理产物（即可审计的主张配以简明文本），接收方在明确的资源约束下应用概率审计进行有界验证。研究构建了机制级的交换-审计模型，并在一个包含交易员和风险管理员的金融模拟环境中进行了实证验证。

Result: 在模糊的边界提案中，可审计的结构化产物有效解决了因信息不对称和保守验证导致的“沉默成本”问题。若无结构化推理，批准率与福利水平会崩溃；引入该机制后，系统在维持持续低不良批准率的同时解锁了协调能力，且该表现在不同审计强度、预算及激励机制下均保持稳定。

Conclusion: 在基于LLM的多智能体系统中，实现可扩展且保障安全的协调，不仅取决于审计强度，更根本地依赖于将推理规范化地外化为可部分验证的产物。这种规范化的推理外化是平衡计算成本与系统安全性的关键。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly coordinate decisions in multi-agent systems, often attaching natural-language reasoning to actions. However, reasoning is neither free nor automatically reliable: it incurs computational cost and, without verification, may degenerate into persuasive cheap talk. We introduce Explanatory Equilibrium as a design principle for explanation-aware multi-agent systems and study a regime in which agents exchange structured reasoning artifacts-auditable claims paired with concise text-while receivers apply bounded verification through probabilistic audits under explicit resource constraints. We contribute (i) a minimal mechanism-level exchange-audit model linking audit intensity, misreporting incentives, and reasoning costs, and (ii) empirical evidence from a finance-inspired LLM setting involving a Trader and a Risk Manager. In ambiguous, borderline proposals, auditable artifacts prevent the cost of silence driven by conservative validation under asymmetric information: without structured claims, approval and welfare collapse. By contrast, structured reasoning unlocks coordination while maintaining consistently low bad-approval rates across audit intensities, audit budgets, and incentive regimes. Our results suggest that scalable, safety-preserving coordination in LLM-based multi-agent systems depends not only on audit strength, but more fundamentally on disciplined externalization of reasoning into partially verifiable artifacts.

</details>


### [387] [Prosociality by Coupling, Not Mere Observation: Homeostatic Sharing in an Inspectable Recurrent Artificial Life Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10760)
*Aishik Sanyal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自身稳态调节的人工智能体助人机制，证明了仅通过将他人的需求耦合到自身的内部状态调节中，无需任何亲社会奖励项，即可涌现出助人行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人工智能体的亲社会行为往往通过显式社会奖励、硬编码的亲社会奖励项或直接访问其他智能体内部状态来实现，这使得最小化的亲社会行为难以解释。研究旨在探索一种更简洁的解释框架：能否仅通过自身调节机制而非外部奖励来产生助人行为。

Method: 在ReCoN-Ipsundrum（一种具有情感耦合调节的可检测循环控制器）基础上，添加显式稳态调节器和社会耦合通道，同时保持规划严格以自我为中心——智能体仅评估自身预测的内部状态，不引入任何关于伙伴福利的奖励项。通过两个简化的实验环境（单步FoodShareToy和多步SocialCorridorWorld）对比四种匹配条件，并进行损伤实验和耦合参数扫描分析。

Result: 在单步FoodShareToy任务中，精确求解器发现在λ*≈0.91处存在从EAT到PASS的急剧切换；实验显示仅自身条件和伙伴观察条件从不助人，而情感耦合条件总是助人。在多步SocialCorridorWorld任务中，耦合将助人率和伙伴恢复率从0提升至1，救援延迟从18步降至9步，将相互生存率从0.15提升至0.33。假损伤保留助人行为，而关闭耦合和打乱伙伴损伤在两项任务中均消除助人行为。耦合扫描显示存在负载依赖的可行性边界：低负载下λ≥0.25时出现助人行为，而中高负载下无测试值能在时限内救助伙伴。

Conclusion: 在所提出的最小架构中，助人行为在他人需求被路由到自身调节时出现。这为人工生命领域提供了一个窄声明：帮助行为的涌现可以通过自我调节机制实现，而无需额外的亲社会奖励项，为理解最小化亲社会行为提供了新的解释框架。

Abstract: Artificial agents can be made to "help" for many reasons, including explicit social reward, hard-coded prosocial bonuses, or direct access to another agent's internal state. Those possibilities make minimal prosocial behavior hard to interpret. Building on ReCoN-Ipsundrum, an inspectable recurrent controller with affect-coupled regulation, I add an explicit homeostat and a social coupling channel while keeping planning strictly self-directed: the agent scores only its own predicted internal state, and no partner-welfare reward term is introduced. I compare four matched conditions in two toy worlds. In a one-step FoodShareToy, an exact solver finds a sharp switch from EAT to PASS at $λ* \approx 0.91$ for the default state. In the experimental runs, the self-only and partner-observing conditions never help, whereas the affectively coupled conditions always do. In a multi-step SocialCorridorWorld, the same dissociation reappears: coupling flips help rate and partner recovery from 0 to 1 and cuts rescue latency from 18 to 9 steps, while raising mutual viability from 0.15 to 0.33. Sham lesions preserve helping, but coupling-off and shuffled-partner lesions abolish it in both tasks. A coupling sweep shows a load-dependent feasibility boundary: under low load, helping appears for $λ \geq 0.25$, whereas under medium and high loads no tested value rescues the partner within horizon. The result is a narrow claim for artificial life: in this minimal architecture, helping appears when another's need is routed into self-regulation.

</details>


### [388] [AgentWebBench: Benchmarking Multi-Agent Coordination in Agentic Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10938)
*Shanshan Zhong,Kate Shen,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了AgentWebBench基准，用于评估用户代理通过与特定网站内容代理交互来合成答案的能力，发现多代理协调虽落后于集中式检索，但差距随模型规模缩小，甚至在问答任务上超越集中式检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体网络（Agentic Web）范式的兴起，内容提供商部署代理管理数据，信息访问从集中式检索转向分布式协调，需要评估用户代理与网站特定内容代理协作的效果。

Method: 构建AgentWebBench基准，设计四类任务覆盖网页搜索、网页推荐、问答和深度研究，评测七个先进LLM和三种协调策略在分布式协调与集中式检索场景下的表现。

Result: 多代理协调整体落后集中式检索，但差距随模型规模缩小，问答任务上甚至超越集中式；分布式访问使流量集中于少数网站，测试时扩展提升交互可靠性与任务性能，良好规划与充分交互是关键。

Conclusion: 用户代理需改进规划与答案合成，内容代理需提升检索可靠性与证据质量；基准为智能体网络范式研究提供了工具与洞察。

Abstract: Agentic Web is an emerging paradigm where autonomous agents help users use online information. As the paradigm develops, content providers are also deploying agents to manage their data and serve it through controlled interfaces. This shift moves information access from centralized retrieval to decentralized coordination. To study this setting, we introduce AgentWebBench, a benchmark that evaluates how well a user agent synthesizes answers by interacting with website-specific content agents. We evaluate four tasks that cover common web information needs, spanning ranked retrieval (web search, web recommendation) and open-ended synthesis (question answering, deep research). Across seven advanced LLMs and three coordination strategies, multi-agent coordination generally lags behind centralized retrieval as expected, because user agent cannot directly access the corpus, but the gap shrinks with model scale and can even outperform centralized retrieval on question answering. This benchmark also enables us to study properties of the emerging paradigm of the digital world. We find that decentralized access concentrates traffic toward a small set of websites, test time scaling improves both interaction reliability and task performance, and strong results require sufficient interactions guided by careful planning. Finally, our failure analysis suggests that user agents need better planning and answer synthesis, while content agents need more reliable retrieval and evidence quality. Code, data, and APIs are released on https://github.com/cxcscmu/AgentWebBench.

</details>


### [389] [Governance by Design: A Parsonian Institutional Architecture for Internet-Wide Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11337)
*Anbang Ruan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文基于帕森斯AGIL框架提出了互联网级多智能体社会的制度治理架构，通过对OpenClaw生态系统（25万+GitHub星标）的诊断发现其治理覆盖率不足19%，且12个支柱间交互路径均无功能，揭示了当前市场驱动开发中存在的系统性治理缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着本地多智能体系统向互联网级智能体社会演进，自主智能体通过开放注册表相互发现、无需中央编排器即可交互，并产生涌现社会行为。现有基于风险枚举或流程合规的治理范式已不足以应对这种新型社会形态，需要制度设计层面的治理框架。

Method: 应用塔尔科特·帕森斯的AGIL框架（适应、目标达成、整合、潜伏四个功能必要条件），推导出十六单元制度架构。通过递归子功能分析（16个单元64个二进制指标）对OpenClaw生态系统进行诊断，并评估支柱间交互媒介，同时扩展诊断至更广泛的智能体原生协议栈（MCP、A2A、ANP、x402、ERC-8004）。

Result: OpenClaw生态系统子功能覆盖率最高仅19%（敏感范围17-30%），12个支柱间交互路径均无功能。生态系统具备技术基础设施但缺乏主动治理、协调层和规范基础，信托和政治支柱服务最为不足。独立开发团队在更广泛协议栈中复现相同结构模式，确认治理缺口是市场驱动开发的特征而非生态不成熟。

Conclusion: 制度设计在社会模式固化前最为有效，需为缺失的治理基础设施制定优先级路线图，重点填补协调层和规范基础，强化信托和政治支柱建设。

Abstract: The dominant paradigm of local multi-agent systems -- orchestrated, enterprise-bounded pipelines -- is being superseded by internet-wide agent societies in which autonomous agents discover each other through open registries, interact without central orchestrators, and generate emergent social behaviors. We argue that governing such societies requires institutional design, not merely risk enumeration or process compliance. Applying Talcott Parsons' AGIL framework -- four functional imperatives (Adaptation, Goal Attainment, Integration, Latency) every viable social system must satisfy -- we derive a prescriptive sixteen-cell institutional architecture for internet-wide agent governance. Diagnostically applied to the OpenClaw ecosystem (250,000+ GitHub stars, 2M+ monthly users, 770,000+ registered agents) via a recursive sub-function analysis (64 binary indicators across 16 cells), we find at most 19% sub-function coverage (sensitivity range 17-30%) -- potential rather than operative capacity, since zero inter-cell coordination prevents existing infrastructure from participating in inter-pillar interchange. A complementary interchange media assessment finds zero of twelve inter-pillar pathways functional: the ecosystem has technical infrastructure but no active governance, no coordination layer, and no normative grounding, with the Fiduciary and Political pillars most severely underserved. Extending the diagnostic to the broader agent-native protocol stack (MCP, A2A, ANP, x402, ERC-8004), independent development teams reproduce the same structural pattern -- confirming the governance gap is a feature of market-driven development, not ecosystem immaturity. Institutional design is most effective before social patterns calcify; we conclude with a prioritized roadmap for the missing governance infrastructure.

</details>


### [390] [SLALOM: Simulation Lifecycle Analysis via Longitudinal Observation Metrics for Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11466)
*Juhoon Lee,Joseph Seering*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SLALOM框架，旨在解决大语言模型（LLM）在生成式社会科学模拟中面临的“停止的时钟”有效性危机，通过从结果验证转向过程保真度评估，确保模拟轨迹的社会学合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM社会模拟评估方法存在“停止的时钟”问题：仅关注最终结果是否正确，却忽视了达到该结果的轨迹是否符合社会学逻辑。由于LLM内部推理具有不透明性，验证社会机制的“黑箱”问题成为一大挑战。

Method: 提出SLALOM（Simulation Lifecycle Analysis via Longitudinal Observation Metrics）框架，借鉴模式导向建模（POM）思想，将社会现象视为多元时间序列，引入“SLALOM门”作为中间路径约束点，并利用动态时间规整（DTW）算法将模拟轨迹与实证真值对齐，从而提供量化指标评估结构现实主义。

Result: SLALOM框架能够有效区分合理的社会动态与随机噪声，为社会动力学模拟提供定量化的结构现实主义评估方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过验证过程保真度而非单纯的结果正确性，为LLM驱动的政策模拟建立了更稳健的标准，推动了生成式社会科学模拟的有效性评估发展。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer a potentially-transformative path forward for generative social science but face a critical crisis of validity. Current simulation evaluation methodologies suffer from the "stopped clock" problem: they confirm that a simulation reached the correct final outcome while ignoring whether the trajectory leading to it was sociologically plausible. Because the internal reasoning of LLMs is opaque, verifying the "black box" of social mechanisms remains a persistent challenge. In this paper, we introduce SLALOM (Simulation Lifecycle Analysis via Longitudinal Observation Metrics), a framework that shifts validation from outcome verification to process fidelity. Drawing on Pattern-Oriented Modeling (POM), SLALOM treats social phenomena as multivariate time series that must traverse specific SLALOM gates, or intermediate waypoint constraints representing distinct phases. By utilizing Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) to align simulated trajectories with empirical ground truth, SLALOM offers a quantitative metric to assess structural realism, helping to differentiate plausible social dynamics from stochastic noise and contributing to more robust policy simulation standards.

</details>
